# Conduit V0.2-R Reliability Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** No data is lost when the server, the leader transport, every transport, or EMQX goes down (EMQX disk-backed durable sessions plus active-standby transports elected through Redis). The e2e suite proves this for four scenarios in a throwaway environment. The simulator also feeds realistic data to every demo tenant's products.

**Architecture:** EMQX 5.8.6 runs with durable sessions (`EMQX_DURABLE_SESSIONS__ENABLE=true`, `EMQX_CLUSTER__DISCOVERY_STRATEGY=singleton`). Every transport uses the same client id `conduit-transport`, `cleanStart=false`, a session expiry of 86400 s and a plain subscription `conduit/+/+/up/#`, because durable sessions reject shared subscriptions. A new `leader` package in conduit-transport elects one leader through the Redis key `conduit:transport:leader`. It uses `SET NX PX`, a Lua compare-and-`PEXPIRE` renew and a Lua compare-and-`DEL` release. Only the leader starts the `MqttProtocolAdapter`. A failed renewal stops the adapter at once. A dropped connection is reopened by the election coordinator after it re-checks the lock, and the adapter does not use HiveMQ auto-reconnect. The e2e module starts PostgreSQL, Redis, InfluxDB and EMQX with Testcontainers. It runs one server jar and two transport jars as local processes and kills or restarts them. The test plays the devices itself: they publish QoS1 and retry until PUBACK. The simulator logs in as the platform admin, enters each selected tenant, resets the secrets of up to N enabled devices per product, and generates values per thing-model property. These values follow the spec §5.2 rules (day curves, cumulative totals, drift, enums and booleans) and always respect min/max and decimals.

**Tech Stack:** Java 21, Spring Boot 3.5.6, Spring Data Redis (Lettuce), HiveMQ MQTT Client 1.3.7, EMQX 5.8.6, Redis 7.4.2, PostgreSQL 16.8, InfluxDB 3.12.0-core, Testcontainers, JUnit 5, AssertJ, Mockito, Awaitility, Jackson, JDK `HttpServer` (stub tests).

**Governing spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-conduit-v0.2r-reliability-design.md` (behavior; §1 holds the probe facts); repo-root `CLAUDE.md` (conventions, comment rules, dev mode, maintenance rule).

## Global Constraints

**Operator-provided placeholders.** The operator sets these in their shell. Never write their real values into any committed file.

| Placeholder | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `$JAVA21_HOME` | Path of a JDK 21 installation |
| `<docker-host>` | Address of the remote Docker host. It runs Testcontainers and the infrastructure Compose stack. |
| `<dev-host>` | Address at which the Docker host reaches this development machine. It is the value of `E2E_DEV_HOST`, and the host part of `CONDUIT_AUTH_URL` in dev mode. |
| `$DOCKER_CLI` | The docker CLI command (with `DOCKER_HOST` pointing at the Docker host) |

| `$SYSADMIN_PASSWORD` | Password of the platform admin `sysadmin` (Task 8 acceptance only) |
| `$SCRATCH` | A temporary directory outside the repository (Task 8 acceptance scripts) |

**Shell preamble.** Run it once per shell before any Maven or Docker command in this plan. All commands run from the repository root, or from the worktree root, unless a step says otherwise.

```bash
export JAVA_HOME="$JAVA21_HOME" PATH="$JAVA21_HOME/bin:$PATH"
export DOCKER_HOST="tcp://<docker-host>:2375" TESTCONTAINERS_HOST_OVERRIDE="<docker-host>"
```

**Command patterns.** The tasks use these verbatim:

- Transport unit tests: `mvn -B -q -pl conduit-transport -am test -Dtest=<Class>[,<Class>] -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`
- Transport integration tests: `mvn -B -pl conduit-transport -am verify -Dtest=NONE -Dit.test=<Class>[,<Class>] -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false -Dfailsafe.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`
- Simulator unit tests: `mvn -B -q -pl conduit-simulator test -Dtest=<Class>[,<Class>] -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`
- Simulator integration tests: `mvn -B -pl conduit-simulator verify -Dtest=NONE -Dit.test=<Class> -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false -Dfailsafe.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`
- e2e unit tests and compile: `mvn -B -q -Pe2e -pl conduit-e2e -am test -Dtest=<Class> -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`
- Full backend build (Task 8 only): `mvn -B verify`
- e2e run (Task 8 only): `mvn -B -DskipTests install` then `E2E_DEV_HOST=<dev-host> mvn -B verify -Pe2e -pl conduit-e2e`

**Execution model (speed rule):**

- **Three parallel tracks in separate git worktrees.** Each track touches its own directories, so the tracks merge without conflicts:

  | Track | Branch / worktree | Tasks | Directories |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | A transport | `v0.2r-transport` in `.claude/worktrees/v0.2r-transport` | 1 → 2 → 3 | `conduit-transport/`, `deploy/docker-compose.yml` (EMQX and transport services only) |
  | B simulator | `v0.2r-simulator` in `.claude/worktrees/v0.2r-simulator` | 4 → 5 → 6 | `conduit-simulator/` |
  | C e2e | `v0.2r-e2e` in `.claude/worktrees/v0.2r-e2e` | 7 | `conduit-e2e/` |

  Create them from `main`, for example `git worktree add .claude/worktrees/v0.2r-transport -b v0.2r-transport main`. The path `.claude/worktrees/` is git-ignored.
- **Order.** A and B start at once. C depends on A's runtime behaviour: the health JSON contract and the plain subscription. It does not depend on A's code, so C codes against the contract below and may run in parallel with A. C's only test in Task 7 is a unit test, and the real e2e run waits for Task 8, after A is merged. C uses only the existing `ConduitApiClient` methods (`login`, `findOrCreateProduct(String, String)`, `createDevice`, `listDeviceKeys`, `deleteDevice`, `deleteProduct`) and `PayloadFormat.STANDARD.encode`. Track B keeps those signatures unchanged.
- **Transport health contract (Task 3 produces it, Task 7 consumes it).** `GET /actuator/health` on a transport returns `components.leader.status = "UP"` and `components.leader.details` with `role` (`"LEADER"` or `"STANDBY"`), `instanceId` (string) and `connected` (boolean). `connected` is `true` only on a leader whose MQTT adapter is connected and subscribed. The transport's HTTP port is `server.port` (default 8081).
- **Task 8 runs on `main` after the three branches are merged.** It is the only task that runs the full `mvn -B verify`, the e2e suite and the acceptance checks against the local stack.
- **Tests per task:** each task runs only the test classes it names.
- **Model split:** implementation subagents use a cheaper model (Sonnet), and reviews use the main model. The code in this plan is complete: implementers copy it and do not design it. If a step does not compile, or a named test fails for a reason the step does not explain, stop and report. Do not improvise.

**Spec values (copied verbatim):**

- Java 21, Spring Boot 3.5 (3.5.6), EMQX `5.8.6` (image `emqx/emqx:5.8.6`).
- EMQX env: `EMQX_DURABLE_SESSIONS__ENABLE=true`, `EMQX_CLUSTER__DISCOVERY_STRATEGY=singleton`. Without singleton the node fails to start with 「cluster discovery strategy must be 'singleton' when durable storage backend is 'builtin_local'」. Durable sessions reject shared subscriptions (「Shared Subscriptions not supported」, code 158). The message retention stays at the default of 1 day (`durable_sessions.message_retention_period`). This configuration goes in compose environment variables, not in `base.hocon`.
- Transport subscription: plain `conduit/+/+/up/#`, plus the `$SYS` connected/disconnected topics as before. The client id is `conduit.transport.mqtt.client-id`, default `conduit-transport`, shared by every instance. The adapter uses `cleanStart=false` and session expiry `86400` seconds. The shared-group config (`share-group`) is removed.
- Leader lock: Redis key `conduit:transport:leader`. Its value is the instance id (host name + process id + random string). Acquire with `SET NX PX <ttl>`. Renew with a Lua compare-and-`PEXPIRE`, and release with a Lua compare-and-`DEL`. Config `conduit.transport.leader.ttl` (default `15s`), `renew-interval` (`5s`), `acquire-interval` (`2s`). Only the leader connects to EMQX. A failed renewal (Redis down, id mismatch, timeout) disconnects MQTT immediately and the instance becomes standby. A normal stop releases the lock. Health shows `role: LEADER | STANDBY`. Election, renewal failures and switch-overs log at INFO or WARN.
- Simulator env: `SIM_PLATFORM_USERNAME` (default `sysadmin`), `SIM_PLATFORM_PASSWORD` (empty → demo product only), `SIM_TENANTS` (default `*`, else comma-separated tenant codes), `SIM_MAX_DEVICES_PER_PRODUCT` (default `100`).
- e2e env: `E2E_DEV_HOST`. If it is unset, the test fails and says why. `DOCKER_HOST` / `TESTCONTAINERS_HOST_OVERRIDE` work as before. Images: `postgres:16.8`, `redis:7.4.2`, `influxdb:3.12.0-core`, `emqx/emqx:5.8.6`. Each scenario uses one new product (「e2e 场景 N 电表」) with 10 devices × 50 QoS1 messages = 500.

**Project rules:**

- **Code comments (owner rule; applies to every code block in this plan):**
  1. Every class, interface, record and enum gets a Chinese header comment saying what it is for, as Javadoc on the type. This includes nested types and test classes.
  2. Every key method gets a Chinese Javadoc stating what it does, its important parameters and return value, and any non-obvious behavior (exceptions, edge cases, side effects). "Key" means public, service and lifecycle methods, pure policy functions, and anything with branching logic. Trivial getters and one-line helpers are exempt.
  3. Comments say "what and why", never restate the code, and are updated together with the code (CLAUDE.md §2.1).
  4. Do not edit `CLAUDE.md` except in Task 8, which its maintenance rule requires.
- **Git:** Conventional Commits. This repo commits with the author name only and an empty email (`.git/config` already holds them). Never run `git config user.email` and never pass an email. Commits and PRs carry no AI attribution lines: no `Co-Authored-By` and no "Generated with".
- **No secrets in tracked files:** no IP addresses, host names, local Windows paths, real user names, real passwords or tokens. Docs use `<docker-host>` / `<dev-host>`. Local config stays untracked: `deploy/.env`, `application-local.yml`, `simulator-local.properties`. The e2e generates its passwords and tokens randomly per run and never writes them to tracked files. Test passwords in test classes are test values only.
- **No deploy steps.** Nothing in this plan runs `deploy/build-local.sh` or starts the application stack on the Docker host (CLAUDE.md §3). Task 8 recreates only the infrastructure EMQX container, which spec §7 item 1 requires. The controller does it with the explicit commands given there. Acceptance is tests plus API checks against the stack the owner runs from the IDE (`Conduit (local all)`).
- **Versions:** this plan adds no Maven dependency except `org.testcontainers:junit-jupiter` to `conduit-e2e` (test scope, version managed by the Spring Boot parent).
- **Tests:** `*Test` is a unit test (surefire) and `*IT` an integration test (failsafe, Testcontainers). Transport integration tests share one Redis and one durable-session EMQX through `TestBroker`. Each test uses its own stream key, leader key, client id and product key, so tests never read each other's data.
- **Logging (CLAUDE.md §2.2):** SLF4J parameterized. Never log device secrets, passwords, JWTs, the internal token or `Authorization`. Payload values appear only at TRACE and are truncated to 256 characters. The election logs one INFO line per role change and a WARN per renewal failure or lost connection. Per-renewal and per-acquire-attempt lines are DEBUG.
- **Data conventions:** demo and test data use meaningful Chinese names (「e2e 场景 1 电表」, 「e2e 场景1 电表01」). Each e2e scenario deletes its product and devices. The transport ITs remove their Redis keys.

| Fact | Where |

| `MqttProtocolAdapter` subscribes with `$share/<group>/conduit/+/+/up/#`, connects with `cleanStart(false)`, uses HiveMQ automatic reconnect and the `SUBSCRIBED` global filter with manual ack, and acks only after `bus.publish` succeeds | `conduit-transport/.../mqtt/MqttProtocolAdapter.java` |
| `MqttTransportProperties(host, port, clientId="conduit-transport-1", username, password, shareGroup="conduit", sessionExpirySeconds=3600, ownClientPrefix="conduit-")` | `mqtt/MqttTransportProperties.java` |
| `AdapterLifecycle` (`@Component`, `SmartLifecycle`) starts every `ProtocolAdapter` at startup | `adapter/AdapterLifecycle.java` |
| The server's MQTT auth grants superuser only to `TRANSPORT_MQTT_USERNAME` + `TRANSPORT_MQTT_PASSWORD` with a client id starting `conduit-` | `conduit-server/.../mqttauth/MqttAuthService.java` |
| The server reads its whole config from env vars: `POSTGRES_HOST/PORT/DB/USER/PASSWORD`, `REDIS_HOST/PORT`, `INFLUX_URL`, `INFLUX_TOKEN`, `CONDUIT_ADMIN_USERNAME/PASSWORD`, `CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD`, `CONDUIT_JWT_SECRET`, `CONDUIT_INTERNAL_TOKEN`, `TRANSPORT_MQTT_USERNAME/PASSWORD`, `PIPELINE_CLAIM_MIN_IDLE`, `CONDUIT_LOG_DIR`; readiness at `/actuator/health/readiness`; it creates the InfluxDB database `telemetry` at startup | `conduit-server/src/main/resources/application.yml` |
| The pipeline consumer name is `server-${random.uuid}` per process, so a restarted server claims the dead one's pending entries after `PIPELINE_CLAIM_MIN_IDLE` | same |
| `deploy/emqx/base.hocon.tpl` holds the HTTP auth (`__CONDUIT_AUTH_URL__/internal/mqtt/auth`, header token `__CONDUIT_INTERNAL_TOKEN__`), the file ACL `/opt/emqx/etc/conduit-acl.conf` and the `$SYS` event switches | `deploy/emqx/` |
| Platform API: `GET /api/v1/platform/tenants?status=&page=&size<=100` → `PageView<TenantView{code,name,status,…}>`; `POST /api/v1/platform/tenants/{code}/enter` → `TokenResponse{accessToken,…}`; `POST /api/v1/auth/exit-tenant` → `TokenResponse` | `platform/PlatformController`, `auth/AuthController` |
| `GET /api/v1/products?page&size` → items `{productKey,name,codec,propertyCount,…}`; `GET /api/v1/products/{pk}/properties` → `[{identifier,name,dataType,unit,minValue,maxValue,decimals,enumItems:[{value,label}]|null,…}]`; `GET /api/v1/devices?productKey=&status=ENABLED&page&size` → items `{deviceKey,name,status,…}`; `POST /api/v1/devices/{key}/reset-secret` → `{deviceKey,deviceSecret}` | product, thingmodel, device controllers |
| The pipeline validates DOUBLE/INT min/max and ENUM membership; decimals are not validated. INT accepts integral JSON numbers | `pipeline/FieldValidator` |
| `sysadmin` and `admin` are bootstrapped with `must_change_password = false` | `bootstrap/AdminBootstrap` |
| In the compose file `conduit-transport` has no `container_name` and no host ports; every Java service writes its log to the shared volume `conduit-logs` as `<service>.log` | `deploy/docker-compose.yml`, `application.yml` |

## Task 1: Transport leader lock and its configuration (track A)

Adds the Redis lock that the election in Task 3 uses, its configuration record, and the shared test containers.

**Files:**
- Create: `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderLock.java`
- Create: `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/RedisLeaderLock.java`
- Create: `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderProperties.java`
- Create: `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/support/TestBroker.java`
- Test: `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderPropertiesTest.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/RedisLeaderLockIT.java` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Spring Data Redis `StringRedisTemplate`; the existing test resource `conduit-transport/src/test/resources/emqx/acl.conf` (`{allow, all}.`).
- Produces (later tasks rely on these exact names):
  - `interface LeaderLock { String instanceId(); boolean tryAcquire(); boolean renew(); boolean release(); }`. All three operations may throw `org.springframework.dao.DataAccessException` when Redis is unavailable.
  - `RedisLeaderLock(StringRedisTemplate redis, String key, Duration ttl, String instanceId)` and `static String RedisLeaderLock.newInstanceId()`.
  - `record LeaderProperties(String key, Duration ttl, Duration renewInterval, Duration acquireInterval)`, prefix `conduit.transport.leader`, defaults `conduit:transport:leader` / `15s` / `5s` / `2s`. The constructor throws `IllegalStateException` unless `renewInterval < ttl` and both intervals are positive.
  - Test support `dev.conduit.transport.support.TestBroker` with `redis()`, `redisHost()`, `redisPort()`, `emqxHost()`, `emqxPort()`. EMQX runs with durable sessions, singleton discovery, `$SYS` client events and allow-all ACL.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- `tryAcquire` is a literal `SET key id NX PX ttl`. It also refuses the current holder. After a transient Redis failure the former leader therefore waits for its own key to expire, at most one TTL, like any other instance.
- `renew` and `release` return `false` when the key holds another id or is missing. Only the Lua script decides, so an expired lock taken by another instance can never be extended or deleted by the old leader.
- Containers start lazily and once per JVM (holder classes), so the Redis-only tests do not wait for EMQX.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing configuration test**

Create `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderPropertiesTest.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.leader;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.bind.Binder;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.source.MapConfigurationPropertySource;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

/** LeaderProperties 单元测试：默认值与 spec 一致、可按配置覆盖、续期间隔不短于 TTL 时拒绝启动。 */
class LeaderPropertiesTest {

    private static LeaderProperties bind(Map<String, String> values) {
        return new Binder(new MapConfigurationPropertySource(values))
                .bindOrCreate("conduit.transport.leader", LeaderProperties.class);
    }

    @Test
    void defaultsMatchTheSpec() {
        LeaderProperties props = bind(Map.of());

        assertThat(props.key()).isEqualTo("conduit:transport:leader");
        assertThat(props.ttl()).isEqualTo(Duration.ofSeconds(15));
        assertThat(props.renewInterval()).isEqualTo(Duration.ofSeconds(5));
        assertThat(props.acquireInterval()).isEqualTo(Duration.ofSeconds(2));
    }

    @Test
    void valuesCanBeOverridden() {
        LeaderProperties props = bind(Map.of(
                "conduit.transport.leader.ttl", "6s",
                "conduit.transport.leader.renew-interval", "2s",
                "conduit.transport.leader.acquire-interval", "500ms"));

        assertThat(props.ttl()).isEqualTo(Duration.ofSeconds(6));
        assertThat(props.renewInterval()).isEqualTo(Duration.ofSeconds(2));
        assertThat(props.acquireInterval()).isEqualTo(Duration.ofMillis(500));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsARenewIntervalThatIsNotShorterThanTheTtl() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new LeaderProperties("k", Duration.ofSeconds(5), Duration.ofSeconds(5),
                Duration.ofSeconds(2)))
                .isInstanceOf(IllegalStateException.class)
                .hasMessageContaining("renew-interval");
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNonPositiveIntervals() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new LeaderProperties("k", Duration.ofSeconds(15), Duration.ofSeconds(5),
                Duration.ZERO))
                .isInstanceOf(IllegalStateException.class)
                .hasMessageContaining("acquire-interval");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `LeaderProperties`**

Create `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderProperties.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.leader;

import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.bind.DefaultValue;

import java.time.Duration;

/**
 * transport 选主配置（前缀 conduit.transport.leader，V0.2-R spec 3.3）。
 *
 * @param key             Redis 锁键，所有 transport 实例共用
 * @param ttl             锁的过期时间：主实例崩溃后最长经过这么久备实例才能接管
 * @param renewInterval   主实例续期间隔，必须短于 ttl
 * @param acquireInterval 备实例尝试抢锁的间隔
 */
@ConfigurationProperties(prefix = "conduit.transport.leader")
public record LeaderProperties(
        @DefaultValue("conduit:transport:leader") String key,
        @DefaultValue("15s") Duration ttl,
        @DefaultValue("5s") Duration renewInterval,
        @DefaultValue("2s") Duration acquireInterval) {

    /** 校验间隔：续期间隔必须为正且短于 ttl，抢锁间隔必须为正，否则启动失败。 */
    public LeaderProperties {
        if (renewInterval.isZero() || renewInterval.isNegative() || renewInterval.compareTo(ttl) >= 0) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("conduit.transport.leader.renew-interval (" + renewInterval
                    + ") must be positive and shorter than ttl (" + ttl + ")");
        }
        if (acquireInterval.isZero() || acquireInterval.isNegative()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("conduit.transport.leader.acquire-interval (" + acquireInterval
                    + ") must be positive");
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the configuration test and confirm it passes**

Run: `mvn -B -q -pl conduit-transport -am test -Dtest=LeaderPropertiesTest -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false`

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the shared test containers**

Create `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/support/TestBroker.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.support;

import org.springframework.data.redis.connection.RedisStandaloneConfiguration;
import org.springframework.data.redis.connection.lettuce.LettuceConnectionFactory;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
import org.testcontainers.containers.GenericContainer;
import org.testcontainers.containers.wait.strategy.Wait;
import org.testcontainers.utility.DockerImageName;
import org.testcontainers.utility.MountableFile;

import java.time.Duration;

/**
 * transport 集成测试共用的容器：Redis 7.4.2 与开启磁盘持久会话的 EMQX 5.8.6（单节点 singleton、$SYS 上下线事件、
 * ACL 全放行、匿名连接）。每个 JVM 只启动一次（持有类惰性初始化），只用 Redis 的测试不会等待 EMQX。
 * 各测试自行使用不同的 stream 键、锁键、clientId 与产品 Key，互不干扰。
 */
public final class TestBroker {

    private TestBroker() {
    }

    /** Redis 容器与连接（首次访问时启动）。 */
    private static final class RedisHolder {
        static final GenericContainer<?> CONTAINER = start(
                new GenericContainer<>(DockerImageName.parse("redis:7.4.2")).withExposedPorts(6379));
        static final StringRedisTemplate TEMPLATE = template();

        private static StringRedisTemplate template() {
            LettuceConnectionFactory factory = new LettuceConnectionFactory(
                    new RedisStandaloneConfiguration(CONTAINER.getHost(), CONTAINER.getMappedPort(6379)));
            factory.afterPropertiesSet();
            return new StringRedisTemplate(factory);
        }
    }

    /** EMQX 容器（首次访问时启动），配置与 deploy/docker-compose.yml 的持久会话设置一致。 */
    private static final class EmqxHolder {
        static final GenericContainer<?> CONTAINER = start(
                new GenericContainer<>(DockerImageName.parse("emqx/emqx:5.8.6"))
                        .withExposedPorts(1883)
                        .withCopyToContainer(MountableFile.forClasspathResource("emqx/acl.conf"),
                                "/opt/emqx/etc/acl.conf")
                        .withEnv("EMQX_SYS_TOPICS__SYS_EVENT_MESSAGES__CLIENT_CONNECTED", "true")
                        .withEnv("EMQX_SYS_TOPICS__SYS_EVENT_MESSAGES__CLIENT_DISCONNECTED", "true")
                        .withEnv("EMQX_DURABLE_SESSIONS__ENABLE", "true")
                        .withEnv("EMQX_CLUSTER__DISCOVERY_STRATEGY", "singleton")
                        .withEnv("EMQX_NODE__NAME", "emqx@127.0.0.1")
                        .waitingFor(Wait.forLogMessage(".*is running now.*", 1))
                        .withStartupTimeout(Duration.ofMinutes(2)));
    }

    private static GenericContainer<?> start(GenericContainer<?> container) {
        container.start();
        return container;
    }

    /** 连接共享 Redis 的模板。 */
    public static StringRedisTemplate redis() {
        return RedisHolder.TEMPLATE;
    }

    public static String redisHost() {
        return RedisHolder.CONTAINER.getHost();
    }

    public static int redisPort() {
        return RedisHolder.CONTAINER.getMappedPort(6379);
    }

    public static String emqxHost() {
        return EmqxHolder.CONTAINER.getHost();
    }

    public static int emqxPort() {
        return EmqxHolder.CONTAINER.getMappedPort(1883);
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.leader;

import dev.conduit.transport.support.TestBroker;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

/** RedisLeaderLock 集成测试（真实 Redis）：只有一个实例抢到锁、只有持锁者能续期与释放、锁过期后他人接管。 */
class RedisLeaderLockIT {

    final StringRedisTemplate redis = TestBroker.redis();
    final String key = "it:leader:" + UUID.randomUUID();
    final Duration ttl = Duration.ofSeconds(2);
    final RedisLeaderLock a = new RedisLeaderLock(redis, key, ttl, "instance-a");
    final RedisLeaderLock b = new RedisLeaderLock(redis, key, ttl, "instance-b");

    @AfterEach
    void removeKey() {
        redis.delete(key);
    }

    @Test
    void onlyOneInstanceAcquiresAndTheValueIsItsId() {
        assertThat(a.tryAcquire()).isTrue();
        assertThat(b.tryAcquire()).isFalse();
        assertThat(a.tryAcquire()).as("SET NX also refuses the holder").isFalse();

        assertThat(redis.opsForValue().get(key)).isEqualTo("instance-a");
        assertThat(redis.getExpire(key, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)).isBetween(1L, 2000L);
    }

    @Test
    void renewExtendsOnlyTheHoldersLock() throws Exception {
        assertThat(a.tryAcquire()).isTrue();
        Thread.sleep(1200);

        assertThat(a.renew()).isTrue();
        assertThat(redis.getExpire(key, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)).isGreaterThan(1500L);
        assertThat(b.renew()).isFalse();
        assertThat(redis.opsForValue().get(key)).isEqualTo("instance-a");
    }

    @Test
    void anExpiredLockGoesToAnotherInstanceAndTheOldHolderCannotRenewIt() {
        assertThat(a.tryAcquire()).isTrue();

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(5)).pollInterval(Duration.ofMillis(100)).until(b::tryAcquire);

        assertThat(a.renew()).isFalse();
        assertThat(redis.opsForValue().get(key)).isEqualTo("instance-b");
    }

    @Test
    void releaseDeletesOnlyTheHoldersLock() {
        assertThat(a.tryAcquire()).isTrue();

        assertThat(b.release()).isFalse();
        assertThat(redis.hasKey(key)).isTrue();
        assertThat(a.release()).isTrue();
        assertThat(b.tryAcquire()).as("a released lock can be taken at once").isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void instanceIdsCarryTheProcessIdAndDifferPerCall() {
        String id = RedisLeaderLock.newInstanceId();

        assertThat(id).contains("-" + ProcessHandle.current().pid() + "-");
        assertThat(RedisLeaderLock.newInstanceId()).isNotEqualTo(id);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement the lock contract and the Redis lock**

Create `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderLock.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.leader;

/**
 * 选主锁：同一时刻至多一个 transport 实例持有。实现须保证只有持锁实例能续期与释放；
 * 三个操作在 Redis 不可用时抛 {@link org.springframework.dao.DataAccessException}，由 {@link LeaderElection} 按续期失败处理。
 */
public interface LeaderLock {

    /** 本实例的 id（锁的值），日志与健康检查中用于区分实例。 */
    String instanceId();

    /** 尝试抢锁；锁已被任何实例（包括本实例）持有时返回 false。 */
    boolean tryAcquire();

    /** 续期；锁不存在或已被其他实例持有时返回 false。 */
    boolean renew();

    /** 释放；锁不存在或已被其他实例持有时返回 false，不删除。 */
    boolean release();
}
```

Create `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/RedisLeaderLock.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.leader;

import org.springframework.data.redis.connection.RedisStringCommands.SetOption;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisCallback;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.script.DefaultRedisScript;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.types.Expiration;

import java.net.InetAddress;
import java.net.UnknownHostException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * 基于 Redis 的选主锁（V0.2-R spec 3.3）：值为实例 id；抢锁 SET NX PX ttl；续期与释放用 Lua 先比对 id 再
 * PEXPIRE / DEL，保证锁过期并被其他实例抢到后，旧主实例的续期与释放都不生效。
 */
public class RedisLeaderLock implements LeaderLock {

    private static final DefaultRedisScript<Long> RENEW = new DefaultRedisScript<>(
            "if redis.call('GET', KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then return redis.call('PEXPIRE', KEYS[1], ARGV[2]) end "
                    + "return 0", Long.class);
    private static final DefaultRedisScript<Long> RELEASE = new DefaultRedisScript<>(
            "if redis.call('GET', KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then return redis.call('DEL', KEYS[1]) end return 0",
            Long.class);

    private final StringRedisTemplate redis;
    private final String key;
    private final Duration ttl;
    private final String instanceId;

    public RedisLeaderLock(StringRedisTemplate redis, String key, Duration ttl, String instanceId) {
        this.redis = redis;
        this.key = key;
        this.ttl = ttl;
        this.instanceId = instanceId;
    }

    @Override
    public String instanceId() {
        return instanceId;
    }

    /** SET key id NX PX ttl：键不存在时写入本实例 id 并设置过期，返回是否写入成功。 */
    @Override
    public boolean tryAcquire() {
        byte[] k = key.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        byte[] v = instanceId.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        Boolean ok = redis.execute((RedisCallback<Boolean>) c -> c.stringCommands()
                .set(k, v, Expiration.milliseconds(ttl.toMillis()), SetOption.ifAbsent()));
        return Boolean.TRUE.equals(ok);
    }

    /** 值仍为本实例 id 时把过期时间重置为 ttl。 */
    @Override
    public boolean renew() {
        Long result = redis.execute(RENEW, List.of(key), instanceId, Long.toString(ttl.toMillis()));
        return Long.valueOf(1).equals(result);
    }

    /** 值仍为本实例 id 时删除键，备实例可立即接管。 */
    @Override
    public boolean release() {
        Long result = redis.execute(RELEASE, List.of(key), instanceId);
        return Long.valueOf(1).equals(result);
    }

    /**
     * 生成实例 id：主机名-进程号-8 位随机十六进制。主机名取不到时用 unknown-host。
     * 同一主机上的多个进程、同一进程的多次调用都得到不同的 id。
     */
    public static String newInstanceId() {
        String host;
        try {
            host = InetAddress.getLocalHost().getHostName();
        } catch (UnknownHostException e) {
            host = "unknown-host";
        }
        return host + "-" + ProcessHandle.current().pid() + "-" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8);
    }
}
```

## Task 2: Restartable MQTT adapter on a durable session with a plain subscription (track A)

Rewrites the adapter so that the election can start and stop it many times. Each `start()` opens one connection: a fixed client id, `cleanStart=false`, expiry 86400 s and a plain subscription. Each `stop()` drops it. A lost connection or a session taken over by another instance is reported through `needsRestart()` instead of HiveMQ auto-reconnect.

**Files:**
- Modify: `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/adapter/ProtocolAdapter.java` (full replacement)
- Modify: `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/mqtt/MqttTransportProperties.java` (full replacement)
- Modify: `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/mqtt/MqttProtocolAdapter.java` (full replacement)
- Test: `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/mqtt/MqttProtocolAdapterTest.java` (full replacement)
- Test: `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/mqtt/MqttProtocolAdapterIT.java` (full replacement)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `MessageBus.publish(RawMessage)`, `TopicParser.parse(String)`, `ParsedTopic`, `TestBroker` (Task 1), `RedisStreamMessageBus(StringRedisTemplate, MessageBusProperties)`.
- Produces:
  - `interface ProtocolAdapter { String name(); void start(); void stop(); boolean isConnected(); boolean needsRestart(); }`. `start()` returns only after connect and SUBACK succeed. It throws `IllegalStateException` on failure, with resources cleaned up, and can be called again after `stop()`. `stop()` is immediate and idempotent, and may run concurrently with `start()`.
  - `record MqttTransportProperties(String host, int port, String clientId, String username, String password, long sessionExpirySeconds, String ownClientPrefix)`, defaults `localhost`, `1883`, `conduit-transport`, `null`, `null`, `86400`, `conduit-`.
  - `MqttProtocolAdapter(MqttTransportProperties, MessageBus, Clock)` (public) and `(…, Duration initialBackoff)` (package-private). Its constants are `DEVICE_TOPIC_FILTER = "conduit/+/+/up/#"`, `CONNECTED_TOPIC_FILTER`, `DISCONNECTED_TOPIC_FILTER`. Package-private test seams: `Session openSession()`, `void onPublish(Session, Mqtt5Publish)`, `void onDisconnected(Session, MqttClientDisconnectedContext)`, `static void requireGranted(Mqtt5SubAck)`, `static boolean isSessionTakenOver(Throwable)`.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- **No HiveMQ automatic reconnect.** A broker-side or network disconnect sets `needsRestart`. The coordinator in Task 3 re-checks the lock and calls `stop()` + `start()`. This keeps three rules in one place: only the leader connects, an old connection is never resurrected after its session was taken over, and a reconnect re-authenticates. The last rule matters after an EMQX restart, when a client may connect before the HTTP authenticator is ready and then lack superuser rights.
- **SUBACK error codes count as failure.** HiveMQ completes the future normally when only some codes are errors, so `requireGranted` checks every code. A connection whose subscription is refused, such as a non-superuser after an EMQX restart, is torn down and retried.
- **Global filter `ALL` instead of `SUBSCRIBED`.** A new client object does not know the subscriptions stored in the resumed durable session. Messages EMQX replays right after CONNACK would otherwise be classified as unsolicited and never reach the ack-after-XADD path. This adapter registers the only publish flow.
- **Session takeover** (DISCONNECT reason `SESSION_TAKEN_OVER`) logs a WARN and requests a restart. Task 3 restarts only after a successful renewal, so a former leader never fights the new one.
- The connected listener and the subscribe-retry loop are removed. `start()` subscribes synchronously, and later reconnects go through `start()` again.

package dev.conduit.transport.mqtt;

import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.datatypes.MqttTopic;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.lifecycle.MqttClientDisconnectedContext;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.lifecycle.MqttDisconnectSource;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.exceptions.Mqtt5DisconnectException;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.message.disconnect.Mqtt5Disconnect;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.message.disconnect.Mqtt5DisconnectReasonCode;

import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.message.subscribe.suback.Mqtt5SubAck;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.message.subscribe.suback.Mqtt5SubAckReasonCode;
import dev.conduit.common.bus.MessageBus;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.mockito.InOrder;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.ServerSocket;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.List;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.doThrow;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.inOrder;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.times;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

/**
 * MqttProtocolAdapter 单元测试：写入总线成功后才 ACK、停止时放弃重试、自有客户端上下线直接 ACK、
 * 意外断线与会话被接管时请求重启、SUBACK 含错误码视为订阅失败、连不上 broker 时 start 失败且不留状态。
 */
class MqttProtocolAdapterTest {

    MessageBus bus;
    Mqtt5Publish publish;
    MqttProtocolAdapter adapter;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        bus = mock(MessageBus.class);
        publish = mock(Mqtt5Publish.class);
        when(publish.getTopic()).thenReturn(MqttTopic.of("conduit/pk1/dk1/up/property"));
        when(publish.getPayloadAsBytes()).thenReturn(new byte[]{1});
        var props = new MqttTransportProperties("localhost", 1883, "conduit-transport-ut",
                null, null, 86400, "conduit-");
        adapter = new MqttProtocolAdapter(props, bus,
                Clock.fixed(Instant.ofEpochMilli(1), ZoneOffset.UTC), Duration.ofMillis(1));
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        adapter.stop();
    }

    @Test
    void acksOnlyAfterSuccessfulPublishAndRetriesInline() {
        when(bus.publish(any()))
                .thenThrow(new IllegalStateException("redis down"))
                .thenThrow(new IllegalStateException("redis down"))
                .thenReturn("1-0");

        adapter.onPublish(adapter.openSession(), publish);

        InOrder order = inOrder(bus, publish);
        order.verify(bus, times(3)).publish(any());
        order.verify(publish, times(1)).acknowledge();
    }

    @Test
    void doesNotAckWhilePublishKeepsFailingThenGivesUpWhenStopped() throws Exception {
        when(bus.publish(any())).thenThrow(new IllegalStateException("redis down"));
        MqttProtocolAdapter.Session session = adapter.openSession();
        Thread t = Thread.startVirtualThread(() -> adapter.onPublish(session, publish));
        Thread.sleep(50);

        adapter.stop();
        t.join(2000);

        assertThat(t.isAlive()).isFalse();
        verify(publish, never()).acknowledge();
    }

    @Test
    void ackFailureDoesNotRepublish() {
        when(bus.publish(any())).thenReturn("1-0");
        doThrow(new IllegalStateException("connection lost")).when(publish).acknowledge();

        adapter.onPublish(adapter.openSession(), publish);

        verify(bus, times(1)).publish(any());
        verify(publish, times(1)).acknowledge();
    }

    @Test
    void presenceOfThePlatformsOwnClientIsAcknowledgedWithoutPublishing() {
        when(publish.getTopic())
                .thenReturn(MqttTopic.of("$SYS/brokers/emqx@127.0.0.1/clients/conduit-transport/connected"));

        adapter.onPublish(adapter.openSession(), publish);

        verify(bus, never()).publish(any());
        verify(publish).acknowledge();
    }

    @Test
    void anUnexpectedDisconnectRequestsARestart() {
        MqttProtocolAdapter.Session session = adapter.openSession();

        adapter.onDisconnected(session, disconnected(MqttDisconnectSource.SERVER,
                new IllegalStateException("broker restarted")));

        assertThat(adapter.needsRestart()).isTrue();
        assertThat(adapter.isConnected()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void aDisconnectByTheAdapterItselfDoesNotRequestARestart() {
        MqttProtocolAdapter.Session session = adapter.openSession();

        adapter.onDisconnected(session, disconnected(MqttDisconnectSource.USER, null));

        assertThat(adapter.needsRestart()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void stopClearsTheRestartRequest() {
        MqttProtocolAdapter.Session session = adapter.openSession();
        adapter.onDisconnected(session, disconnected(MqttDisconnectSource.CLIENT, null));

        adapter.stop();

        assertThat(adapter.needsRestart()).isFalse();
        assertThat(adapter.isConnected()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void recognisesASessionTakenOverByAnotherConnection() {
        Mqtt5Disconnect takenOver = mock(Mqtt5Disconnect.class);
        when(takenOver.getReasonCode()).thenReturn(Mqtt5DisconnectReasonCode.SESSION_TAKEN_OVER);
        Mqtt5Disconnect shutdown = mock(Mqtt5Disconnect.class);
        when(shutdown.getReasonCode()).thenReturn(Mqtt5DisconnectReasonCode.SERVER_SHUTTING_DOWN);

        assertThat(MqttProtocolAdapter.isSessionTakenOver(new Mqtt5DisconnectException(takenOver, "taken over")))
                .isTrue();
        assertThat(MqttProtocolAdapter.isSessionTakenOver(new Mqtt5DisconnectException(shutdown, "shutdown")))
                .isFalse();
        assertThat(MqttProtocolAdapter.isSessionTakenOver(new IllegalStateException("other"))).isFalse();
        assertThat(MqttProtocolAdapter.isSessionTakenOver(null)).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void aSubAckWithAnyErrorCodeCountsAsAFailedSubscription() {
        Mqtt5SubAck partial = mock(Mqtt5SubAck.class);
        when(partial.getReasonCodes()).thenReturn(List.of(Mqtt5SubAckReasonCode.GRANTED_QOS_1,
                Mqtt5SubAckReasonCode.NOT_AUTHORIZED, Mqtt5SubAckReasonCode.GRANTED_QOS_1));
        Mqtt5SubAck granted = mock(Mqtt5SubAck.class);
        when(granted.getReasonCodes()).thenReturn(List.of(Mqtt5SubAckReasonCode.GRANTED_QOS_1,
                Mqtt5SubAckReasonCode.GRANTED_QOS_1, Mqtt5SubAckReasonCode.GRANTED_QOS_1));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> MqttProtocolAdapter.requireGranted(partial))
                .isInstanceOf(IllegalStateException.class)
                .hasMessageContaining("NOT_AUTHORIZED");
        MqttProtocolAdapter.requireGranted(granted);
    }

    @Test
    void startFailsCleanlyWhenTheBrokerIsUnreachable() throws IOException {
        int closedPort;
        try (ServerSocket socket = new ServerSocket(0)) {
            closedPort = socket.getLocalPort();
        }
        var props = new MqttTransportProperties("127.0.0.1", closedPort, "conduit-transport-ut",
                null, null, 86400, "conduit-");
        MqttProtocolAdapter unreachable = new MqttProtocolAdapter(props, bus, Clock.systemUTC());

        assertThatThrownBy(unreachable::start)
                .isInstanceOf(IllegalStateException.class)
                .hasMessageContaining("127.0.0.1:" + closedPort);
        assertThat(unreachable.isConnected()).isFalse();
        assertThat(unreachable.needsRestart()).isFalse();
    }

    private static MqttClientDisconnectedContext disconnected(MqttDisconnectSource source, Throwable cause) {
        MqttClientDisconnectedContext context = mock(MqttClientDisconnectedContext.class);
        when(context.getSource()).thenReturn(source);
        when(context.getCause()).thenReturn(cause);
        return context;
    }
}

- [ ] **Step 3: Replace the adapter contract**

Replace `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/adapter/ProtocolAdapter.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.adapter;

/**
 * 协议适配器：把某种设备协议的消息转换为 RawMessage 投递到总线。新增协议（HTTP、Modbus TCP 等）只需新增一个实现。
 * 生命周期由选主协调器 LeaderElection 驱动：只有主实例启动适配器，下台或进程停止时立即停止；同一实例可多次 start / stop。
 */
public interface ProtocolAdapter {

    /** 适配器名称，用于日志。 */
    String name();

    /**
     * 建立连接并开始接收，就绪（连接与订阅都成功）后才返回。
     *
     * @throws IllegalStateException 连接或订阅失败、超时或被并发 stop；资源已清理，调用方可稍后重试
     */
    void start();

    /** 立即停止接收并断开，幂等；可与进行中的 start 并发调用，使其失败返回。 */
    void stop();

    /** 当前是否已连接并在接收。 */
    boolean isConnected();

    /** 连接已断开且不会自行恢复（断线、会话被接管等），需由调用方 stop 后重新 start。 */
    boolean needsRestart();
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.mqtt;

import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.bind.DefaultValue;

/**
 * transport 连接 EMQX 的 MQTT 配置（前缀 conduit.transport.mqtt）。
 *
 * @param clientId             所有 transport 实例共用的 clientId：EMQX 磁盘持久会话按 clientId 保存离线积压，新主实例接管同一会话
 * @param username             为空表示匿名连接（仅测试环境）
 * @param sessionExpirySeconds 会话保留秒数，与 EMQX 持久会话消息保留期（1 天）一致
 * @param ownClientPrefix      平台内部客户端的 clientId 前缀，这些客户端的上下线事件不转发
 */
@ConfigurationProperties(prefix = "conduit.transport.mqtt")
public record MqttTransportProperties(
        @DefaultValue("localhost") String host,
        @DefaultValue("1883") int port,
        @DefaultValue("conduit-transport") String clientId,
        String username,
        String password,
        @DefaultValue("86400") long sessionExpirySeconds,
        @DefaultValue("conduit-") String ownClientPrefix) {
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Replace the adapter**

Replace `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/mqtt/MqttProtocolAdapter.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.mqtt;

import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.MqttClient;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.MqttGlobalPublishFilter;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.datatypes.MqttQos;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.lifecycle.MqttClientDisconnectedContext;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.lifecycle.MqttDisconnectSource;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.Mqtt5AsyncClient;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.Mqtt5ClientBuilder;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.exceptions.Mqtt5DisconnectException;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.message.disconnect.Mqtt5DisconnectReasonCode;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.message.publish.Mqtt5Publish;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.message.subscribe.suback.Mqtt5SubAck;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.message.subscribe.suback.Mqtt5SubAckReasonCode;
import dev.conduit.common.bus.MessageBus;
import dev.conduit.common.message.MessageType;
import dev.conduit.common.message.RawMessage;
import dev.conduit.transport.adapter.ProtocolAdapter;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

/**
 * MQTT 协议适配器：以固定 clientId、cleanStart=false 的会话普通订阅设备上行 topic 与 EMQX 的 $SYS 上下线 topic，
 * XADD 成功后才回 PUBACK（手动 ACK），保证至少一次投递。EMQX 开启磁盘持久会话，transport 离线期间的消息保存在会话中，
 * 下次 start（本实例或接管的新主实例）时补发。
 *
 * <p>每次 start 打开一个独立的 {@link Session}（客户端、投递线程、状态标志），stop 关闭它，可反复调用。
 * 不使用 HiveMQ 自动重连：意外断线或会话被接管时置 needsRestart，由选主协调器确认仍持锁后重新 start。
 */
public class MqttProtocolAdapter implements ProtocolAdapter {

    static final String DEVICE_TOPIC_FILTER = "conduit/+/+/up/#";
    static final String CONNECTED_TOPIC_FILTER = "$SYS/brokers/+/clients/+/connected";
    static final String DISCONNECTED_TOPIC_FILTER = "$SYS/brokers/+/clients/+/disconnected";
    static final Duration CONNECT_TIMEOUT = Duration.ofSeconds(10);
    static final Duration SUBSCRIBE_TIMEOUT = Duration.ofSeconds(10);

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(MqttProtocolAdapter.class);
    private static final Duration DISCONNECT_TIMEOUT = Duration.ofSeconds(5);
    private static final Duration MAX_RETRY_DELAY = Duration.ofSeconds(30);

    private final MqttTransportProperties props;
    private final MessageBus bus;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final Duration initialBackoff;
    private final AtomicReference<Session> current = new AtomicReference<>();

    /** 一次 start 到 stop 之间的连接及其资源；停止后即作废，不再复用。 */
    final class Session {
        // 单线程串行处理：保序，且阻塞重试不会卡住 Netty 事件循环
        final ExecutorService deliveryExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(
                Thread.ofPlatform().name("mqtt-delivery").daemon().factory());
        volatile Mqtt5AsyncClient client;
        volatile boolean stopping;
        volatile boolean needsRestart;

        boolean connected() {
            Mqtt5AsyncClient c = client;
            return c != null && c.getState().isConnected();
        }
    }

    public MqttProtocolAdapter(MqttTransportProperties props, MessageBus bus, Clock clock) {
        this(props, bus, clock, Duration.ofSeconds(1));
    }

    MqttProtocolAdapter(MqttTransportProperties props, MessageBus bus, Clock clock, Duration initialBackoff) {
        this.props = props;
        this.bus = bus;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.initialBackoff = initialBackoff;
    }

    @Override
    public String name() {
        return "mqtt";
    }

    /**
     * 关闭上一次的连接（若有），新建客户端并连接 EMQX（MQTT 5，cleanStart=false，会话保留 sessionExpirySeconds），
     * 再同步订阅并检查 SUBACK。先注册全局回调再连接：恢复持久会话后 broker 会立即补发积压消息。
     *
     * @throws IllegalStateException 连接或订阅失败、超时、被中断或被并发 stop；会话已清理
     */
    @Override
    public void start() {
        stop();
        Session s = openSession();
        Mqtt5ClientBuilder builder = MqttClient.builder().useMqttVersion5()
                .identifier(props.clientId())
                .serverHost(props.host())
                .serverPort(props.port())
                .addDisconnectedListener(context -> onDisconnected(s, context));
        if (props.username() != null && !props.username().isBlank()) {
            byte[] password = props.password() == null
                    ? new byte[0] : props.password().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            builder = builder.simpleAuth().username(props.username()).password(password).applySimpleAuth();
        }
        Mqtt5AsyncClient client = builder.buildAsync();
        s.client = client;
        // ALL 而非 SUBSCRIBED：新客户端对象不知道持久会话里已有的订阅，恢复会话后补发的消息也必须走写总线再 ACK 的路径
        client.publishes(MqttGlobalPublishFilter.ALL, p -> onPublish(s, p), s.deliveryExecutor, true);

        try {
            client.connectWith()
                    .cleanStart(false)
                    .sessionExpiryInterval(props.sessionExpirySeconds())
                    .send().get(CONNECT_TIMEOUT.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
            requireGranted(subscribe(client).get(SUBSCRIBE_TIMEOUT.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS));
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            abandon(s);
            throw new IllegalStateException("Interrupted while connecting to MQTT " + endpoint(), e);
        } catch (ExecutionException e) {
            abandon(s);
            throw new IllegalStateException("MQTT connect/subscribe to " + endpoint() + " failed: "
                    + describe(e.getCause()), e.getCause());
        } catch (TimeoutException e) {
            abandon(s);
            throw new IllegalStateException("MQTT connect/subscribe to " + endpoint() + " timed out", e);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            abandon(s);
            throw new IllegalStateException("MQTT connect/subscribe to " + endpoint() + " failed: "
                    + describe(e), e);
        }
        if (s.stopping) {
            abandon(s);
            throw new IllegalStateException("MQTT adapter was stopped while connecting to " + endpoint());
        }
        log.info("MQTT connected to {} as {} (session expiry {} s), subscribed to {}", endpoint(), props.clientId(),
                props.sessionExpirySeconds(), DEVICE_TOPIC_FILTER);
    }

    /** 停止当前连接：置停止标志、有界等待断开、关闭投递线程；未 ACK 的消息留在 EMQX 会话中，下次 start 时重投。 */
    @Override
    public void stop() {
        Session s = current.getAndSet(null);
        if (s != null) {
            close(s);
            log.info("MQTT adapter stopped ({} as {})", endpoint(), props.clientId());
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isConnected() {
        Session s = current.get();
        return s != null && !s.stopping && s.connected();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean needsRestart() {
        Session s = current.get();
        return s != null && s.needsRestart;
    }

    /** 新建并登记当前会话（测试接缝：单元测试不连接 broker，直接用会话驱动 onPublish / onDisconnected）。 */
    Session openSession() {
        Session s = new Session();
        current.set(s);
        return s;
    }

    /** start 失败时作废会话：若仍是当前会话则清除登记，再关闭。 */
    private void abandon(Session s) {
        current.compareAndSet(s, null);
        close(s);
    }

    /** 关闭会话，可重复调用：断开失败只记 DEBUG（broker 已不可达时属正常）。 */
    private void close(Session s) {
        s.stopping = true;
        Mqtt5AsyncClient c = s.client;
        if (c != null) {
            try {
                c.disconnect().get(DISCONNECT_TIMEOUT.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            } catch (ExecutionException | TimeoutException | RuntimeException e) {
                log.debug("MQTT disconnect did not complete cleanly: {}", describe(e));
            }
        }
        s.deliveryExecutor.shutdownNow();
    }

    /**
     * 连接断开回调（在事件循环上，只设标志与打日志）。本适配器主动断开或会话已停止时忽略；
     * 其余情况（broker 重启、网络断开、会话被同 clientId 的新连接接管）置 needsRestart，由协调器决定是否重连。
     */
    void onDisconnected(Session s, MqttClientDisconnectedContext context) {
        if (s.stopping || context.getSource() == MqttDisconnectSource.USER) {
            log.debug("MQTT disconnected from {} by this adapter", endpoint());
            return;
        }
        s.needsRestart = true;
        Throwable cause = context.getCause();
        if (isSessionTakenOver(cause)) {
            log.warn("MQTT session {} on {} was taken over by another connection with the same client id",
                    props.clientId(), endpoint());
        } else {
            log.warn("MQTT connection to {} lost (source={}): {}", endpoint(), context.getSource(),
                    cause == null ? "no cause" : cause.getMessage());
        }
    }

    /** 断开原因是否为「会话被接管」（EMQX 收到同 clientId 的新连接，DISCONNECT 原因码 0x8E）。 */
    static boolean isSessionTakenOver(Throwable cause) {
        return cause instanceof Mqtt5DisconnectException e
                && e.getMqttMessage().getReasonCode() == Mqtt5DisconnectReasonCode.SESSION_TAKEN_OVER;
    }

    /**
     * SUBACK 中任一订阅被拒（错误码）即视为订阅失败。HiveMQ 只在全部被拒时才让 future 异常完成，部分被拒需在此检查。
     *
     * @throws IllegalStateException 含错误码，消息中列出全部原因码
     */
    static void requireGranted(Mqtt5SubAck ack) {
        List<Mqtt5SubAckReasonCode> codes = ack.getReasonCodes();
        if (codes.stream().anyMatch(Mqtt5SubAckReasonCode::isError)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("MQTT subscription rejected: " + codes);
        }
    }

    /** 订阅设备上行 topic（普通订阅，磁盘持久会话不支持共享订阅）及 EMQX 的上下线系统 topic，均为 QoS 1。 */
    private CompletableFuture<Mqtt5SubAck> subscribe(Mqtt5AsyncClient client) {
        return client.subscribeWith()
                .addSubscription().topicFilter(DEVICE_TOPIC_FILTER).qos(MqttQos.AT_LEAST_ONCE).applySubscription()
                .addSubscription().topicFilter(CONNECTED_TOPIC_FILTER).qos(MqttQos.AT_LEAST_ONCE).applySubscription()
                .addSubscription().topicFilter(DISCONNECTED_TOPIC_FILTER).qos(MqttQos.AT_LEAST_ONCE)
                .applySubscription()
                .send();
    }

    /**
     * 处理一条收到的 MQTT 消息（在会话的单线程投递线程上串行执行，保序）。
     * 无法路由的 topic 与平台自身客户端（ownClientPrefix）的上下线事件直接 ACK 丢弃；
     * 其余组装为 RawMessage 并阻塞重试发布到总线，发布成功后才 ACK；会话停止时放弃且不 ACK。
     */
    void onPublish(Session s, Mqtt5Publish publish) {
        String topic = publish.getTopic().toString();
        Optional<ParsedTopic> parsed = TopicParser.parse(topic);
        if (parsed.isEmpty()) {
            log.warn("Dropping unroutable topic {}", topic);
            acknowledge(publish);
            return;
        }
        ParsedTopic t = parsed.get();
        if (isPresence(t.type()) && t.deviceKey().startsWith(props.ownClientPrefix())) {
            log.debug("Ignoring presence of platform client {}", t.deviceKey());
            acknowledge(publish);
            return;
        }
        RawMessage message = new RawMessage(t.productKey(), t.deviceKey(), t.type(),
                publish.getPayloadAsBytes(), clock.millis(), Map.of("topic", topic));
        if (isPresence(t.type())) {
            log.debug("MQTT presence event: clientId={} {}", t.deviceKey(),
                    t.type() == MessageType.PRESENCE_CONNECTED ? "connected" : "disconnected");
        } else {
            log.debug("MQTT received: topic={} productKey={} deviceKey={} type={} payloadBytes={}", topic,
                    t.productKey(), t.deviceKey(), t.type(), message.payload().length);
        }
        if (log.isTraceEnabled()) {
            log.trace("MQTT payload of {}: {}", t.deviceKey(), preview(message.payload()));
        }
        if (publishWithRetry(s, message)) {
            acknowledge(publish);
        }
    }

    /** 取 payload 的前 256 个字符用于 TRACE 日志（按 UTF-8 解码，避免大报文刷屏）。 */
    private static String preview(byte[] payload) {
        String text = new String(payload, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        return text.length() <= 256 ? text : text.substring(0, 256) + "...(truncated)";
    }

    /** 内联重试直到成功；会话停止或线程被中断时放弃（不 ACK，由 broker 经持久会话重投）。 */
    private boolean publishWithRetry(Session s, RawMessage message) {
        for (int attempt = 0; !s.stopping; attempt++) {
            try {
                String id = bus.publish(message);
                log.debug("Published to bus: id={} deviceKey={} type={}", id, message.deviceKey(), message.type());
                return true;
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                Duration delay = backoff(attempt);
                log.warn("Bus publish failed for device {} (attempt {}), retrying in {} ms: {}",
                        message.deviceKey(), attempt + 1, delay.toMillis(), e.getMessage());
                try {
                    Thread.sleep(delay);
                } catch (InterruptedException ie) {
                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /** 指数退避：initialBackoff * 2^attempt（指数封顶 5），上限 30 秒。 */
    private Duration backoff(int attempt) {
        Duration delay = initialBackoff.multipliedBy(1L << Math.min(attempt, 5));
        return delay.compareTo(MAX_RETRY_DELAY) > 0 ? MAX_RETRY_DELAY : delay;
    }

    /** ACK 失败不能当作总线失败重投：消息已入总线，重复投递由 broker 重发（至少一次）。 */
    private void acknowledge(Mqtt5Publish publish) {
        try {
            publish.acknowledge();
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            log.warn("Failed to acknowledge MQTT publish {}: {}", publish.getTopic(), e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    /** 是否为设备上下线事件（而非业务数据）。 */
    private static boolean isPresence(MessageType type) {
        return type == MessageType.PRESENCE_CONNECTED || type == MessageType.PRESENCE_DISCONNECTED;
    }

    private String endpoint() {
        return props.host() + ":" + props.port();
    }

    private static String describe(Throwable e) {
        return e == null ? "unknown" : e.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + e.getMessage();
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Replace the adapter integration test**

Replace `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/mqtt/MqttProtocolAdapterIT.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.mqtt;

import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.MqttClient;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.datatypes.MqttQos;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.Mqtt5BlockingClient;
import dev.conduit.common.bus.Delivery;
import dev.conduit.common.bus.MessageBus;
import dev.conduit.common.bus.MessageBusProperties;
import dev.conduit.common.bus.RedisStreamMessageBus;
import dev.conduit.common.message.MessageType;
import dev.conduit.common.message.RawMessage;
import dev.conduit.transport.support.TestBroker;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

/**
 * MqttProtocolAdapter 对真实 EMQX（磁盘持久会话）的集成测试：属性与上下线事件进入总线、无法路由的 topic 不阻塞、
 * 停止期间发布的 2000 条在重新 start 后全部收到、同 clientId 的新连接踢掉旧连接且旧连接不自行重连。
 */
class MqttProtocolAdapterIT {

    static final String GROUP = "it";
    static final long NOW = 1_760_400_000_000L;

    final StringRedisTemplate redis = TestBroker.redis();
    final String streamKey = "it:up:" + UUID.randomUUID();
    final String clientId = "conduit-transport-it-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    final String productKey = "pk" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8);
    final List<MqttProtocolAdapter> adapters = new ArrayList<>();
    final List<Mqtt5BlockingClient> devices = new ArrayList<>();
    MessageBus bus;
    MqttProtocolAdapter adapter;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        bus = new RedisStreamMessageBus(redis, new MessageBusProperties(streamKey, "it:dead", 100_000, 1000));
        bus.ensureGroup(GROUP);
        adapter = newAdapter();
        adapter.start();
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        devices.forEach(d -> {
            if (d.getState().isConnected()) {
                d.disconnect();
            }
        });
        adapters.forEach(MqttProtocolAdapter::stop);
        redis.delete(streamKey);
    }

    private MqttProtocolAdapter newAdapter() {
        var props = new MqttTransportProperties(TestBroker.emqxHost(), TestBroker.emqxPort(), clientId,
                null, null, 86400, "conduit-");
        MqttProtocolAdapter a = new MqttProtocolAdapter(props, bus,
                Clock.fixed(Instant.ofEpochMilli(NOW), ZoneOffset.UTC));
        adapters.add(a);
        return a;
    }

    private Mqtt5BlockingClient device(String deviceKey) {
        Mqtt5BlockingClient client = MqttClient.builder().useMqttVersion5()
                .identifier(deviceKey)
                .serverHost(TestBroker.emqxHost()).serverPort(TestBroker.emqxPort())
                .buildBlocking();
        client.connect();
        devices.add(client);
        return client;
    }

    private void publish(Mqtt5BlockingClient device, String deviceKey, String suffix, byte[] payload) {
        device.publishWith().topic("conduit/" + productKey + "/" + deviceKey + "/up/" + suffix)
                .qos(MqttQos.AT_LEAST_ONCE).payload(payload).send();
    }

    private List<Delivery> drain() {
        List<Delivery> all = new ArrayList<>();
        List<Delivery> batch;
        while (!(batch = bus.read(GROUP, "test", 500, Duration.ofMillis(50))).isEmpty()) {
            all.addAll(batch);
        }
        return all;
    }

    @Test
    void forwardsPropertyMessageToBus() {
        byte[] payload = "{\"params\":{\"Ua\":220.1}}".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        publish(device("dk-prop"), "dk-prop", "property", payload);

        List<Delivery> received = new ArrayList<>();
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(10)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            received.addAll(drain());
            assertThat(received).anySatisfy(d -> assertThat(d.message().type()).isEqualTo(MessageType.PROPERTY));
        });

        RawMessage message = received.stream().map(Delivery::message)
                .filter(m -> m.type() == MessageType.PROPERTY).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        assertThat(message.productKey()).isEqualTo(productKey);
        assertThat(message.deviceKey()).isEqualTo("dk-prop");
        assertThat(message.payload()).containsExactly(payload);
        assertThat(message.receivedAt()).isEqualTo(NOW);
        assertThat(message.attributes()).containsEntry("topic", "conduit/" + productKey + "/dk-prop/up/property");
    }

    @Test
    void forwardsPresenceEventsButIgnoresOwnClient() {
        Mqtt5BlockingClient own = device("conduit-internal-" + UUID.randomUUID());
        own.disconnect();
        String deviceKey = "dk-presence-" + UUID.randomUUID();
        Mqtt5BlockingClient client = device(deviceKey);
        client.disconnect();

        List<Delivery> received = new ArrayList<>();
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(10)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            received.addAll(drain());
            assertThat(received).anySatisfy(d -> {
                assertThat(d.message().deviceKey()).isEqualTo(deviceKey);
                assertThat(d.message().type()).isEqualTo(MessageType.PRESENCE_DISCONNECTED);
            });
        });

        assertThat(received).anySatisfy(d -> {
            assertThat(d.message().deviceKey()).isEqualTo(deviceKey);
            assertThat(d.message().type()).isEqualTo(MessageType.PRESENCE_CONNECTED);
        });
        // 自有客户端的事件先于本设备产生，此时必已处理完毕
        assertThat(received).noneSatisfy(d -> assertThat(d.message().deviceKey()).startsWith("conduit-"));
    }

    @Test
    void dropsUnroutableTopicWithoutBlocking() {
        Mqtt5BlockingClient client = device("dk-bad");
        publish(client, "dk-bad", "unknown", new byte[]{1});
        publish(client, "dk-bad", "property", new byte[]{2});

        List<Delivery> received = new ArrayList<>();
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(10)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            received.addAll(drain());
            assertThat(received).anySatisfy(d -> assertThat(d.message().type()).isEqualTo(MessageType.PROPERTY));
        });
        assertThat(received).noneSatisfy(d ->
                assertThat(d.message().attributes().get("topic")).endsWith("/up/unknown"));
    }

    @Test
    void receivesEveryMessagePublishedWhileStopped() {
        adapter.stop();
        Mqtt5BlockingClient client = device("dk-offline");
        for (int i = 1; i <= 2000; i++) {
            publish(client, "dk-offline", "property", ("{\"seq\":" + i + "}").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        }

        adapter.start();

        Set<String> payloads = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(60)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            drain().stream().map(Delivery::message)
                    .filter(m -> m.type() == MessageType.PROPERTY && productKey.equals(m.productKey()))
                    .forEach(m -> payloads.add(new String(m.payload(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
            assertThat(payloads).hasSize(2000);
        });
    }

    @Test
    void aSecondConnectionWithTheSameClientIdKicksTheFirstWhichStaysDown() throws Exception {
        MqttProtocolAdapter second = newAdapter();

        second.start();

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(10)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            assertThat(adapter.isConnected()).isFalse();
            assertThat(adapter.needsRestart()).isTrue();
        });
        assertThat(second.isConnected()).isTrue();
        Thread.sleep(2000);
        assertThat(adapter.isConnected()).as("the kicked connection does not reconnect by itself").isFalse();
        assertThat(second.isConnected()).isTrue();
    }
}
```
