## Task 3: Leader election, health role, wiring, and EMQX/transport compose settings (track A)

Adds the coordinator that only lets the leader run the adapter, and the health details. It wires both into the application, removes `AdapterLifecycle`, and switches the compose file: EMQX gets durable sessions, and the full deployment gets two transports.

**Files:**
- Create: `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderElection.java`
- Create: `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderHealthIndicator.java`
- Modify: `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/TransportApplication.java` (full replacement)
- Modify: `conduit-transport/src/main/resources/application.yml` (full replacement)
- Delete: `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/adapter/AdapterLifecycle.java`
- Modify: `deploy/docker-compose.yml` (the `emqx` environment and the `conduit-transport` service)
- Test: `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderElectionTest.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderElectionIT.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/TransportApplicationIT.java` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LeaderLock`, `RedisLeaderLock`, `LeaderProperties` (Task 1); `ProtocolAdapter`, `MqttProtocolAdapter`, `MqttTransportProperties` (Task 2); `TestBroker`.
- Produces:
  - `LeaderElection implements SmartLifecycle`: public constructor `(LeaderLock, List<ProtocolAdapter>, LeaderProperties)` and a package-private one `(…, Duration superviseInterval, Duration initialBackoff, Duration maxBackoff)`. Nested `enum Role { LEADER, STANDBY }`. Public methods `role()`, `instanceId()`, `adaptersConnected()`.
  - `LeaderHealthIndicator(LeaderElection)`, bean name `leaderHealthIndicator`. Health component `leader` with details `role`, `instanceId`, `connected`, always status `UP` (the contract in Global Constraints).
  - Config keys `conduit.transport.leader.{key,ttl,renew-interval,acquire-interval}`, `spring.data.redis.timeout: 2s`, `management.endpoint.health.show-details: always`.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- One scheduler thread (`transport-leader`) runs three fixed-delay ticks: acquire every `acquire-interval` while standby, renew every `renew-interval` while leader, and supervise every second. Lock operations therefore never run concurrently. Adapter `start()` runs on a separate thread (`transport-adapter`), so a slow connect never delays a renewal. Renewal failure calls `adapter.stop()` directly on the scheduler thread, which is the spec's "立即断开".
- A term counter increases on every role change. A start loop from an old term stops itself, and it also stops the adapter if it finished connecting after the step-down.
- Adapter start retries back off from 1 s to at most 10 s. This keeps recovery after an EMQX restart within about 10 s of the broker coming back.
- Before restarting an adapter that `needsRestart()`, the supervisor renews the lock. If the renewal fails, it steps down instead. A former leader whose session was taken over therefore never kicks the new leader.
- "Renewal timeout" is the Redis command timeout. The transport sets `spring.data.redis.timeout: 2s`, below the 5 s renew interval, so a hung Redis fails the renewal instead of blocking past the TTL.
- The health status is always `UP`, because a standby is healthy. The role is in the details, and `show-details: always` exposes them. The transport port is not published in compose.
- **Compose: two service definitions, not `deploy.replicas: 2`.** Compose v2 would honour `replicas`, since `conduit-transport` has no `container_name` and no host port. Both replicas would then get the same `CONDUIT_LOG_DIR` and roll the same `conduit-transport.log` on the shared volume `conduit-logs`. `conduit-transport-2` reuses the first definition through a YAML anchor and only moves its log directory to `/var/log/conduit/transport-2`. Both services use the image `conduit-conduit-transport:latest`, which `deploy/build-local.sh` already builds.
- **EMQX gets a fixed node name `emqx@127.0.0.1`.** Without it, the image's entrypoint derives the node name from the container IP. A recreate that changes the IP would start a new data directory and lose the durable sessions. The previous runtime data under the old IP-based node name is no longer used, including any dashboard password changed in the UI, which reverts to `.env`. Task 8 documents this.
- `TRANSPORT_CLIENT_ID: conduit-transport-1` is removed from compose, so both containers use the default `conduit-transport`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing election unit test**

Create `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderElectionTest.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.leader;

import dev.conduit.transport.adapter.ProtocolAdapter;
import dev.conduit.transport.leader.LeaderElection.Role;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

/**
 * LeaderElection 单元测试（内存锁 + 假适配器，毫秒级间隔）：抢到锁才启动适配器、别人持锁时保持备用、
 * 两个实例只有一个成为主、续期失败或 Redis 出错立即停止适配器并转为备用、适配器启动失败按退避重试、
 * 断线后确认仍持锁才重启、失锁时不重启、停止时只释放自己持有的锁。
 */
class LeaderElectionTest {

    static final LeaderProperties PROPS = new LeaderProperties("k", Duration.ofMillis(500),
            Duration.ofMillis(50), Duration.ofMillis(50));
    static final Duration WAIT = Duration.ofSeconds(5);

    final LockState state = new LockState();
    final List<LeaderElection> elections = new ArrayList<>();

    @AfterEach
    void stopAll() {
        elections.forEach(LeaderElection::stop);
    }

    private LeaderElection election(String id, FakeAdapter adapter) {
        LeaderElection e = new LeaderElection(new FakeLock(state, id), List.of(adapter), PROPS,
                Duration.ofMillis(50), Duration.ofMillis(10), Duration.ofMillis(40));
        elections.add(e);
        return e;
    }

    @Test
    void becomesLeaderStartsTheAdapterAndReleasesTheLockOnStop() {
        FakeAdapter adapter = new FakeAdapter();
        LeaderElection e = election("a", adapter);

        e.start();

        await().atMost(WAIT).until(() -> e.role() == Role.LEADER && e.adaptersConnected());
        assertThat(adapter.starts).hasValue(1);
        assertThat(state.holder()).isEqualTo("a");

        e.stop();

        assertThat(e.role()).isEqualTo(Role.STANDBY);
        assertThat(adapter.connected).isFalse();
        assertThat(state.holder()).isNull();
    }

    @Test
    void staysStandbyWhileAnotherInstanceHoldsTheLock() throws Exception {
        state.set("someone-else");
        FakeAdapter adapter = new FakeAdapter();
        LeaderElection e = election("a", adapter);

        e.start();
        Thread.sleep(300);

        assertThat(e.role()).isEqualTo(Role.STANDBY);
        assertThat(adapter.starts).hasValue(0);
        assertThat(e.adaptersConnected()).isFalse();

        state.set(null);
        await().atMost(WAIT).until(() -> e.role() == Role.LEADER && e.adaptersConnected());
    }

    @Test
    void onlyOneOfTwoInstancesBecomesLeader() throws Exception {
        FakeAdapter adapterA = new FakeAdapter();
        FakeAdapter adapterB = new FakeAdapter();
        LeaderElection a = election("a", adapterA);
        LeaderElection b = election("b", adapterB);

        a.start();
        b.start();

        await().atMost(WAIT).until(() -> a.role() == Role.LEADER || b.role() == Role.LEADER);
        for (int i = 0; i < 25; i++) {
            assertThat(elections.stream().filter(e -> e.role() == Role.LEADER).count()).isEqualTo(1);
            Thread.sleep(20);
        }
        LeaderElection standby = a.role() == Role.LEADER ? b : a;
        FakeAdapter standbyAdapter = standby == a ? adapterA : adapterB;
        assertThat(standbyAdapter.starts).hasValue(0);
    }

    @Test
    void aLostLockStopsTheAdapterAndMakesTheInstanceStandby() {
        FakeAdapter adapter = new FakeAdapter();
        LeaderElection e = election("a", adapter);
        e.start();
        await().atMost(WAIT).until(e::adaptersConnected);

        state.set("someone-else");

        await().atMost(WAIT).until(() -> e.role() == Role.STANDBY);
        assertThat(adapter.connected).isFalse();
        assertThat(adapter.stops.get()).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(1);
    }

    @Test
    void aRedisErrorDuringRenewalStepsDownAndTheInstanceReturnsWhenRedisRecovers() {
        FakeAdapter adapter = new FakeAdapter();
        LeaderElection e = election("a", adapter);
        e.start();
        await().atMost(WAIT).until(e::adaptersConnected);

        state.redisDown(true);

        await().atMost(WAIT).until(() -> e.role() == Role.STANDBY && !adapter.connected);

        state.set(null); // 锁已过期
        state.redisDown(false);
        await().atMost(WAIT).until(() -> e.role() == Role.LEADER && e.adaptersConnected());
    }

    @Test
    void retriesAFailingAdapterStartWithBackoffUntilItConnects() {
        FakeAdapter adapter = new FakeAdapter();
        adapter.failuresLeft.set(2);
        LeaderElection e = election("a", adapter);

        e.start();

        await().atMost(WAIT).until(e::adaptersConnected);
        assertThat(adapter.starts).hasValue(3);
    }

    @Test
    void restartsAnAdapterThatLostItsConnectionWhileStillLeader() {
        FakeAdapter adapter = new FakeAdapter();
        LeaderElection e = election("a", adapter);
        e.start();
        await().atMost(WAIT).until(e::adaptersConnected);

        adapter.dropConnection();

        await().atMost(WAIT).until(() -> adapter.starts.get() == 2 && adapter.connected);
        assertThat(e.role()).isEqualTo(Role.LEADER);
    }

    @Test
    void doesNotRestartTheAdapterWhenTheLockWasLostMeanwhile() throws Exception {
        FakeAdapter adapter = new FakeAdapter();
        LeaderElection e = election("a", adapter);
        e.start();
        await().atMost(WAIT).until(e::adaptersConnected);

        state.set("new-leader");
        adapter.dropConnection();

        await().atMost(WAIT).until(() -> e.role() == Role.STANDBY);
        Thread.sleep(200);
        assertThat(adapter.starts).hasValue(1);
    }

    @Test
    void stopDoesNotReleaseALockHeldByAnotherInstance() throws Exception {
        state.set("someone-else");
        LeaderElection e = election("a", new FakeAdapter());
        e.start();
        Thread.sleep(150);

        e.stop();

        assertThat(state.holder()).isEqualTo("someone-else");
    }

    /** 多个 FakeLock 共享的锁状态，模拟同一个 Redis 键；redisDown 时所有操作抛异常。 */
    static final class LockState {
        private String holder;
        private boolean redisDown;

        synchronized String holder() {
            return holder;
        }

        synchronized void set(String value) {
            holder = value;
        }

        synchronized void redisDown(boolean down) {
            redisDown = down;
        }
    }

    /** 内存中的 LeaderLock：语义与 RedisLeaderLock 相同（SET NX、比对后续期与释放），不计过期。 */
    static final class FakeLock implements LeaderLock {
        private final LockState state;
        private final String id;

        FakeLock(LockState state, String id) {
            this.state = state;
            this.id = id;
        }

        @Override
        public String instanceId() {
            return id;
        }

        @Override
        public boolean tryAcquire() {
            synchronized (state) {
                check();
                if (state.holder != null) {
                    return false;
                }
                state.holder = id;
                return true;
            }
        }

        @Override
        public boolean renew() {
            synchronized (state) {
                check();
                return id.equals(state.holder);
            }
        }

        @Override
        public boolean release() {
            synchronized (state) {
                check();
                if (!id.equals(state.holder)) {
                    return false;
                }
                state.holder = null;
                return true;
            }
        }

        private void check() {
            if (state.redisDown) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("redis down");
            }
        }
    }

    /** 记录启停次数的假适配器：可设定前 N 次启动失败，dropConnection 模拟断线（需重启）。 */
    static final class FakeAdapter implements ProtocolAdapter {
        final AtomicInteger starts = new AtomicInteger();
        final AtomicInteger stops = new AtomicInteger();
        final AtomicInteger failuresLeft = new AtomicInteger();
        volatile boolean connected;
        volatile boolean restartRequested;

        @Override
        public String name() {
            return "fake";
        }

        @Override
        public void start() {
            starts.incrementAndGet();
            if (failuresLeft.getAndDecrement() > 0) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("broker unreachable");
            }
            connected = true;
            restartRequested = false;
        }

        @Override
        public void stop() {
            stops.incrementAndGet();
            connected = false;
            restartRequested = false;
        }

        @Override
        public boolean isConnected() {
            return connected;
        }

        @Override
        public boolean needsRestart() {
            return restartRequested;
        }

        void dropConnection() {
            connected = false;
            restartRequested = true;
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the election**

Create `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderElection.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.leader;

import dev.conduit.transport.adapter.ProtocolAdapter;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.context.SmartLifecycle;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.RejectedExecutionException;
import java.util.concurrent.ScheduledExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

/**
 * transport 单活多备的选主协调器（V0.2-R spec 3.3），随 Spring 生命周期启停，取代原先的 AdapterLifecycle。
 * 备实例每 acquire-interval 尝试抢锁；抢到后成为主实例，在独立线程上启动全部协议适配器（失败按 1s→10s 退避重试）。
 * 主实例每 renew-interval 续期，续期失败（锁已不属于本实例、Redis 出错或超时）立即停止适配器并转为备用；
 * 每秒检查适配器是否需要重连，重连前先续期确认仍持锁。正常停止时停止适配器并释放锁，备实例可立即接管。
 * Redis 不可用时所有实例都不连接 EMQX，不会出现无锁的主实例。
 */
public class LeaderElection implements SmartLifecycle {

    /** 实例当前角色。 */
    public enum Role { LEADER, STANDBY }

    static final Duration SUPERVISE_INTERVAL = Duration.ofSeconds(1);
    static final Duration INITIAL_BACKOFF = Duration.ofSeconds(1);
    static final Duration MAX_BACKOFF = Duration.ofSeconds(10);

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(LeaderElection.class);

    private final LeaderLock lock;
    private final List<ProtocolAdapter> adapters;
    private final LeaderProperties props;
    private final Duration superviseInterval;
    private final Duration initialBackoff;
    private final Duration maxBackoff;

    /** 每次角色变化加一；旧任期的启动循环据此放弃。 */
    private final AtomicLong term = new AtomicLong();
    private final AtomicBoolean restarting = new AtomicBoolean();
    private volatile Role role = Role.STANDBY;
    private volatile boolean running;
    private ScheduledExecutorService scheduler;
    private ExecutorService adapterExecutor;

    public LeaderElection(LeaderLock lock, List<ProtocolAdapter> adapters, LeaderProperties props) {
        this(lock, adapters, props, SUPERVISE_INTERVAL, INITIAL_BACKOFF, MAX_BACKOFF);
    }

    LeaderElection(LeaderLock lock, List<ProtocolAdapter> adapters, LeaderProperties props,
                   Duration superviseInterval, Duration initialBackoff, Duration maxBackoff) {
        this.lock = lock;
        this.adapters = List.copyOf(adapters);
        this.props = props;
        this.superviseInterval = superviseInterval;
        this.initialBackoff = initialBackoff;
        this.maxBackoff = maxBackoff;
    }

    public Role role() {
        return role;
    }

    public String instanceId() {
        return lock.instanceId();
    }

    /** 是否为主实例且全部适配器已连接（健康检查的 connected）。 */
    public boolean adaptersConnected() {
        return role == Role.LEADER && !adapters.isEmpty() && adapters.stream().allMatch(ProtocolAdapter::isConnected);
    }

    /** 启动调度线程：立即尝试一次抢锁，之后按配置的间隔抢锁、续期与检查适配器。重复调用无效果。 */
    @Override
    public synchronized void start() {
        if (running) {
            return;
        }
        running = true;
        scheduler = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor(
                Thread.ofPlatform().name("transport-leader").daemon().factory());
        adapterExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(
                Thread.ofPlatform().name("transport-adapter").daemon().factory());
        scheduler.scheduleWithFixedDelay(guard("acquire", this::acquireTick), 0,
                props.acquireInterval().toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
        scheduler.scheduleWithFixedDelay(guard("renew", this::renewTick), props.renewInterval().toMillis(),
                props.renewInterval().toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
        scheduler.scheduleWithFixedDelay(guard("supervise", this::superviseTick), superviseInterval.toMillis(),
                superviseInterval.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
        log.info("Leader election started as instance {} (ttl={}, renew every {}, acquire every {})",
                lock.instanceId(), props.ttl(), props.renewInterval(), props.acquireInterval());
    }

    /**
     * 停止：先停调度（不再抢锁或续期），再停止适配器与启动线程；本实例是主实例时释放锁（比对 id 后删除），
     * 释放失败只记 WARN，锁在 ttl 后自然过期。
     */
    @Override
    public synchronized void stop() {
        if (!running) {
            return;
        }
        running = false;
        scheduler.shutdownNow();
        awaitQuietly(scheduler);
        boolean wasLeader = role == Role.LEADER;
        term.incrementAndGet();
        role = Role.STANDBY;
        stopAdapters();
        adapterExecutor.shutdownNow();
        awaitQuietly(adapterExecutor);
        if (wasLeader) {
            try {
                boolean released = lock.release();
                log.info("Leader {} on shutdown by instance {}", released ? "lock released" : "lock was no longer held",
                        lock.instanceId());
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                log.warn("Could not release the leader lock on shutdown (it expires after {}): {}", props.ttl(),
                        e.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isRunning() {
        return running;
    }

    /** 备实例尝试抢锁；抢到即成为主实例并异步启动适配器。Redis 不可用时保持备用，只记 WARN。 */
    void acquireTick() {
        if (role == Role.LEADER) {
            return;
        }
        boolean acquired;
        try {
            acquired = lock.tryAcquire();
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            log.warn("Cannot reach Redis to acquire the leader lock, staying standby: {}", e.getMessage());
            return;
        }
        if (!acquired) {
            log.debug("Leader lock is held by another instance, {} stays standby", lock.instanceId());
            return;
        }
        long t = term.incrementAndGet();
        role = Role.LEADER;
        log.info("Instance {} became leader, starting protocol adapters", lock.instanceId());
        submit(() -> startAdapters(t));
    }

    /** 主实例续期；失败则下台（见 renewOrStepDown）。 */
    void renewTick() {
        if (role != Role.LEADER) {
            return;
        }
        if (renewOrStepDown()) {
            log.debug("Leader lock renewed by {}", lock.instanceId());
        }
    }

    /**
     * 主实例检查适配器：有适配器需要重连时，先续期确认仍持锁（失败则下台、不重连，避免与新主互相踢），
     * 再在适配器线程上 stop 后重新 start。同一时刻只有一个重连任务。
     */
    void superviseTick() {
        if (role != Role.LEADER || restarting.get()) {
            return;
        }
        List<ProtocolAdapter> broken = adapters.stream().filter(ProtocolAdapter::needsRestart).toList();
        if (broken.isEmpty() || !renewOrStepDown()) {
            return;
        }
        long t = term.get();
        restarting.set(true);
        boolean submitted = submit(() -> {
            try {
                for (ProtocolAdapter adapter : broken) {
                    log.info("Reconnecting protocol adapter {} as leader {}", adapter.name(), lock.instanceId());
                    adapter.stop();
                    startAdapter(adapter, t);
                }
            } finally {
                restarting.set(false);
            }
        });
        if (!submitted) {
            restarting.set(false);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 续期锁；锁已不属于本实例或 Redis 出错时 WARN 并下台：任期加一、转为备用、立即停止全部适配器。
     *
     * @return 是否仍是主实例
     */
    private boolean renewOrStepDown() {
        String problem;
        try {
            if (lock.renew()) {
                return true;
            }
            problem = "the lock is no longer held by this instance";
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            problem = "Redis error: " + e.getMessage();
        }
        log.warn("Leader lock renewal failed for {} ({}), stopping protocol adapters and becoming standby",
                lock.instanceId(), problem);
        term.incrementAndGet();
        role = Role.STANDBY;
        stopAdapters();
        return false;
    }

    /** 依次启动全部适配器（任期 t 内）。 */
    private void startAdapters(long t) {
        for (ProtocolAdapter adapter : adapters) {
            startAdapter(adapter, t);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 在任期 t 仍有效时反复尝试启动一个适配器，失败按 initialBackoff 起翻倍、最多 maxBackoff 退避。
     * 启动成功后若发现已经下台（任期变化），立即停止它。线程被中断时放弃。
     */
    private void startAdapter(ProtocolAdapter adapter, long t) {
        for (int attempt = 0; running && term.get() == t; attempt++) {
            try {
                adapter.start();
                if (!running || term.get() != t) {
                    adapter.stop();
                    return;
                }
                log.info("Protocol adapter {} connected on leader {}", adapter.name(), lock.instanceId());
                return;
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                Duration delay = backoff(attempt);
                log.warn("Protocol adapter {} failed to start (attempt {}), retrying in {} ms: {}", adapter.name(),
                        attempt + 1, delay.toMillis(), e.getMessage());
                try {
                    Thread.sleep(delay);
                } catch (InterruptedException ie) {
                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /** 停止全部适配器，单个失败只记 WARN。 */
    private void stopAdapters() {
        for (ProtocolAdapter adapter : adapters) {
            try {
                adapter.stop();
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                log.warn("Protocol adapter {} did not stop cleanly: {}", adapter.name(), e.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    private Duration backoff(int attempt) {
        Duration delay = initialBackoff.multipliedBy(1L << Math.min(attempt, 10));
        return delay.compareTo(maxBackoff) > 0 ? maxBackoff : delay;
    }

    /** 提交到适配器线程；协调器正在停止时返回 false。 */
    private boolean submit(Runnable task) {
        try {
            adapterExecutor.execute(task);
            return true;
        } catch (RejectedExecutionException e) {
            log.debug("Adapter task skipped, leader election is stopping");
            return false;
        }
    }

    /** 包装定时任务：异常只记日志，避免 scheduleWithFixedDelay 因异常停止后续执行。 */
    private Runnable guard(String what, Runnable task) {
        return () -> {
            try {
                task.run();
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                log.error("Leader election {} tick failed unexpectedly", what, e);
            }
        };
    }

    private static void awaitQuietly(ExecutorService executor) {
        try {
            executor.awaitTermination(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the health indicator, the wiring and the configuration**

Create `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderHealthIndicator.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.leader;

import org.springframework.boot.actuate.health.Health;
import org.springframework.boot.actuate.health.HealthIndicator;

/**
 * 健康检查组件 leader（/actuator/health 的 components.leader）：details 含 role（LEADER / STANDBY）、instanceId
 * 与 connected（主实例的协议适配器是否已连接）。备实例同样健康，状态恒为 UP。
 */
public class LeaderHealthIndicator implements HealthIndicator {

    private final LeaderElection election;

    public LeaderHealthIndicator(LeaderElection election) {
        this.election = election;
    }

    @Override
    public Health health() {
        return Health.up()
                .withDetail("role", election.role().name())
                .withDetail("instanceId", election.instanceId())
                .withDetail("connected", election.adaptersConnected())
                .build();
    }
}
```

Replace `conduit-transport/src/main/java/dev/conduit/transport/TransportApplication.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport;

import dev.conduit.common.bus.MessageBus;
import dev.conduit.common.bus.MessageBusConfiguration;
import dev.conduit.transport.adapter.ProtocolAdapter;
import dev.conduit.transport.leader.LeaderElection;
import dev.conduit.transport.leader.LeaderHealthIndicator;
import dev.conduit.transport.leader.LeaderLock;
import dev.conduit.transport.leader.LeaderProperties;
import dev.conduit.transport.leader.RedisLeaderLock;
import dev.conduit.transport.mqtt.MqttProtocolAdapter;
import dev.conduit.transport.mqtt.MqttTransportProperties;
import org.springframework.boot.SpringApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.SpringBootApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.EnableConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * conduit-transport 进程入口：接入层。订阅 EMQX 上的设备消息，组装为 RawMessage 写入 Redis Streams 总线，
 * 不做任何业务解析（解码与校验由 conduit-server 流水线完成）。多个实例单活多备：经 Redis 锁选主，只有主实例连接 EMQX。
 */
@SpringBootApplication
@Import(MessageBusConfiguration.class)
@EnableConfigurationProperties({MqttTransportProperties.class, LeaderProperties.class})
public class TransportApplication {

    /** 启动 transport 进程。 */
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        SpringApplication.run(TransportApplication.class, args);
    }

    /** MQTT 协议适配器；何时连接由 {@link LeaderElection} 决定。 */
    @Bean
    MqttProtocolAdapter mqttProtocolAdapter(MqttTransportProperties props, MessageBus bus) {
        return new MqttProtocolAdapter(props, bus, Clock.systemUTC());
    }

    /** Redis 选主锁，实例 id 为 主机名-进程号-随机串。 */
    @Bean
    LeaderLock leaderLock(StringRedisTemplate redis, LeaderProperties props) {
        return new RedisLeaderLock(redis, props.key(), props.ttl(), RedisLeaderLock.newInstanceId());
    }

    /** 选主协调器（SmartLifecycle，容器启动后开始抢锁，关闭时释放锁）。 */
    @Bean
    LeaderElection leaderElection(LeaderLock lock, List<ProtocolAdapter> adapters, LeaderProperties props) {
        return new LeaderElection(lock, adapters, props);
    }

    /** 健康检查组件 leader：显示角色、实例 id 与连接状态。 */
    @Bean
    LeaderHealthIndicator leaderHealthIndicator(LeaderElection election) {
        return new LeaderHealthIndicator(election);
    }
}
```

```yaml
server:
  port: 8081

spring:
  application:
    name: conduit-transport
  data:
    redis:
      host: ${REDIS_HOST:localhost}
      port: ${REDIS_PORT:6379}
      # 选主续期依赖 Redis 及时返回：命令超时短于续期间隔，Redis 卡住时按续期失败处理（立即断开 MQTT）
      timeout: 2s

conduit:
  transport:
    mqtt:
      host: ${EMQX_HOST:localhost}
      port: ${EMQX_PORT:1883}
      # 所有 transport 实例共用同一 clientId：EMQX 磁盘持久会话按 clientId 保存积压，新主实例接管同一会话
      client-id: ${TRANSPORT_CLIENT_ID:conduit-transport}
      # 与 EMQX 持久会话消息保留期（durable_sessions.message_retention_period，默认 1 天）一致
      session-expiry-seconds: 86400
      username: ${TRANSPORT_MQTT_USERNAME:}
      password: ${TRANSPORT_MQTT_PASSWORD:}
    # 单活多备选主（Redis 锁）：只有主实例连接 EMQX，主实例故障后备实例最长 ttl 后接管
    leader:
      key: conduit:transport:leader
      ttl: 15s
      renew-interval: 5s
      acquire-interval: 2s

management:
  endpoint:
    health:
      # components.leader.details 显示 role / instanceId / connected
      show-details: always
  endpoints:
    web:
      exposure:
        include: health,metrics

# 日志写入文件：目录由 CONDUIT_LOG_DIR 指定（默认工作目录下 logs），按天 + 按大小滚动，控制台输出保持不变
logging:
  file:
    name: ${CONDUIT_LOG_DIR:logs}/${spring.application.name}.log
  logback:
    rollingpolicy:
      max-file-size: 50MB
      max-history: 14
      total-size-cap: 1GB
      file-name-pattern: ${CONDUIT_LOG_DIR:logs}/${spring.application.name}.%d{yyyy-MM-dd}.%i.log.gz
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the election and application integration tests**

Create `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/leader/LeaderElectionIT.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport.leader;

import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.MqttClient;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.datatypes.MqttQos;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.Mqtt5BlockingClient;
import dev.conduit.common.bus.Delivery;
import dev.conduit.common.bus.MessageBus;
import dev.conduit.common.bus.MessageBusProperties;
import dev.conduit.common.bus.RedisStreamMessageBus;
import dev.conduit.common.message.MessageType;
import dev.conduit.common.message.RawMessage;
import dev.conduit.transport.leader.LeaderElection.Role;
import dev.conduit.transport.mqtt.MqttProtocolAdapter;
import dev.conduit.transport.mqtt.MqttTransportProperties;
import dev.conduit.transport.support.TestBroker;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

/**
 * 选主集成测试（真实 Redis + 磁盘持久会话 EMQX，ttl 3s / 续期 1s / 抢锁 0.5s）：两个实例只有一个成为主且只有它连接；
 * 主实例停止后备实例在 ttl 内接管并补齐切换期间发布的消息；续期失败（锁被改写）立即断开并保持备用，锁恢复空闲后重新成为主。
 */
class LeaderElectionIT {

    static final Duration TTL = Duration.ofSeconds(3);
    static final Duration RENEW = Duration.ofSeconds(1);
    static final Duration ACQUIRE = Duration.ofMillis(500);

    final StringRedisTemplate redis = TestBroker.redis();
    final String key = "it:leader:" + UUID.randomUUID();
    final String streamKey = "it:up:" + UUID.randomUUID();
    final String clientId = "conduit-transport-it-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    final String productKey = "pk" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8);
    final List<LeaderElection> elections = new ArrayList<>();
    final Map<LeaderElection, MqttProtocolAdapter> adapters = new HashMap<>();
    final List<Mqtt5BlockingClient> devices = new ArrayList<>();
    final Set<String> received = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
    MessageBus bus;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        bus = new RedisStreamMessageBus(redis, new MessageBusProperties(streamKey, "it:dead", 100_000, 1000));
        bus.ensureGroup("it");
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        elections.forEach(LeaderElection::stop);
        devices.forEach(d -> {
            if (d.getState().isConnected()) {
                d.disconnect();
            }
        });
        redis.delete(List.of(key, streamKey));
    }

    private LeaderElection instance(String name) {
        var props = new MqttTransportProperties(TestBroker.emqxHost(), TestBroker.emqxPort(), clientId,
                null, null, 86400, "conduit-");
        MqttProtocolAdapter adapter = new MqttProtocolAdapter(props, bus, Clock.systemUTC());
        LeaderElection election = new LeaderElection(new RedisLeaderLock(redis, key, TTL, name), List.of(adapter),
                new LeaderProperties(key, TTL, RENEW, ACQUIRE));
        elections.add(election);
        adapters.put(election, adapter);
        return election;
    }

    private LeaderElection awaitConnectedLeader() {
        AtomicReference<LeaderElection> leader = new AtomicReference<>();
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(20)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            List<LeaderElection> leaders = elections.stream().filter(e -> e.role() == Role.LEADER).toList();
            assertThat(leaders).hasSize(1);
            assertThat(leaders.getFirst().adaptersConnected()).isTrue();
            leader.set(leaders.getFirst());
        });
        return leader.get();
    }

    private Mqtt5BlockingClient device(String deviceKey) {
        Mqtt5BlockingClient client = MqttClient.builder().useMqttVersion5().identifier(deviceKey)
                .serverHost(TestBroker.emqxHost()).serverPort(TestBroker.emqxPort()).buildBlocking();
        client.connect();
        devices.add(client);
        return client;
    }

    private void drain() {
        List<Delivery> batch;
        while (!(batch = bus.read("it", "test", 500, Duration.ofMillis(50))).isEmpty()) {
            for (Delivery d : batch) {
                RawMessage m = d.message();
                if (m.type() == MessageType.PROPERTY && productKey.equals(m.productKey())) {
                    received.add(new String(m.payload(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    void onlyOneOfTwoInstancesBecomesLeaderAndOnlyItConnects() throws Exception {
        LeaderElection a = instance("instance-a");
        LeaderElection b = instance("instance-b");

        a.start();
        b.start();

        LeaderElection leader = awaitConnectedLeader();
        LeaderElection standby = leader == a ? b : a;
        for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
            assertThat(elections.stream().filter(e -> e.role() == Role.LEADER).count()).isEqualTo(1);
            Thread.sleep(100);
        }
        assertThat(redis.opsForValue().get(key)).isEqualTo(leader.instanceId());
        assertThat(adapters.get(standby).isConnected()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void theStandbyTakesOverWithinTheTtlAndReceivesTheBacklog() throws Exception {
        LeaderElection a = instance("instance-a");
        LeaderElection b = instance("instance-b");
        a.start();
        b.start();
        LeaderElection leader = awaitConnectedLeader();
        LeaderElection standby = leader == a ? b : a;
        Mqtt5BlockingClient device = device("dk-failover");
        AtomicInteger published = new AtomicInteger();
        CompletableFuture<Void> publishing = CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> {
            for (int i = 1; i <= 300; i++) {
                device.publishWith().topic("conduit/" + productKey + "/dk-failover/up/property")
                        .qos(MqttQos.AT_LEAST_ONCE).payload(("{\"seq\":" + i + "}").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
                        .send();
                published.incrementAndGet();
                try {
                    Thread.sleep(10);
                } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                    return;
                }
            }
        });
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(20)).until(() -> published.get() >= 100);

        leader.stop();

        await().atMost(TTL).until(() -> standby.role() == Role.LEADER);
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(15)).until(standby::adaptersConnected);
        publishing.get(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(30)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            drain();
            assertThat(received).hasSize(300);
        });
    }

    @Test
    void aFailedRenewalDisconnectsAtOnceAndTheInstanceWaitsAsStandby() throws Exception {
        LeaderElection a = instance("instance-a");
        a.start();
        awaitConnectedLeader();

        redis.opsForValue().set(key, "another-instance", Duration.ofMinutes(1));

        await().atMost(RENEW.plusSeconds(3)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            assertThat(a.role()).isEqualTo(Role.STANDBY);
            assertThat(adapters.get(a).isConnected()).isFalse();
        });
        Thread.sleep(ACQUIRE.toMillis() * 3);
        assertThat(a.role()).as("the lock belongs to another instance").isEqualTo(Role.STANDBY);

        redis.delete(key);
        awaitConnectedLeader();
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-transport/src/test/java/dev/conduit/transport/TransportApplicationIT.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.transport;

import dev.conduit.transport.support.TestBroker;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.SpringBootTest;
import org.springframework.boot.test.web.client.TestRestTemplate;
import org.springframework.test.context.DynamicPropertyRegistry;
import org.springframework.test.context.DynamicPropertySource;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.UUID;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

/** transport 装配冒烟测试：完整 Spring 上下文连接 Redis 与 EMQX，单实例成为主实例，健康检查显示 role 与 connected。 */
@SpringBootTest(webEnvironment = SpringBootTest.WebEnvironment.RANDOM_PORT, properties = {
        "logging.file.name=target/test-logs/conduit-transport.log",
        "logging.logback.rollingpolicy.file-name-pattern=target/test-logs/conduit-transport.%d{yyyy-MM-dd}.%i.log.gz"})
class TransportApplicationIT {

    static final String CLIENT_ID = "conduit-transport-app-it-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    static final String LEADER_KEY = "it:leader:" + UUID.randomUUID();
    static final String STREAM_KEY = "it:app:up:" + UUID.randomUUID();

    @DynamicPropertySource
    static void properties(DynamicPropertyRegistry registry) {
        registry.add("spring.data.redis.host", TestBroker::redisHost);
        registry.add("spring.data.redis.port", TestBroker::redisPort);
        registry.add("conduit.transport.mqtt.host", TestBroker::emqxHost);
        registry.add("conduit.transport.mqtt.port", TestBroker::emqxPort);
        registry.add("conduit.transport.mqtt.client-id", () -> CLIENT_ID);
        registry.add("conduit.transport.leader.key", () -> LEADER_KEY);
        registry.add("conduit.bus.stream-key", () -> STREAM_KEY);
    }

    @Autowired
    TestRestTemplate rest;

    @Test
    void healthShowsTheLeaderRoleOnceConnected() {
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(30)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            String body = rest.getForObject("/actuator/health", String.class);
            assertThat(body).contains("\"role\":\"LEADER\"").contains("\"connected\":true")
                    .contains("\"instanceId\":");
        });
    }
}
```

Then replace the whole `conduit-transport` service, from `  conduit-transport:` through its `    restart: unless-stopped`, with:

```yaml
  # transport 单活多备（V0.2-R）：两个实例经 Redis 锁 conduit:transport:leader 选主，只有主实例连接 EMQX；
  # 两者共用 clientId conduit-transport（默认值），主实例故障后备实例最长 15 秒后接管同一个磁盘持久会话
  conduit-transport: &transport
    profiles: ["app"]
    image: conduit-conduit-transport:latest
    build:
      context: ..
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
      args:
        MODULE: conduit-transport
    environment: &transport-env
      REDIS_HOST: redis
      EMQX_HOST: emqx
      TRANSPORT_MQTT_USERNAME: ${TRANSPORT_MQTT_USERNAME:-conduit-transport}
      TRANSPORT_MQTT_PASSWORD: ${TRANSPORT_MQTT_PASSWORD}
      CONDUIT_LOG_DIR: /var/log/conduit
    volumes:
      - conduit-logs:/var/log/conduit
    depends_on:
      redis:
        condition: service_healthy
      emqx:
        condition: service_healthy
      conduit-server:
        condition: service_healthy
    restart: unless-stopped

  # 第二个 transport（与上面相同的镜像与配置）：日志写到共享卷的子目录，避免两个进程滚动同一个日志文件
  conduit-transport-2:
    <<: *transport
    environment:
      <<: *transport-env
      CONDUIT_LOG_DIR: /var/log/conduit/transport-2
```

Adds the four new environment values, the `SIM_TENANTS` filter, and the codec-to-format lookup that discovery needs.

- Produces:

**Decisions recorded here:**
- `SIM_TENANTS` is trimmed and split on commas. `*`, an empty value, or a list that contains `*` means all tenants. Codes are matched exactly.
- `SIM_MAX_DEVICES_PER_PRODUCT` must be at least 1, otherwise `IllegalStateException`. A typo should stop the simulator instead of silently simulating nothing.
- The platform password stays optional (empty = demo product only). Any other platform setting is ignored then.

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.NullSource;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.ValueSource;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/** TenantFilter 单元测试：* 与空值表示全部租户，逗号分隔的编码精确匹配并忽略空白。 */
class TenantFilterTest {

    @ParameterizedTest
    @NullSource
    @ValueSource(strings = {"*", "", "  ", " * ", ",", "east,*"})
    void starEmptyOrAListWithStarMeansAllTenants(String value) {
        TenantFilter filter = TenantFilter.parse(value);

        assertThat(filter.all()).isTrue();
        assertThat(filter.includes("anything")).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void aListMatchesExactCodesIgnoringWhitespace() {
        TenantFilter filter = TenantFilter.parse(" east-power , south-water,,");

        assertThat(filter.all()).isFalse();
        assertThat(filter.codes()).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("east-power", "south-water");
        assertThat(filter.includes("east-power")).isTrue();
        assertThat(filter.includes("south-water")).isTrue();
        assertThat(filter.includes("east")).isFalse();
        assertThat(filter.includes("default")).isFalse();
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

class SimulatorConfigTest {

        Map<String, String> env = Map.of("MQTT_HOST", "env-host");

    @Test

        assertThat(config.apiUrl()).hasToString("http://localhost:8080");
        assertThat(config.productName()).isEqualTo("三相电表（演示）");

        assertThat(config.platformUsername()).isEqualTo("sysadmin");
        assertThat(config.platformPassword()).isEmpty();
        assertThat(config.multiTenant()).isFalse();
        assertThat(config.tenants().all()).isTrue();
        assertThat(config.maxDevicesPerProduct()).isEqualTo(100);

                .hasMessageContaining("CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD");

        assertThat(SimulatorConfig.fromEnv(Map.of("CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD", "pw")).payloadFormat())
                .isEqualTo(PayloadFormat.STANDARD);
        assertThat(SimulatorConfig.fromEnv(Map.of("CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD", "pw", "SIM_PAYLOAD_FORMAT", "FLAT"))
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/TenantFilter.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * 模拟哪些租户（SIM_TENANTS）：codes 为空表示全部启用中的租户，否则只模拟编码精确匹配的租户。
 *
 * @param codes 租户编码集合；空集合表示不限
 */
public record TenantFilter(Set<String> codes) {

    /** 不限租户。 */
    public static final TenantFilter ALL = new TenantFilter(Set.of());

    public TenantFilter {
        codes = Set.copyOf(codes);
    }

    /**
     * 解析 SIM_TENANTS：去掉首尾空白后按逗号分隔；null、空白、* 或列表中含 * 都表示全部租户，空项忽略。
     */
    public static TenantFilter parse(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return ALL;
        }
        Set<String> codes = new LinkedHashSet<>();
        for (String part : value.split(",")) {
            String code = part.trim();
            if (code.equals("*")) {
                return ALL;
            }
            if (!code.isEmpty()) {
                codes.add(code);
            }
        }
        return codes.isEmpty() ? ALL : new TenantFilter(codes);
    }

    /** 是否不限租户。 */
    public boolean all() {
        return codes.isEmpty();
    }

    /** 租户是否在模拟范围内。 */
    public boolean includes(String code) {
        return codes.isEmpty() || codes.contains(code);
    }
}
```

Replace `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/SimulatorConfig.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.UncheckedIOException;
import java.net.URI;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Properties;

/**
 * 模拟器配置，来自环境变量（或 simulator-local.properties）。
 *
 * @param apiUrl               conduit-server REST 地址
 * @param adminUsername        用于登录 REST 的管理员账号（默认租户，负责演示产品）
 * @param adminPassword        管理员密码（必填）
 * @param mqttHost             EMQX 地址
 * @param mqttPort             EMQX MQTT 端口
 * @param productName          演示产品名称
 * @param deviceCount          演示设备数量
 * @param interval             每台设备的上报周期
 * @param messagesPerDevice    每台设备发送条数；0 表示无限发送
 * @param payloadFormat        演示产品的上报格式，同时决定新建演示产品的解码器
 * @param platformUsername     平台管理员账号（SIM_PLATFORM_USERNAME），用于发现各租户的产品与设备
 * @param platformPassword     平台管理员密码（SIM_PLATFORM_PASSWORD）；为空时只模拟演示产品
 * @param tenants              模拟哪些租户（SIM_TENANTS）
 * @param maxDevicesPerProduct 每个租户产品最多模拟的设备数（SIM_MAX_DEVICES_PER_PRODUCT）
 */
public record SimulatorConfig(URI apiUrl, String adminUsername, String adminPassword,
                              String mqttHost, int mqttPort, String productName,
                              int deviceCount, Duration interval, int messagesPerDevice,
                              PayloadFormat payloadFormat,
                              String platformUsername, String platformPassword,
                              TenantFilter tenants, int maxDevicesPerProduct) {

    /** 本地联调用的配置文件（classpath 根目录，不入库），键名与环境变量相同。 */
    static final String LOCAL_PROPERTIES = "simulator-local.properties";

    /** 环境变量优先，其次是 classpath 上可选的 simulator-local.properties。 */
    public static SimulatorConfig load() {
        return fromEnv(merge(readLocalProperties(), System.getenv()));
    }

    /** 合并配置来源，同名键以 env 为准。 */
    static Map<String, String> merge(Map<String, String> local, Map<String, String> env) {
        Map<String, String> merged = new HashMap<>(local);
        merged.putAll(env);
        return merged;
    }

    /**
     * 从键值表构造配置，未提供的项取默认值。
     *
     * @throws IllegalStateException CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD 缺失或为空、SIM_PAYLOAD_FORMAT 非法、
     *                               SIM_MAX_DEVICES_PER_PRODUCT 小于 1
     * @throws NumberFormatException 数值类配置项格式错误
     */
    public static SimulatorConfig fromEnv(Map<String, String> env) {
        return new SimulatorConfig(
                URI.create(env.getOrDefault("CONDUIT_API_URL", "http://localhost:8080")),
                env.getOrDefault("CONDUIT_ADMIN_USERNAME", "admin"),
                require(env, "CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD"),
                env.getOrDefault("MQTT_HOST", "localhost"),
                Integer.parseInt(env.getOrDefault("MQTT_PORT", "1883")),
                env.getOrDefault("SIM_PRODUCT_NAME", "三相电表（演示）"),
                Integer.parseInt(env.getOrDefault("SIM_DEVICE_COUNT", "100")),
                Duration.ofMillis(Long.parseLong(env.getOrDefault("SIM_INTERVAL_MS", "10000"))),
                Integer.parseInt(env.getOrDefault("SIM_MESSAGES_PER_DEVICE", "0")),
                PayloadFormat.parse(env.getOrDefault("SIM_PAYLOAD_FORMAT", "standard")),
                env.getOrDefault("SIM_PLATFORM_USERNAME", "sysadmin"),
                env.getOrDefault("SIM_PLATFORM_PASSWORD", ""),
                TenantFilter.parse(env.getOrDefault("SIM_TENANTS", "*")),
                atLeastOne(env, "SIM_MAX_DEVICES_PER_PRODUCT", 100));
    }

    /** 是否模拟各租户的产品：平台管理员密码非空时才开启。 */
    public boolean multiTenant() {
        return platformPassword != null && !platformPassword.isBlank();
    }

    /** 读取 classpath 上的本地配置文件；文件不存在返回空表。 */
    private static Map<String, String> readLocalProperties() {
        try (InputStream in = SimulatorConfig.class.getClassLoader().getResourceAsStream(LOCAL_PROPERTIES)) {
            if (in == null) {
                return Map.of();
            }
            Properties props = new Properties();
            props.load(new InputStreamReader(in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            Map<String, String> values = new HashMap<>();
            props.stringPropertyNames().forEach(k -> values.put(k, props.getProperty(k).trim()));
            return values;
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException("Cannot read " + LOCAL_PROPERTIES, e);
        }
    }

    private static String require(Map<String, String> env, String name) {
        String value = env.get(name);
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Environment variable " + name + " is required");
        }
        return value;
    }

    /** 读取不小于 1 的整数配置，缺省取 defaultValue。 */
    private static int atLeastOne(Map<String, String> env, String name, int defaultValue) {
        int value = Integer.parseInt(env.getOrDefault(name, Integer.toString(defaultValue)).trim());
        if (value < 1) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(name + " must be at least 1, got: " + value);
        }
        return value;
    }
}
```

In `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/PayloadFormat.java`:

1. Add `import java.util.Optional;` below `import java.util.Map;`.
2. Add this method at the end of the enum body, after `parse`:

```java
    /**
     * 按平台解码器名称找对应的上报格式。
     *
     * @return standard-json → STANDARD，flat-json → FLAT；其他解码器（模拟器不会生成的格式）或 null 为 empty
     */
    public static Optional<PayloadFormat> fromCodec(String codec) {
        for (PayloadFormat format : values()) {
            if (format.codec.equals(codec)) {
                return Optional.of(format);
            }
        }
        return Optional.empty();
    }
```

## Task 5: Thing-model value generation (track B)

Implements the spec §5.2 table as pure, deterministic code. Properties are classified by type, identifier, name and unit. Values come from day curves, integration for cumulative totals, drift and random walks. Every value is shaped to the property's min, max, decimals and type, so the pipeline never rejects it (`FIELD_REJECTED`).

**Files:**
- Create: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/PropertyDef.java`
- Create: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/PointKind.java`
- Create: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/PointClassifier.java`
- Create: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/ValueRanges.java`
- Create: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/DayCurves.java`
- Create: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/ValueShaper.java`
- Create: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/PointGenerator.java`
- Create: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/ReadingSource.java`
- Create: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/ThingModelReadingSource.java`
- Test: `conduit-simulator/src/test/java/dev/conduit/simulator/PointClassifierTest.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-simulator/src/test/java/dev/conduit/simulator/DayCurvesTest.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-simulator/src/test/java/dev/conduit/simulator/ValueShaperTest.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-simulator/src/test/java/dev/conduit/simulator/ThingModelReadingSourceTest.java` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `OperatingState.mix(long)` (package-private, same package).
- Produces:
  - `record PropertyDef(String identifier, String name, String dataType, String unit, Double minValue, Double maxValue, Integer decimals, List<PropertyDef.EnumOption> enumItems)`, where `enumItems` is never null, and `record PropertyDef.EnumOption(long value, String label)`.
  - `enum PointKind { VOLTAGE, LOAD, PV_POWER, IRRADIANCE, CUMULATIVE, DAILY_ENERGY, TEMPERATURE, FLOW, DRIFT, ENUM, BOOL, OTHER, SKIP }`.
  - `PointClassifier.classify(PropertyDef) → PointKind`, `PointClassifier.simulated(PropertyDef) → boolean` (both public).
  - `@FunctionalInterface interface ReadingSource { Map<String, Object> next(long epochMillis, double intervalSeconds); }`.
  - `ThingModelReadingSource(List<PropertyDef> properties, String deviceKey, ZoneId zone) implements ReadingSource`, plus `int pointCount()`.

**Decisions recorded here (how the spec §5.2 table is read):**
- Classification order, first match wins. The unit is normalized: lower case, `²`→`2`, `³`→`3`, `°c`→`℃`, `μ`/`µ`→`u`, spaces removed. The match text is `identifier + " " + name`, lower-cased.
  1. TEXT → SKIP. ENUM without items → SKIP. ENUM → ENUM. BOOL → BOOL. Other types → SKIP.
  2. Unit `kwh`/`mwh`/`wh`/`m3` → CUMULATIVE, or DAILY_ENERGY when the text contains `eday`, `daily`, `today` or `日`. A property with no unit whose text contains `累计`/`total`/`energy`, or whose identifier starts with `EP`, gets the same treatment. The keyword rule needs a missing unit, so `TEMP` (℃) never counts as cumulative.
  3. Unit `w/m2` or text `辐照`/`irradiance` → IRRADIANCE.
  4. Unit `w`/`kw`/`mw` and text `光伏`/`发电`/`pv`/`solar`/`eday`/`etotal` → PV_POWER.
  5. Unit `v`, or no unit and text `电压` → VOLTAGE.
  6. Unit `a`/`w`/`kw`/`mw` → LOAD (current or non-PV power).
  7. Unit `℃` or text `温度`/`temperature` → TEMPERATURE.
  8. Unit `m3/h` or text `流量`/`flow` → FLOW.
  9. Water pressure, pH, turbidity, residual chlorine, humidity, PM2.5, CO₂ or wind speed (by keyword or unit) → DRIFT within a fixed plausible range.
  10. Otherwise → OTHER.
- Value rules:
  - VOLTAGE: 220 V plus a fixed per-phase offset (identifier ending `a` −0.8, `b` 0, `c` +0.8) plus N(0, 0.6).
  - LOAD: nominal × `loadFactor(hour)` × (1 + N(0, 0.03)). The nominal is 0.75 × max if a max is set, otherwise 50 A / 30 kW / 30000 W / 0.03 MW. `loadFactor` lies in 0.25–1 with peaks at about 10:30 and 20:00.
  - PV_POWER / IRRADIANCE: capacity × `sun(hour)` × cloud. `sun` is the sine from 6 to 18 h and exactly 0 otherwise. The cloud factor follows a random walk in 0.55–1. The capacity is 0.9 × max, or 50 kW / 50000 W / 0.05 MW; irradiance capacity is min(max, 1000) or 1000 W/m².
  - CUMULATIVE / DAILY_ENERGY: integrate a rate over the interval. Volume (m³, or a water keyword on a non-energy unit) integrates the device's FLOW points, or 30 × `flowFactor`. Generation keywords integrate the device's PV points, or 50 × `sun` × 0.85. Anything else integrates the device's LOAD points in power units, or 30 × `loadFactor`. Watts are converted to kW and the result is scaled to kWh, MWh or Wh. Totals never decrease. A DAILY_ENERGY total restarts at 0 when the local date changes. Initial values: lifetime totals start at 1000–10000 kWh or 500–5000 m³, capped at max/2. Daily totals start at the integral of the curve from midnight up to the current time.
  - TEMPERATURE: ambient 18 + 7·sin(2π(h−9)/24), so about 11 ℃ at 03:00 and 25 ℃ at 15:00, plus N(0, 0.3). Device temperatures (text `绕组`/`机内`/`内部`/`设备`/`模块`/`油温`/`igbt`/`winding`/`internal`) add 10 + 30 × `loadFactor`.
  - FLOW: nominal (0.7 × max, or 30 m³/h) × `flowFactor` (0.15–1, peaks at 08:00 and 19:30) × (1 + N(0, 0.05)).
  - DRIFT / OTHER: a mean-reverting random walk inside the range. The DRIFT range comes from the table in `ValueRanges.drift`. The OTHER range is [min, max] when both are set. Otherwise it is inferred from the unit (Hz 49.8–50.2, % 0–100, kvar 0–50, kVA 0–100, V 200–240, A 0–50, kW 0–100, ℃ 0–40, MPa 0–1, m/s 0–10), else 0–100.
  - ENUM: a normal value (the first item whose label contains `运行`/`发电`/`开启`/`正常`, else the first item) in 85 % of the (device, property, hour) slots, otherwise another item. The value is constant within a clock hour.
  - BOOL: `true` in 5 % of the (device, property, hour) slots, otherwise `false`.
- Shaping: DOUBLE is clamped to [min, max] and rounded to `decimals` (3 when null, at most 6), and stays inside the bounds after rounding. INT is a `Long`, rounded and clamped to the integer bounds. ENUM is the item's `Long` value and BOOL a `Boolean`.
- Randomness is seeded from `deviceKey` and `identifier`, so the same device produces the same sequence after a restart (state starts afresh, as the spec allows). The time of day uses the zone passed in. Task 6 passes `ZoneId.systemDefault()`, and Task 8 sets `TZ: Asia/Shanghai` for the simulator container.

Create `conduit-simulator/src/test/java/dev/conduit/simulator/PointClassifierTest.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.List;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/** PointClassifier 单元测试：按类型、标识、名称与单位把常见测点归入 spec 5.2 的规则类别。 */
class PointClassifierTest {

    private static PropertyDef def(String id, String name, String type, String unit) {
        return new PropertyDef(id, name, type, unit, null, null, null, List.of());
    }

    private static PointKind kind(String id, String name, String type, String unit) {
        return PointClassifier.classify(def(id, name, type, unit));
    }

    @Test
    void classifiesElectricalPoints() {
        assertThat(kind("Ua", "A 相电压", "DOUBLE", "V")).isEqualTo(PointKind.VOLTAGE);
        assertThat(kind("Uab", "线电压", "DOUBLE", null)).isEqualTo(PointKind.VOLTAGE);
        assertThat(kind("Ia", "A 相电流", "DOUBLE", "A")).isEqualTo(PointKind.LOAD);
        assertThat(kind("P", "有功功率", "DOUBLE", "kW")).isEqualTo(PointKind.LOAD);
        assertThat(kind("Pac", "交流功率", "DOUBLE", "W")).isEqualTo(PointKind.LOAD);
        assertThat(kind("Ppv", "光伏功率", "DOUBLE", "kW")).isEqualTo(PointKind.PV_POWER);
        assertThat(kind("pdc", "直流发电功率", "DOUBLE", "W")).isEqualTo(PointKind.PV_POWER);
        assertThat(kind("irr", "辐照度", "DOUBLE", "W/m²")).isEqualTo(PointKind.IRRADIANCE);
    }

    @Test
    void classifiesCumulativeTotals() {
        assertThat(kind("EPimp", "正向有功电能", "DOUBLE", "kWh")).isEqualTo(PointKind.CUMULATIVE);
        assertThat(kind("EPexp", "反向有功电能", "DOUBLE", null)).isEqualTo(PointKind.CUMULATIVE);
        assertThat(kind("total", "累计用水量", "DOUBLE", "m³")).isEqualTo(PointKind.CUMULATIVE);
        assertThat(kind("etotal", "累计发电量", "DOUBLE", "MWh")).isEqualTo(PointKind.CUMULATIVE);
        assertThat(kind("eday", "日发电量", "DOUBLE", "kWh")).isEqualTo(PointKind.DAILY_ENERGY);
        assertThat(kind("TEMP", "温度", "DOUBLE", "℃")).as("EP inside TEMP is not a total")
                .isEqualTo(PointKind.TEMPERATURE);
    }

    @Test
    void classifiesEnvironmentAndWaterPoints() {
        assertThat(kind("tw", "绕组温度", "DOUBLE", "°C")).isEqualTo(PointKind.TEMPERATURE);
        assertThat(kind("flow", "瞬时流量", "DOUBLE", "m³/h")).isEqualTo(PointKind.FLOW);
        assertThat(kind("press", "出水压力", "DOUBLE", "MPa")).isEqualTo(PointKind.DRIFT);
        assertThat(kind("ph", "pH", "DOUBLE", null)).isEqualTo(PointKind.DRIFT);
        assertThat(kind("turb", "浊度", "DOUBLE", "NTU")).isEqualTo(PointKind.DRIFT);
        assertThat(kind("cl", "余氯", "DOUBLE", "mg/L")).isEqualTo(PointKind.DRIFT);
        assertThat(kind("hum", "湿度", "DOUBLE", "%RH")).isEqualTo(PointKind.DRIFT);
        assertThat(kind("pm25", "PM2.5", "DOUBLE", "μg/m³")).isEqualTo(PointKind.DRIFT);
        assertThat(kind("co2", "CO₂ 浓度", "INT", "ppm")).isEqualTo(PointKind.DRIFT);
        assertThat(kind("ws", "风速", "DOUBLE", "m/s")).isEqualTo(PointKind.DRIFT);
        assertThat(kind("freq", "频率", "DOUBLE", "Hz")).isEqualTo(PointKind.OTHER);
    }

    @Test
    void classifiesStatesAndSkipsText() {
        PropertyDef state = new PropertyDef("st", "运行状态", "ENUM", null, null, null, null,
                List.of(new PropertyDef.EnumOption(0, "停机"), new PropertyDef.EnumOption(1, "运行")));
        assertThat(PointClassifier.classify(state)).isEqualTo(PointKind.ENUM);
        assertThat(kind("st", "空枚举", "ENUM", null)).isEqualTo(PointKind.SKIP);
        assertThat(kind("alarm", "告警", "BOOL", null)).isEqualTo(PointKind.BOOL);
        assertThat(kind("note", "备注", "TEXT", null)).isEqualTo(PointKind.SKIP);
        assertThat(PointClassifier.simulated(def("note", "备注", "TEXT", null))).isFalse();
        assertThat(PointClassifier.simulated(def("Ua", "A 相电压", "DOUBLE", "V"))).isTrue();
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/test/java/dev/conduit/simulator/DayCurvesTest.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.within;

/** DayCurves 单元测试：日照只在 6～18 点为正、负荷与用水有早晚高峰、环境温度昼夜变化、日照积分。 */
class DayCurvesTest {

    @Test
    void sunshineIsZeroOutsideSixToEighteenAndPeaksAtNoon() {
        for (double hour : new double[]{0, 3, 5.99, 6, 18, 20.5, 23.9}) {
            assertThat(DayCurves.sun(hour)).as("hour %s", hour).isZero();
        }
        assertThat(DayCurves.sun(12)).isCloseTo(1.0, within(1e-9));
        assertThat(DayCurves.sun(9)).isBetween(0.6, 0.8);
    }

    @Test
    void loadIsLowAtNightAndHighInTheEvening() {
        for (double hour = 0; hour < 24; hour += 0.25) {
            assertThat(DayCurves.loadFactor(hour)).isBetween(0.25, 1.0);
        }
        assertThat(DayCurves.loadFactor(20)).isGreaterThan(0.8);
        assertThat(DayCurves.loadFactor(10.5)).isGreaterThan(0.7);
        assertThat(DayCurves.loadFactor(3.5)).isLessThan(0.3);
    }

    @Test
    void waterFlowPeaksInTheMorningAndEvening() {
        for (double hour = 0; hour < 24; hour += 0.25) {
            assertThat(DayCurves.flowFactor(hour)).isBetween(0.15, 1.0);
        }
        assertThat(DayCurves.flowFactor(8)).isGreaterThan(DayCurves.flowFactor(14));
        assertThat(DayCurves.flowFactor(19.5)).isGreaterThan(DayCurves.flowFactor(14));
        assertThat(DayCurves.flowFactor(3)).isLessThan(DayCurves.flowFactor(14));
    }

    @Test
    void ambientTemperatureIsCoolestBeforeDawnAndWarmestInTheAfternoon() {
        assertThat(DayCurves.ambientTemperature(3)).isCloseTo(11.0, within(1e-9));
        assertThat(DayCurves.ambientTemperature(15)).isCloseTo(25.0, within(1e-9));
    }

    @Test
    void sunHoursIntegrateTheSunshineCurve() {
        assertThat(DayCurves.sunHoursUntil(5)).isZero();
        assertThat(DayCurves.sunHoursUntil(12)).isCloseTo(12 / Math.PI, within(1e-9));
        assertThat(DayCurves.sunHoursUntil(23)).isCloseTo(24 / Math.PI, within(1e-9));
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/test/java/dev/conduit/simulator/ValueShaperTest.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.List;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/** ValueShaper 单元测试：按小数位四舍五入、夹在上下限内（舍入后不越界）、INT 为 Long。 */
class ValueShaperTest {

    private static PropertyDef def(String type, Double min, Double max, Integer decimals) {
        return new PropertyDef("x", "x", type, null, min, max, decimals, List.of());
    }

    @Test
    void roundsToTheDeclaredDecimals() {
        assertThat(ValueShaper.shape(def("DOUBLE", null, null, 1), 220.149)).isEqualTo(220.1);
        assertThat(ValueShaper.shape(def("DOUBLE", null, null, 0), 845.6)).isEqualTo(846.0);
        assertThat(ValueShaper.shape(def("DOUBLE", null, null, null), 1.23456)).isEqualTo(1.235);
    }

    @Test
    void clampsToTheBoundsAndStaysInsideAfterRounding() {
        assertThat(ValueShaper.shape(def("DOUBLE", 0.0, 100.0, 2), 130.0)).isEqualTo(100.0);
        assertThat(ValueShaper.shape(def("DOUBLE", 0.0, 100.0, 2), -3.0)).isEqualTo(0.0);
        assertThat(ValueShaper.shape(def("DOUBLE", 0.0, 10.05, 1), 10.06)).isEqualTo(10.0);
        assertThat(ValueShaper.shape(def("DOUBLE", 0.05, 1.0, 1), 0.0)).isEqualTo(0.1);
    }

    @Test
    void intValuesAreLongsInsideTheIntegerBounds() {
        assertThat(ValueShaper.shape(def("INT", 10.0, 20.0, null), 14.6)).isEqualTo(15L);
        assertThat(ValueShaper.shape(def("INT", 10.5, 20.0, null), 3.0)).isEqualTo(11L);
        assertThat(ValueShaper.shape(def("INT", 10.0, 19.5, null), 25.0)).isEqualTo(19L);
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/test/java/dev/conduit/simulator/ThingModelReadingSourceTest.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.ZonedDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.within;

/**
 * ThingModelReadingSource 单元测试：光伏夜间为 0、累计量单调递增且与功率相符、日发电量零点归零、电压三相略有差别、
 * 设备温度高于环境温度、枚举多数时间为正常值且一小时内不变、布尔低概率为是、TEXT 跳过、所有取值满足物模型约束。
 */
class ThingModelReadingSourceTest {

    static final ZoneId ZONE = ZoneId.of("Asia/Shanghai");

    private static PropertyDef def(String id, String name, String type, String unit, Double min, Double max,
                                   Integer decimals) {
        return new PropertyDef(id, name, type, unit, min, max, decimals, List.of());
    }

    private static PropertyDef.EnumOption opt(long value, String label) {
        return new PropertyDef.EnumOption(value, label);
    }

    private static long at(int day, int hour, int minute) {
        return ZonedDateTime.of(2026, 10, day, hour, minute, 0, 0, ZONE).toInstant().toEpochMilli();
    }

    @Test
    void pvPowerIsZeroAtNightAndPositiveAtNoon() {
        var source = new ThingModelReadingSource(
                List.of(def("Ppv", "光伏功率", "DOUBLE", "kW", 0.0, 100.0, 2)), "pv-1", ZONE);

        for (long t : new long[]{at(16, 0, 30), at(16, 5, 30), at(16, 18, 0), at(16, 22, 15)}) {
            assertThat(source.next(t, 60).get("Ppv")).isEqualTo(0.0);
        }
        assertThat((Double) source.next(at(16, 12, 0), 60).get("Ppv")).isBetween(40.0, 100.0);
    }

    @Test
    void energyIsMonotonicAndFollowsThePower() {
        var source = new ThingModelReadingSource(List.of(
                def("P", "有功功率", "DOUBLE", "kW", 0.0, 60.0, 2),
                def("EPimp", "正向有功电能", "DOUBLE", "kWh", null, null, 3)), "meter-1", ZONE);

        double first = 0;
        double last = 0;
        double expected = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 600; i++) {
            Map<String, Object> reading = source.next(at(16, 8, 0) + i * 60_000L, 60);
            double energy = (Double) reading.get("EPimp");
            if (i == 0) {
                first = energy;
            } else {
                assertThat(energy).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(last);
                expected += (Double) reading.get("P") / 60;
            }
            last = energy;
        }
        assertThat(expected).isGreaterThan(100);
        assertThat(last - first).isCloseTo(expected, within(expected * 0.01 + 0.01));
    }

    @Test
    void waterTotalIsMonotonic() {
        var source = new ThingModelReadingSource(List.of(
                def("flow", "瞬时流量", "DOUBLE", "m³/h", 0.0, 200.0, 2),
                def("total", "累计用水量", "DOUBLE", "m³", null, null, 2)), "water-1", ZONE);

        double last = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < 2000; i++) {
            double total = (Double) source.next(at(16, 0, 0) + i * 300_000L, 300).get("total");
            assertThat(total).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(last);
            last = total;
        }
    }

    @Test
    void dailyEnergyResetsAtMidnightAndStaysZeroUntilSunrise() {
        var source = new ThingModelReadingSource(List.of(
                def("Ppv", "光伏功率", "DOUBLE", "kW", 0.0, 100.0, 2),
                def("eday", "日发电量", "DOUBLE", "kWh", 0.0, 2000.0, 2)), "inv-1", ZONE);

        double previous = -1;
        for (long t = at(16, 10, 0); t < at(17, 0, 0); t += 600_000L) {
            double value = (Double) source.next(t, 600).get("eday");
            assertThat(value).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(previous);
            previous = value;
        }
        assertThat(previous).isGreaterThan(0);
        assertThat(source.next(at(17, 0, 0), 600).get("eday")).isEqualTo(0.0);
        for (long t = at(17, 0, 10); t <= at(17, 5, 50); t += 600_000L) {
            assertThat(source.next(t, 600).get("eday")).isEqualTo(0.0);
        }
        for (long t = at(17, 6, 0); t <= at(17, 12, 0); t += 600_000L) {
            source.next(t, 600);
        }
        assertThat((Double) source.next(at(17, 12, 10), 600).get("eday")).isGreaterThan(0);
    }

    @Test
    void voltagesStayNear220WithSmallPhaseDifferences() {
        var source = new ThingModelReadingSource(List.of(
                def("Ua", "A 相电压", "DOUBLE", "V", null, null, 1),
                def("Ub", "B 相电压", "DOUBLE", "V", null, null, 1),
                def("Uc", "C 相电压", "DOUBLE", "V", null, null, 1)), "meter-2", ZONE);

        double sumA = 0;
        double sumB = 0;
        double sumC = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 500; i++) {
            Map<String, Object> reading = source.next(at(16, 0, 0) + i * 60_000L, 60);
            for (String phase : new String[]{"Ua", "Ub", "Uc"}) {
                assertThat((Double) reading.get(phase)).isBetween(215.0, 225.0);
            }
            sumA += (Double) reading.get("Ua");
            sumB += (Double) reading.get("Ub");
            sumC += (Double) reading.get("Uc");
        }
        assertThat(sumA / 500).isLessThan(sumB / 500);
        assertThat(sumB / 500).isLessThan(sumC / 500);
    }

    @Test
    void deviceTemperatureIsAboveTheAmbientTemperature() {
        var source = new ThingModelReadingSource(List.of(
                def("ta", "环境温度", "DOUBLE", "℃", -40.0, 60.0, 1),
                def("tw", "绕组温度", "DOUBLE", "℃", -40.0, 150.0, 1)), "trafo-1", ZONE);

        Map<String, Object> evening = source.next(at(16, 20, 0), 60);

        assertThat((Double) evening.get("tw") - (Double) evening.get("ta")).isGreaterThan(10.0);
        assertThat((Double) evening.get("ta")).isBetween(10.0, 26.0);
    }

    @Test
    void enumsUseTheNormalValueMostOfTheTimeAndHoldItForAnHour() {
        var source = new ThingModelReadingSource(List.of(new PropertyDef("st", "运行状态", "ENUM", null, null, null,
                null, List.of(opt(0, "停机"), opt(1, "运行"), opt(2, "检修")))), "inv-2", ZONE);

        int normal = 0;
        for (int h = 0; h < 1000; h++) {
            long hourStart = at(16, 0, 0) + h * 3_600_000L;
            Object first = source.next(hourStart, 60).get("st");
            for (int m = 1; m < 60; m += 7) {
                assertThat(source.next(hourStart + m * 60_000L, 60).get("st")).isEqualTo(first);
            }
            if (Long.valueOf(1).equals(first)) {
                normal++;
            }
        }
        assertThat(normal).isGreaterThan(750);
    }

    @Test
    void theFirstItemIsNormalWhenNoLabelLooksNormal() {
        assertThat(PointGenerator.normalOption(List.of(opt(5, "低速档"), opt(6, "高速档"))).value()).isEqualTo(5);
        assertThat(PointGenerator.normalOption(List.of(opt(0, "关闭"), opt(1, "开启"))).value()).isEqualTo(1);
    }

    @Test
    void booleansAreRarelyTrue() {
        var source = new ThingModelReadingSource(List.of(new PropertyDef("alarm", "告警", "BOOL", null, null,
                null, null, List.of())), "env-1", ZONE);

        int trues = 0;
        for (int h = 0; h < 2000; h++) {
            if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(source.next(at(16, 0, 0) + h * 3_600_000L, 60).get("alarm"))) {
                trues++;
            }
        }
        assertThat(trues).isBetween(20, 240);
    }

    @Test
    void textPropertiesAreSkipped() {
        var source = new ThingModelReadingSource(List.of(
                def("note", "备注", "TEXT", null, null, null, null),
                def("Ua", "A 相电压", "DOUBLE", "V", null, null, 1)), "meter-3", ZONE);

        assertThat(source.pointCount()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(source.next(at(16, 9, 0), 60)).containsOnlyKeys("Ua");
    }

    @Test
    void everyValueSatisfiesTheThingModelConstraints() {
        List<PropertyDef> model = List.of(
                def("Ua", "A 相电压", "DOUBLE", "V", 200.0, 240.0, 1),
                def("Ia", "A 相电流", "DOUBLE", "A", 0.0, 80.0, 2),
                def("P", "有功功率", "DOUBLE", "kW", 0.0, 60.0, 2),
                def("Ppv", "光伏功率", "DOUBLE", "kW", 0.0, 100.0, 2),
                def("irr", "辐照度", "DOUBLE", "W/m²", 0.0, 1200.0, 0),
                def("EPimp", "正向有功电能", "DOUBLE", "kWh", null, null, 3),
                def("eday", "日发电量", "DOUBLE", "kWh", 0.0, 1000.0, 2),
                def("flow", "瞬时流量", "DOUBLE", "m³/h", 0.0, 200.0, 2),
                def("total", "累计用水量", "DOUBLE", "m³", null, null, 2),
                def("tw", "绕组温度", "DOUBLE", "℃", -20.0, 120.0, 1),
                def("press", "出水压力", "DOUBLE", "MPa", 0.0, 1.0, 3),
                def("ph", "pH", "DOUBLE", null, 0.0, 14.0, 2),
                def("freq", "频率", "DOUBLE", "Hz", null, null, 2),
                def("count", "启动次数", "INT", null, 10.0, 20.0, null),
                new PropertyDef("st", "运行状态", "ENUM", null, null, null, null,
                        List.of(opt(0, "停机"), opt(1, "运行"), opt(2, "检修"))),
                new PropertyDef("alarm", "告警", "BOOL", null, null, null, null, List.of()),
                def("note", "备注", "TEXT", null, null, null, null));
        var source = new ThingModelReadingSource(model, "mixed-1", ZONE);

        for (int i = 0; i < 2016; i++) {
            Map<String, Object> reading = source.next(at(16, 0, 0) + i * 300_000L, 300);
            for (PropertyDef p : model) {
                Object v = reading.get(p.identifier());
                if (p.dataType().equals("TEXT")) {
                    assertThat(reading).doesNotContainKey(p.identifier());
                    continue;
                }
                assertThat(v).as(p.identifier()).isNotNull();
                switch (p.dataType()) {
                    case "DOUBLE" -> {
                        assertThat(v).as(p.identifier()).isInstanceOf(Double.class);
                        double d = (Double) v;
                        if (p.minValue() != null) {
                            assertThat(d).as(p.identifier()).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(p.minValue());
                        }
                        if (p.maxValue() != null) {
                            assertThat(d).as(p.identifier()).isLessThanOrEqualTo(p.maxValue());
                        }
                        double scaled = d * Math.pow(10, p.decimals() == null ? 3 : p.decimals());
                        assertThat(Math.abs(scaled - Math.rint(scaled))).as(p.identifier() + " decimals")
                                .isLessThan(1e-6);
                    }
                    case "INT" -> {
                        assertThat(v).as(p.identifier()).isInstanceOf(Long.class);
                        assertThat((Long) v).isBetween(10L, 20L);
                    }
                    case "ENUM" -> assertThat(List.of(0L, 1L, 2L)).contains((Long) v);
                    case "BOOL" -> assertThat(v).isInstanceOf(Boolean.class);
                    default -> throw new AssertionError(p.dataType());
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```
