Create `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/PropertyDef.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 物模型属性定义（模拟器从 GET /api/v1/products/{pk}/properties 读取的字段），用于按属性生成取值。
 *
 * @param dataType  DOUBLE / INT / BOOL / TEXT / ENUM
 * @param minValue  下限，未设置为 null
 * @param maxValue  上限，未设置为 null
 * @param decimals  小数位，未设置为 null
 * @param enumItems 枚举项；非枚举或未设置时为空列表（不为 null）
 */
public record PropertyDef(String identifier, String name, String dataType, String unit, Double minValue,
                          Double maxValue, Integer decimals, List<EnumOption> enumItems) {

    public PropertyDef {
        enumItems = enumItems == null ? List.of() : List.copyOf(enumItems);
    }

    /**
     * 枚举项。
     *
     * @param value 设备上报的整数值
     * @param label 显示名称
     */
    public record EnumOption(long value, String label) {
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/PointKind.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

/** 测点类别，对应 V0.2-R spec 5.2 表中的生成规则；由 {@link PointClassifier} 判定。 */
public enum PointKind {
    /** 电压：220 附近小幅波动，三相略有差别。 */
    VOLTAGE,
    /** 电流、非光伏的有功/交流功率：按日用电曲线变化。 */
    LOAD,
    /** 光伏功率：6～18 点正弦日照 × 容量，夜间 0，叠加云层扰动。 */
    PV_POWER,
    /** 辐照度：同光伏功率，容量 1000 W/m²。 */
    IRRADIANCE,
    /** 累计量（电能、用水）：单调递增，增量与功率/流量相符。 */
    CUMULATIVE,
    /** 日累计（日发电量等）：同累计量，每天零点归零。 */
    DAILY_ENERGY,
    /** 温度：环境温度昼夜变化，设备温度随负载升高。 */
    TEMPERATURE,
    /** 流量：早晚用水高峰。 */
    FLOW,
    /** 水压、pH、浊度、余氯、湿度、PM2.5、CO₂、风速：合理范围内缓慢漂移。 */
    DRIFT,
    /** 枚举：多数时间为正常值，偶尔切换，同设备一小时内不变。 */
    ENUM,
    /** 布尔：多数为否，偶尔为是。 */
    BOOL,
    /** 其他数值：上下限内随机游走，无上下限时按单位推断范围。 */
    OTHER,
    /** 不生成（TEXT、无枚举项的 ENUM、未知类型）。 */
    SKIP
}
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/PointClassifier.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * 测点分类（纯函数）：按物模型的类型、标识、名称与单位判断测点适用 spec 5.2 表中的哪条规则。
 * 规则按顺序匹配，先匹配者生效（顺序见实施计划 Task 5）。
 */
public final class PointClassifier {

    static final Set<String> ENERGY_UNITS = Set.of("kwh", "mwh", "wh");
    static final Set<String> VOLUME_UNITS = Set.of("m3");
    static final Set<String> POWER_UNITS = Set.of("w", "kw", "mw");

    private PointClassifier() {
    }

    /** 是否会为该属性生成取值（即分类不是 SKIP）。 */
    public static boolean simulated(PropertyDef p) {
        return classify(p) != PointKind.SKIP;
    }

    /** 判定属性的类别；TEXT、无枚举项的 ENUM 与未知类型为 SKIP。 */
    public static PointKind classify(PropertyDef p) {
        String type = p.dataType() == null ? "" : p.dataType().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        return switch (type) {
            case "ENUM" -> p.enumItems().isEmpty() ? PointKind.SKIP : PointKind.ENUM;
            case "BOOL" -> PointKind.BOOL;
            case "DOUBLE", "INT" -> numeric(p);
            default -> PointKind.SKIP;
        };
    }

    /** 数值属性的分类：累计量 → 辐照 → 光伏功率 → 电压 → 负载（电流/功率）→ 温度 → 流量 → 漂移类 → 其他。 */
    private static PointKind numeric(PropertyDef p) {
        String unit = normalizeUnit(p.unit());
        String text = text(p);
        boolean daily = isDaily(text);
        if (ENERGY_UNITS.contains(unit) || VOLUME_UNITS.contains(unit)) {
            return daily ? PointKind.DAILY_ENERGY : PointKind.CUMULATIVE;
        }
        if (unit.isEmpty() && (containsAny(text, "累计", "total", "energy") || p.identifier().startsWith("EP"))) {
            return daily ? PointKind.DAILY_ENERGY : PointKind.CUMULATIVE;
        }
        if (unit.equals("w/m2") || containsAny(text, "辐照", "irradiance")) {
            return PointKind.IRRADIANCE;
        }
        if (POWER_UNITS.contains(unit) && isGeneration(text)) {
            return PointKind.PV_POWER;
        }
        if (unit.equals("v") || (unit.isEmpty() && text.contains("电压"))) {
            return PointKind.VOLTAGE;
        }
        if (unit.equals("a") || POWER_UNITS.contains(unit)) {
            return PointKind.LOAD;
        }
        if (unit.equals("℃") || containsAny(text, "温度", "temperature")) {
            return PointKind.TEMPERATURE;
        }
        if (unit.equals("m3/h") || containsAny(text, "流量", "flow")) {
            return PointKind.FLOW;
        }
        if (ValueRanges.drift(p) != null) {
            return PointKind.DRIFT;
        }
        return PointKind.OTHER;
    }

    /** 是否与发电（光伏、逆变器）有关。 */
    static boolean isGeneration(String text) {
        return containsAny(text, "光伏", "发电", "pv", "solar", "eday", "etotal");
    }

    /** 是否为按天归零的累计量。 */
    static boolean isDaily(String text) {
        return containsAny(text, "eday", "daily", "today", "日");
    }

    /** 累计量是否为体积（用水）：单位 m³，或非电能单位且名称与水有关。 */
    static boolean isVolume(String unit, String text) {
        return VOLUME_UNITS.contains(unit) || (!ENERGY_UNITS.contains(unit) && containsAny(text, "水", "water"));
    }

    /** 是否为设备内部温度（随负载升高）。 */
    static boolean isDeviceTemperature(String text) {
        return containsAny(text, "绕组", "机内", "内部", "设备", "模块", "油温", "igbt", "winding", "internal");
    }

    /** 用于关键字匹配的文字：标识 + 空格 + 名称，转小写。 */
    static String text(PropertyDef p) {
        return (p.identifier() + " " + (p.name() == null ? "" : p.name())).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
    }

    /** 规范化单位：小写、去空格，²→2、³→3、°c→℃、μ/µ→u；null 为空串。 */
    static String normalizeUnit(String unit) {
        if (unit == null) {
            return "";
        }
        return unit.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)
                .replace("²", "2").replace("³", "3").replace("°c", "℃")
                .replace("μ", "u").replace("µ", "u").replace(" ", "");
    }

    static boolean containsAny(String text, String... needles) {
        for (String needle : needles) {
            if (text.contains(needle)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

/** 数值范围（纯函数）：漂移类测点的合理范围表，以及无上下限的其他测点按单位推断的范围。 */
final class ValueRanges {

    private ValueRanges() {
    }

    /**
     * 漂移类测点（水压、pH、浊度、余氯、湿度、PM2.5、CO₂、风速）的合理范围。
     *
     * @return {下限, 上限}；不属于漂移类时为 null
     */
    static double[] drift(PropertyDef p) {
        String unit = PointClassifier.normalizeUnit(p.unit());
        String text = PointClassifier.text(p).replace("₂", "2");
        String name = p.name() == null ? "" : p.name();
        if (PointClassifier.containsAny(text, "水压", "压力", "pressure")) {
            return switch (unit) {
                case "kpa" -> new double[]{250, 450};
                case "bar" -> new double[]{2.5, 4.5};
                default -> new double[]{0.25, 0.45};
            };
        }
        if (p.identifier().equalsIgnoreCase("ph") || name.contains("pH") || text.contains("酸碱")) {
            return new double[]{6.8, 7.8};
        }
        if (PointClassifier.containsAny(text, "浊度", "turbidity") || unit.equals("ntu")) {
            return new double[]{0.1, 1.0};
        }
        if (PointClassifier.containsAny(text, "余氯", "chlorine")) {
            return new double[]{0.3, 0.8};
        }
        if (PointClassifier.containsAny(text, "湿度", "humidity") || unit.equals("%rh")) {
            return new double[]{40, 75};
        }
        if (PointClassifier.containsAny(text, "pm2.5", "pm25")) {
            return new double[]{10, 75};
        }
        if (PointClassifier.containsAny(text, "co2", "二氧化碳") || unit.equals("ppm")) {
            return new double[]{420, 900};
        }
        if (PointClassifier.containsAny(text, "风速", "wind")) {
            return new double[]{1, 8};
        }
        return null;
    }

    /**
     * 其他数值测点的游走范围：上下限都有时就是 [min, max]；否则按单位推断（再无则 0～100），
     * 再用已有的一侧上限或下限替换推断值。
     */
    static double[] infer(PropertyDef p) {
        Double min = p.minValue();
        Double max = p.maxValue();
        if (min != null && max != null) {
            return new double[]{min, max};
        }
        double[] byUnit = switch (PointClassifier.normalizeUnit(p.unit())) {
            case "hz" -> new double[]{49.8, 50.2};
            case "%" -> new double[]{0, 100};
            case "kvar" -> new double[]{0, 50};
            case "kva" -> new double[]{0, 100};
            case "v" -> new double[]{200, 240};
            case "a" -> new double[]{0, 50};
            case "kw" -> new double[]{0, 100};
            case "℃" -> new double[]{0, 40};
            case "mpa" -> new double[]{0, 1};
            case "m/s" -> new double[]{0, 10};
            default -> new double[]{0, 100};
        };
        double span = byUnit[1] - byUnit[0];
        double lo = byUnit[0];
        double hi = byUnit[1];
        if (min != null) {
            lo = min;
            if (hi <= lo) {
                hi = lo + span;
            }
        }
        if (max != null) {
            hi = max;
            if (lo >= hi) {
                lo = hi - span;
            }
        }
        return new double[]{lo, hi};
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/DayCurves.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

/** 日变化曲线（纯函数，hour 为本地时间 0～24 的小数小时）：用电负荷、日照、用水、环境温度。 */
final class DayCurves {

    private DayCurves() {
    }

    /** 以 center 为中心、宽度 width 的高斯峰，按 24 小时环绕计算距离（23 点与 1 点相距 2 小时）。 */
    static double bump(double hour, double center, double width) {
        double d = Math.abs(hour - center);
        d = Math.min(d, 24 - d);
        return Math.exp(-(d * d) / (2 * width * width));
    }

    /** 日用电负荷系数 0.25～1：凌晨最低，上午约 10:30 与晚上 8 点两个高峰。 */
    static double loadFactor(double hour) {
        double v = 0.45 + 0.35 * bump(hour, 10.5, 2.5) + 0.45 * bump(hour, 20, 2.0) - 0.2 * bump(hour, 3.5, 2.5);
        return clamp(v, 0.25, 1.0);
    }

    /** 日照系数：6～18 点为正弦（正午为 1），其余时间恰为 0。 */
    static double sun(double hour) {
        return hour <= 6 || hour >= 18 ? 0 : Math.sin(Math.PI * (hour - 6) / 12);
    }

    /** 从 0 点到 hour 的日照系数积分（相当于满功率小时数），用于日发电量的初值。 */
    static double sunHoursUntil(double hour) {
        if (hour <= 6) {
            return 0;
        }
        if (hour >= 18) {
            return 24 / Math.PI;
        }
        return 12 / Math.PI * (1 - Math.cos(Math.PI * (hour - 6) / 12));
    }

    /** 用水流量系数 0.15～1：早 8 点、晚 7 点半两个高峰，深夜最低。 */
    static double flowFactor(double hour) {
        double v = 0.2 + 0.8 * bump(hour, 8, 1.3) + 0.65 * bump(hour, 19.5, 1.6) - 0.1 * bump(hour, 3, 2);
        return clamp(v, 0.15, 1.0);
    }

    /** 环境温度（℃）：凌晨 3 点约 11℃，下午 3 点约 25℃。 */
    static double ambientTemperature(double hour) {
        return 18 + 7 * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * (hour - 9) / 24);
    }

    static double clamp(double v, double min, double max) {
        return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, v));
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/ValueShaper.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

/** 把生成的原始数值整理为满足物模型约束的上报值（纯函数），保证不会被流水线以 FIELD_REJECTED 剔除。 */
final class ValueShaper {

    private ValueShaper() {
    }

    /**
     * 整理数值。
     *
     * @return INT：四舍五入后夹在整数上下限内的 Long；DOUBLE：夹在上下限内并按小数位（未设置为 3，最多 6）
     *         四舍五入的 Double，舍入后仍在上下限内
     */
    static Object shape(PropertyDef p, double raw) {
        double lo = p.minValue() == null ? Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY : p.minValue();
        double hi = p.maxValue() == null ? Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY : p.maxValue();
        if ("INT".equalsIgnoreCase(p.dataType())) {
            long v = Math.round(raw);
            if (v < lo) {
                v = (long) Math.ceil(lo);
            }
            if (v > hi) {
                v = (long) Math.floor(hi);
            }
            return v;
        }
        int digits = p.decimals() == null ? 3 : Math.max(0, Math.min(p.decimals(), 6));
        double scale = Math.pow(10, digits);
        double v = Math.round(DayCurves.clamp(raw, lo, hi) * scale) / scale;
        if (v > hi) {
            v = Math.floor(hi * scale) / scale;
        }
        if (v < lo) {
            v = Math.ceil(lo * scale) / scale;
        }
        return v;
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import java.util.Map;

/** 一台模拟设备的读数来源：每次上报调用一次，返回测点标识到取值的映射。 */
@FunctionalInterface
public interface ReadingSource {

    /**
     * 生成一次读数。
     *
     * @param epochMillis     上报时间（设备时间戳，毫秒）
     * @param intervalSeconds 距上次上报的秒数，用于累计量积分
     */
    Map<String, Object> next(long epochMillis, double intervalSeconds);
}
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/PointGenerator.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

/**
 * 单个测点的取值生成器（每台设备每个测点一个实例，保存游走、云层与累计状态），实现 spec 5.2 的各条规则。
 * 随机数以 deviceKey 与 identifier 为种子，同一设备每次启动得到相同的序列；状态只在内存中，重启后从合理初值开始。
 */
final class PointGenerator {

    /** 一轮读数中已生成的功率与流量合计，供累计量按功率/流量积分。 */
    static final class Rates {
        double pvKw;
        double loadKw;
        double flowM3h;
        boolean hasPv;
        boolean hasLoad;
        boolean hasFlow;
    }

    /**
     * 设备的额定量，供没有对应功率/流量测点的累计量估算速率与初值。
     *
     * @param pvKw    光伏容量（kW）
     * @param loadKw  用电负荷额定值（kW）
     * @param flowM3h 用水流量额定值（m³/h）
     */
    record Nominals(double pvKw, double loadKw, double flowM3h) {
    }

    private static final long HOUR_MS = 3_600_000L;

    final PropertyDef def;
    final PointKind kind;
    private final String unit;
    private final String text;
    private final long seed;
    private final Random random;
    private final double nominal;
    private final double phaseOffset;
    private final double[] range;
    private boolean started;
    private double state;
    private double cloud = 0.9;
    private LocalDate day;

    PointGenerator(PropertyDef def, String deviceKey) {
        this.def = def;
        this.kind = PointClassifier.classify(def);
        this.unit = PointClassifier.normalizeUnit(def.unit());
        this.text = PointClassifier.text(def);
        this.seed = deviceKey.hashCode() * 31L + def.identifier().hashCode();
        this.random = new Random(seed);
        this.nominal = nominal();
        this.phaseOffset = phaseOffset(def.identifier());
        this.range = switch (kind) {
            case DRIFT -> ValueRanges.drift(def);
            case OTHER -> ValueRanges.infer(def);
            default -> null;
        };
    }

    /** 是否为累计量（需在本轮其他测点之后计算）。 */
    boolean cumulative() {
        return kind == PointKind.CUMULATIVE || kind == PointKind.DAILY_ENERGY;
    }

    /** 额定值换算为 kW（电流测点为 0，不计入用电功率）。 */
    double nominalKw() {
        return unit.equals("a") ? 0 : toKw(nominal);
    }

    double nominalValue() {
        return nominal;
    }

    /**
     * 生成非累计测点的值，并把光伏功率、用电功率（非电流）、流量计入 rates。
     *
     * @param hour 本地时间的小数小时
     */
    Object next(long epochMillis, double hour, Rates rates) {
        return switch (kind) {
            case ENUM -> enumValue(epochMillis);
            case BOOL -> boolValue(epochMillis);
            default -> {
                double raw = instantaneous(hour);
                addToRates(raw, rates);
                yield ValueShaper.shape(def, raw);
            }
        };
    }

    /**
     * 生成累计测点的值：按本轮功率/流量（没有对应测点时按额定值 × 日曲线）积分 intervalSeconds；
     * 日累计在本地日期变化时归零；有上限时不超过上限。除日累计归零外只增不减。
     */
    Object accumulate(double hour, LocalDate date, double intervalSeconds, Rates rates, Nominals nominals) {
        if (!started) {
            state = initialTotal(hour, nominals);
            day = date;
            started = true;
        }
        if (kind == PointKind.DAILY_ENERGY && !date.equals(day)) {
            day = date;
            state = 0;
        }
        state += rate(hour, rates, nominals) * intervalSeconds / 3600 * unitScale();
        if (def.maxValue() != null) {
            state = Math.min(state, def.maxValue());
        }
        return ValueShaper.shape(def, state);
    }

    /** 非累计数值测点的原始值。 */
    private double instantaneous(double hour) {
        return switch (kind) {
            case VOLTAGE -> 220 + phaseOffset + random.nextGaussian() * 0.6;
            case LOAD -> nominal * DayCurves.loadFactor(hour) * (1 + random.nextGaussian() * 0.03);
            case PV_POWER, IRRADIANCE -> solar(hour);
            case TEMPERATURE -> temperature(hour);
            case FLOW -> Math.max(0, nominal * DayCurves.flowFactor(hour) * (1 + random.nextGaussian() * 0.05));
            default -> walk();
        };
    }

    /** 光伏功率 / 辐照度：容量 × 日照 × 云层系数（0.55～1 之间随机游走），夜间恰为 0。 */
    private double solar(double hour) {
        cloud = DayCurves.clamp(cloud + random.nextGaussian() * 0.05, 0.55, 1.0);
        double sun = DayCurves.sun(hour);
        return sun == 0 ? 0 : nominal * sun * cloud;
    }

    /** 环境温度按昼夜变化；设备内部温度在环境温度上加 10 + 30 × 负荷系数。 */
    private double temperature(double hour) {
        double ambient = DayCurves.ambientTemperature(hour) + random.nextGaussian() * 0.3;
        return PointClassifier.isDeviceTemperature(text) ? ambient + 10 + 30 * DayCurves.loadFactor(hour) : ambient;
    }

    /** 范围内均值回归的随机游走：首次取范围中部附近，之后每步向中点回拉 2% 并叠加范围 2% 的噪声。 */
    private double walk() {
        double lo = range[0];
        double hi = range[1];
        double span = hi - lo;
        if (!started) {
            state = lo + span * (0.3 + 0.4 * random.nextDouble());
            started = true;
        } else {
            state += ((lo + hi) / 2 - state) * 0.02 + random.nextGaussian() * span * 0.02;
        }
        state = DayCurves.clamp(state, lo, hi);
        return state;
    }

    /** 把本测点的原始值计入本轮速率合计（光伏功率、功率单位的负载、流量）。 */
    private void addToRates(double raw, Rates rates) {
        switch (kind) {
            case PV_POWER -> {
                rates.pvKw += toKw(raw);
                rates.hasPv = true;
            }
            case LOAD -> {
                if (!unit.equals("a")) {
                    rates.loadKw += toKw(raw);
                    rates.hasLoad = true;
                }
            }
            case FLOW -> {
                rates.flowM3h += raw;
                rates.hasFlow = true;
            }
            default -> {
            }
        }
    }

    /** 累计量的速率（kW 或 m³/h）：用水 → 流量，发电 → 光伏功率，其余 → 用电功率。 */
    private double rate(double hour, Rates rates, Nominals n) {
        if (PointClassifier.isVolume(unit, text)) {
            return rates.hasFlow ? rates.flowM3h : n.flowM3h() * DayCurves.flowFactor(hour);
        }
        if (PointClassifier.isGeneration(text)) {
            return rates.hasPv ? rates.pvKw : n.pvKw() * DayCurves.sun(hour) * 0.85;
        }
        return rates.hasLoad ? rates.loadKw : n.loadKw() * DayCurves.loadFactor(hour);
    }

    /** 累计量初值：日累计为 0 点至今的曲线积分，终身累计取 1000～10000 kWh 或 500～5000 m³；有上限时不超过一半。 */
    private double initialTotal(double hour, Nominals n) {
        double value;
        boolean volume = PointClassifier.isVolume(unit, text);
        if (kind == PointKind.DAILY_ENERGY) {
            if (volume) {
                value = n.flowM3h() * 0.5 * hour;
            } else if (PointClassifier.isGeneration(text)) {
                value = n.pvKw() * 0.85 * DayCurves.sunHoursUntil(hour);
            } else {
                value = n.loadKw() * 0.6 * hour;
            }
        } else {
            value = volume ? 500 + random.nextDouble() * 4500 : 1000 + random.nextDouble() * 9000;
        }
        value *= unitScale();
        if (def.maxValue() != null) {
            value = Math.min(value, def.maxValue() * 0.5);
        }
        if (def.minValue() != null) {
            value = Math.max(value, def.minValue());
        }
        return value;
    }

    /** kWh 为基准的单位换算：MWh 0.001，Wh 1000，其余（kWh、m³、无单位）为 1。 */
    private double unitScale() {
        return switch (unit) {
            case "mwh" -> 0.001;
            case "wh" -> 1000;
            default -> 1;
        };
    }

    /** 枚举：每个（设备, 测点, 整点小时）有 85% 为正常值，其余取另一个枚举项；同一小时内不变。 */
    private Object enumValue(long epochMillis) {
        List<PropertyDef.EnumOption> items = def.enumItems();
        PropertyDef.EnumOption normal = normalOption(items);
        long hourIndex = Math.floorDiv(epochMillis, HOUR_MS);
        long draw = Math.floorMod(OperatingState.mix(seed * 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15L ^ hourIndex), 100L);
        if (draw < 85 || items.size() == 1) {
            return normal.value();
        }
        List<PropertyDef.EnumOption> others = items.stream().filter(i -> i != normal).toList();
        int pick = (int) Math.floorMod(OperatingState.mix(seed ^ (hourIndex * 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15L)),
                (long) others.size());
        return others.get(pick).value();
    }

    /** 布尔：每个（设备, 测点, 整点小时）有 5% 为 true。 */
    private Object boolValue(long epochMillis) {
        long hourIndex = Math.floorDiv(epochMillis, HOUR_MS);
        return Math.floorMod(OperatingState.mix((seed + 0x632BE59BD9B4E019L) * 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15L ^ hourIndex),
                100L) < 5;
    }

    /** 枚举的正常值：第一个标签含 运行/发电/开启/正常 的枚举项，否则第一个枚举项。 */
    static PropertyDef.EnumOption normalOption(List<PropertyDef.EnumOption> items) {
        for (PropertyDef.EnumOption item : items) {
            if (item.label() != null && PointClassifier.containsAny(item.label(), "运行", "发电", "开启", "正常")) {
                return item;
            }
        }
        return items.getFirst();
    }

    /** 额定值：负载 0.75×上限，光伏 0.9×上限，辐照 min(上限,1000)，流量 0.7×上限；无上限时按单位取默认值。 */
    private double nominal() {
        Double max = def.maxValue();
        boolean hasMax = max != null && max > 0;
        return switch (kind) {
            case LOAD -> hasMax ? max * 0.75 : switch (unit) {
                case "a" -> 50.0;
                case "w" -> 30_000.0;
                case "mw" -> 0.03;
                default -> 30.0;
            };
            case PV_POWER -> hasMax ? max * 0.9 : switch (unit) {
                case "w" -> 50_000.0;
                case "mw" -> 0.05;
                default -> 50.0;
            };
            case IRRADIANCE -> hasMax ? Math.min(max, 1000.0) : 1000.0;
            case FLOW -> hasMax ? max * 0.7 : 30.0;
            default -> 0.0;
        };
    }

    /** 三相电压的固定偏移：标识以 a/b/c 结尾时分别为 -0.8 / 0 / +0.8 V，其他按标识哈希取 -0.8～+0.8。 */
    private static double phaseOffset(String identifier) {
        char last = Character.toLowerCase(identifier.charAt(identifier.length() - 1));
        return switch (last) {
            case 'a' -> -0.8;
            case 'b' -> 0.0;
            case 'c' -> 0.8;
            default -> (Math.floorMod(identifier.hashCode(), 5) - 2) * 0.4;
        };
    }

    private double toKw(double value) {
        return switch (unit) {
            case "w" -> value / 1000;
            case "mw" -> value * 1000;
            default -> value;
        };
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/ThingModelReadingSource.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.ZonedDateTime;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 按产品物模型为一台设备生成读数（spec 5.2）：每个可模拟的属性一个 {@link PointGenerator}，TEXT 等跳过；
 * 先生成瞬时量，再按本轮功率/流量积分累计量。日曲线按 zone 的本地时间计算。
 */
public final class ThingModelReadingSource implements ReadingSource {

    private final ZoneId zone;
    private final List<PointGenerator> instant = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<PointGenerator> totals = new ArrayList<>();
    private final PointGenerator.Nominals nominals;

    /**
     * @param properties 产品物模型的属性定义
     * @param deviceKey  设备 Key（随机数种子，不同设备的序列不同）
     * @param zone       计算日曲线所用的时区
     */
    public ThingModelReadingSource(List<PropertyDef> properties, String deviceKey, ZoneId zone) {
        this.zone = zone;
        double pv = 0;
        double load = 0;
        double flow = 0;
        for (PropertyDef p : properties) {
            if (!PointClassifier.simulated(p)) {
                continue;
            }
            PointGenerator g = new PointGenerator(p, deviceKey);
            (g.cumulative() ? totals : instant).add(g);
            switch (g.kind) {
                case PV_POWER -> pv += g.nominalKw();
                case LOAD -> load += g.nominalKw();
                case FLOW -> flow += g.nominalValue();
                default -> {
                }
            }
        }
        this.nominals = new PointGenerator.Nominals(pv > 0 ? pv : 50, load > 0 ? load : 30, flow > 0 ? flow : 30);
    }

    /** 会生成取值的测点数。 */
    public int pointCount() {
        return instant.size() + totals.size();
    }

    @Override
    public Map<String, Object> next(long epochMillis, double intervalSeconds) {
        ZonedDateTime time = Instant.ofEpochMilli(epochMillis).atZone(zone);
        double hour = time.getHour() + time.getMinute() / 60.0 + time.getSecond() / 3600.0;
        PointGenerator.Rates rates = new PointGenerator.Rates();
        Map<String, Object> reading = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (PointGenerator g : instant) {
            reading.put(g.def.identifier(), g.next(epochMillis, hour, rates));
        }
        for (PointGenerator g : totals) {
            reading.put(g.def.identifier(), g.accumulate(hour, time.toLocalDate(), intervalSeconds, rates, nominals));
        }
        return reading;
    }
}
```

## Task 6: Tenant discovery and per-device readings in the running simulator (track B)

Adds the REST calls the platform admin needs, the discovery flow (spec §5.1) and the assembly of simulated devices. The device simulator gets per-device formats and reading sources, and the main flow simulates the demo product plus every discovered tenant product.

**Files:**
- Modify: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/ConduitApiClient.java` (full replacement)
- Create: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/TenantDiscovery.java`
- Create: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/SimulationPlanner.java`
- Create: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/SimulatedDevice.java`
- Modify: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/DeviceSimulator.java` (full replacement)
- Modify: `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/SimulatorApplication.java` (full replacement)
- Test: `conduit-simulator/src/test/java/dev/conduit/simulator/TenantDiscoveryTest.java` (new)
- Test: `conduit-simulator/src/test/java/dev/conduit/simulator/DeviceSimulatorIT.java` (add one test)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SimulatorConfig` (`platformUsername()`, `platformPassword()`, `multiTenant()`, `tenants()`, `maxDevicesPerProduct()`), `TenantFilter`, `PayloadFormat.fromCodec` (Task 4); `PropertyDef`, `ReadingSource`, `ThingModelReadingSource`, `PointClassifier.simulated` (Task 5); the existing `DemoProvisioner`, `ReadingGenerator`, `OperatingState`, `ConnectRetry`, `DeviceCredentials`.
- Produces:
  - `ConduitApiClient` keeps every existing public method unchanged. Task 7 depends on `login`, `findOrCreateProduct(String, String)`, `createDevice`, `listDeviceKeys`, `deleteDevice` and `deleteProduct`. It adds:
    - `record TenantSummary(String code, String name, String status)`, `record ProductSummary(String productKey, String name, String codec, int propertyCount)`;
    - `List<TenantSummary> listActiveTenants()`, `void enterTenant(String code)`, `void exitTenant()`;
    - `List<ProductSummary> listProducts()`, `List<PropertyDef> properties(String productKey)`, `List<DeviceSummary> listEnabledDevices(String productKey, int limit)`.
  - `record TenantDiscovery.DiscoveredProduct(String tenantCode, String productKey, String productName, PayloadFormat format, List<PropertyDef> properties, List<DeviceCredentials> devices)` and `static List<DiscoveredProduct> TenantDiscovery.discover(ConduitApiClient platform, TenantFilter filter, int maxDevicesPerProduct, Set<String> skipProductKeys)`.
  - `static List<SimulatedDevice> SimulationPlanner.tenantDevices(SimulatorConfig config, Supplier<ConduitApiClient> clients, Set<String> demoProductKeys)`.
  - `record SimulatedDevice(DeviceCredentials credentials, PayloadFormat format, ReadingSource readings)` with `static SimulatedDevice demo(DeviceCredentials, PayloadFormat, boolean operatingState)`.
  - `DeviceSimulator.runDevices(List<SimulatedDevice>)`. The existing `run(List<DeviceCredentials>)` stays and delegates to it.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- Only `ACTIVE` tenants and `ENABLED` devices are simulated. The simulator asks the server for them (`status=ACTIVE`, `status=ENABLED`) and filters the returned items again on their `status` field.
- Products are skipped, at DEBUG unless noted, when:
  - they have no properties (`propertyCount == 0`);
  - none of their properties can be simulated (all TEXT);
  - they belong to the demo product handled by `DemoProvisioner`, matched by product key;
  - their codec has no matching payload format. This one is logged at WARN.
- Failure isolation:
  - A tenant that cannot be entered is skipped with a WARN.
  - A product whose properties, devices or secret resets fail is skipped with a WARN, and the tenant's other products continue.
  - The simulator exits the tenant after every successful enter, even when discovery inside it failed.
  - A failed platform-admin login is a WARN, and the simulator continues with the demo product only.
- Device secrets are reset only for the devices that will be simulated, at most `SIM_MAX_DEVICES_PER_PRODUCT` per product. The secret is never logged.
- Tenant devices use the system time zone (`ZoneId.systemDefault()`) for their day curves.

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpExchange;
import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpServer;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
import java.net.URI;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/**
 * TenantDiscovery 与 SimulationPlanner 单元测试（本地 HttpServer 模拟 Conduit REST）：按 SIM_TENANTS 过滤、只进入启用中的租户、
 * 只取启用设备且每产品不超过上限、按解码器选格式、跳过无物模型/演示/不支持解码器的产品、单个租户失败不影响其他租户、
 * 平台密码为空时不发现租户。
 */
class TenantDiscoveryTest {

    HttpServer server;
    URI baseUrl;
    final List<String> calls = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
    final List<String> queries = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
    final List<String> authorized = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
    final List<String> bodies = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
    volatile boolean westProductsFail;

    @BeforeEach
    void start() throws IOException {
        server = HttpServer.create(new InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", 0), 0);
        server.createContext("/", this::handle);
        server.start();
        baseUrl = URI.create("http://127.0.0.1:" + server.getAddress().getPort());
    }

    @AfterEach
    void stop() {
        server.stop(0);
    }

    private void handle(HttpExchange ex) throws IOException {
        String path = ex.getRequestURI().getPath();
        String query = ex.getRequestURI().getQuery();
        String call = ex.getRequestMethod() + " " + path;
        String auth = ex.getRequestHeaders().getFirst("Authorization");
        calls.add(call);
        queries.add(call + "?" + query);
        authorized.add(call + " " + auth);
        bodies.add(call + " " + new String(ex.getRequestBody().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        int status = 200;
        String body;
        if (call.equals("POST /api/v1/auth/login")) {
            body = "{\"accessToken\":\"tok-platform\"}";
        } else if (call.equals("GET /api/v1/platform/tenants")) {
            body = """
                    {"items":[{"code":"east","name":"华东电力","status":"ACTIVE"},
                              {"code":"off","name":"停用租户","status":"DISABLED"},
                              {"code":"west","name":"西北新能源","status":"ACTIVE"}],
                     "total":3,"page":1,"size":100}""";
        } else if (call.startsWith("POST /api/v1/platform/tenants/") && call.endsWith("/enter")) {
            String code = path.split("/")[5];
            body = "{\"accessToken\":\"imp-" + code + "\"}";
        } else if (call.equals("POST /api/v1/auth/exit-tenant")) {
            body = "{\"accessToken\":\"tok-platform\"}";
        } else if (call.equals("GET /api/v1/products") && "Bearer imp-east".equals(auth)) {
            body = """
                    {"items":[{"productKey":"pk-meter","name":"10kV 进线电表","codec":"flat-json","propertyCount":3},
                              {"productKey":"pk-empty","name":"无物模型网关","codec":"standard-json","propertyCount":0},
                              {"productKey":"pk-demo","name":"三相电表（演示）","codec":"standard-json","propertyCount":8},
                              {"productKey":"pk-binary","name":"二进制采集器","codec":"modbus-hex","propertyCount":2}],
                     "total":4,"page":1,"size":100}""";
        } else if (call.equals("GET /api/v1/products") && "Bearer imp-west".equals(auth)) {
            if (westProductsFail) {
                status = 500;
                body = "{\"detail\":\"boom\"}";
            } else {
                body = """
                        {"items":[{"productKey":"pk-pv","name":"组串式逆变器","codec":"standard-json","propertyCount":1}],
                         "total":1,"page":1,"size":100}""";
            }
        } else if (call.equals("GET /api/v1/products/pk-meter/properties")) {
            body = """
                    [{"identifier":"Ua","name":"A 相电压","dataType":"DOUBLE","unit":"V","minValue":0,"maxValue":300,
                      "decimals":1,"enumItems":null},
                     {"identifier":"st","name":"运行状态","dataType":"ENUM","unit":null,"minValue":null,"maxValue":null,
                      "decimals":null,"enumItems":[{"value":0,"label":"停机"},{"value":1,"label":"运行"}]},
                     {"identifier":"note","name":"备注","dataType":"TEXT","unit":null,"minValue":null,"maxValue":null,
                      "decimals":null,"enumItems":null}]""";
        } else if (call.equals("GET /api/v1/products/pk-pv/properties")) {
            body = """
                    [{"identifier":"Ppv","name":"光伏功率","dataType":"DOUBLE","unit":"kW","minValue":0,"maxValue":100,
                      "decimals":2,"enumItems":null}]""";
        } else if (call.equals("GET /api/v1/devices") && query.contains("productKey=pk-meter")) {
            body = """
                    {"items":[{"deviceKey":"d-1","name":"1号进线 电表01","status":"ENABLED"},
                              {"deviceKey":"d-2","name":"1号进线 电表02","status":"DISABLED"},
                              {"deviceKey":"d-3","name":"1号进线 电表03","status":"ENABLED"},
                              {"deviceKey":"d-4","name":"1号进线 电表04","status":"ENABLED"}],
                     "total":4,"page":1,"size":100}""";
        } else if (call.equals("GET /api/v1/devices") && query.contains("productKey=pk-pv")) {
            body = """
                    {"items":[{"deviceKey":"pv-1","name":"1号方阵 逆变器01","status":"ENABLED"}],
                     "total":1,"page":1,"size":100}""";
        } else if (call.startsWith("POST /api/v1/devices/") && call.endsWith("/reset-secret")) {
            String deviceKey = path.split("/")[4];
            body = "{\"deviceKey\":\"" + deviceKey + "\",\"deviceSecret\":\"s-" + deviceKey + "\"}";
        } else {
            status = 404;
            body = "{}";
        }
        byte[] bytes = body.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        ex.getResponseHeaders().add("Content-Type", "application/json");
        ex.sendResponseHeaders(status, bytes.length);
        ex.getResponseBody().write(bytes);
        ex.close();
    }

    private ConduitApiClient platformClient() {
        ConduitApiClient api = new ConduitApiClient(baseUrl);
        api.login("sysadmin", "platform-pass-1");
        return api;
    }

    @Test
    void discoversOnlySelectedActiveTenantsEnabledDevicesAndSupportedProducts() {
        List<TenantDiscovery.DiscoveredProduct> products = TenantDiscovery.discover(platformClient(),
                TenantFilter.parse("east,off"), 2, Set.of("pk-demo"));

        assertThat(products).singleElement().satisfies(p -> {
            assertThat(p.tenantCode()).isEqualTo("east");
            assertThat(p.productKey()).isEqualTo("pk-meter");
            assertThat(p.productName()).isEqualTo("10kV 进线电表");
            assertThat(p.format()).isEqualTo(PayloadFormat.FLAT);
            assertThat(p.devices()).extracting(DeviceCredentials::deviceKey).containsExactly("d-1", "d-3");
            assertThat(p.devices()).extracting(DeviceCredentials::secret).containsExactly("s-d-1", "s-d-3");
            assertThat(p.devices()).allSatisfy(d -> assertThat(d.productKey()).isEqualTo("pk-meter"));
            assertThat(p.properties()).hasSize(3);
            PropertyDef ua = p.properties().getFirst();
            assertThat(ua.unit()).isEqualTo("V");
            assertThat(ua.minValue()).isEqualTo(0.0);
            assertThat(ua.maxValue()).isEqualTo(300.0);
            assertThat(ua.decimals()).isEqualTo(1);
            assertThat(p.properties().get(1).enumItems())
                    .containsExactly(new PropertyDef.EnumOption(0, "停机"), new PropertyDef.EnumOption(1, "运行"));
            assertThat(p.properties().get(2).enumItems()).isEmpty();
        });
        assertThat(calls).contains("POST /api/v1/platform/tenants/east/enter", "POST /api/v1/auth/exit-tenant")
                .doesNotContain("POST /api/v1/platform/tenants/off/enter",
                        "POST /api/v1/platform/tenants/west/enter",
                        "GET /api/v1/products/pk-empty/properties",
                        "GET /api/v1/products/pk-demo/properties",
                        "GET /api/v1/products/pk-binary/properties",
                        "POST /api/v1/devices/d-2/reset-secret",
                        "POST /api/v1/devices/d-4/reset-secret");
        assertThat(queries).contains("GET /api/v1/platform/tenants?status=ACTIVE&page=1&size=100");
        assertThat(queries).anySatisfy(q -> assertThat(q).startsWith("GET /api/v1/devices?")
                .contains("productKey=pk-meter").contains("status=ENABLED"));
        assertThat(authorized).contains("GET /api/v1/products Bearer imp-east",
                "GET /api/v1/platform/tenants Bearer tok-platform");
    }

    @Test
    void aFailingTenantIsSkippedAndTheOthersContinue() {
        westProductsFail = true;

        List<TenantDiscovery.DiscoveredProduct> products = TenantDiscovery.discover(platformClient(),
                TenantFilter.ALL, 100, Set.of("pk-demo"));

        assertThat(products).extracting(TenantDiscovery.DiscoveredProduct::productKey).containsExactly("pk-meter");
        assertThat(products.getFirst().devices()).hasSize(3);
        assertThat(calls).contains("POST /api/v1/platform/tenants/west/enter")
                .doesNotContain("POST /api/v1/platform/tenants/off/enter");
        assertThat(calls).filteredOn("POST /api/v1/auth/exit-tenant"::equals).hasSize(2);
    }

    @Test
    void thePlannerSkipsDiscoveryWithoutAPlatformPassword() {
        SimulatorConfig config = SimulatorConfig.fromEnv(Map.of("CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD", "pw",
                "CONDUIT_API_URL", baseUrl.toString()));

        List<SimulatedDevice> devices = SimulationPlanner.tenantDevices(config, () -> {
            throw new AssertionError("no platform client may be created without SIM_PLATFORM_PASSWORD");
        }, Set.of());

        assertThat(devices).isEmpty();
        assertThat(calls).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void thePlannerBuildsThingModelDevicesForTheSelectedTenants() {
        SimulatorConfig config = SimulatorConfig.fromEnv(Map.of("CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD", "pw",
                "CONDUIT_API_URL", baseUrl.toString(),
                "SIM_PLATFORM_PASSWORD", "platform-pass-1",
                "SIM_TENANTS", "west"));

        List<SimulatedDevice> devices = SimulationPlanner.tenantDevices(config,
                () -> new ConduitApiClient(baseUrl), Set.of());

        assertThat(devices).singleElement().satisfies(d -> {
            assertThat(d.credentials().deviceKey()).isEqualTo("pv-1");
            assertThat(d.credentials().productKey()).isEqualTo("pk-pv");
            assertThat(d.format()).isEqualTo(PayloadFormat.STANDARD);
            assertThat(d.readings().next(System.currentTimeMillis(), 10)).containsOnlyKeys("Ppv");
        });
        assertThat(bodies).anySatisfy(b -> assertThat(b).startsWith("POST /api/v1/auth/login ")
                .contains("\"username\":\"sysadmin\""));
    }

    @Test
    void thePlannerFallsBackToTheDemoProductWhenThePlatformLoginFails() {
        server.removeContext("/");
        server.createContext("/", ex -> {
            byte[] bytes = "{\"detail\":\"用户名或密码错误\"}".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            ex.sendResponseHeaders(401, bytes.length);
            ex.getResponseBody().write(bytes);
            ex.close();
        });
        SimulatorConfig config = SimulatorConfig.fromEnv(Map.of("CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD", "pw",
                "CONDUIT_API_URL", baseUrl.toString(), "SIM_PLATFORM_PASSWORD", "wrong-pass-1"));

        assertThat(SimulationPlanner.tenantDevices(config, () -> new ConduitApiClient(baseUrl), Set.of())).isEmpty();
    }
}
```

Replace `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/ConduitApiClient.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.UncheckedIOException;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.URLEncoder;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * Conduit REST 的最小客户端，只覆盖模拟器与端到端测试需要的接口。登录后的令牌保存在实例中；
 * 平台管理员进入租户后令牌换成代入令牌，退出后换回平台令牌，因此一个实例同一时刻只代表一个身份。
 */
public class ConduitApiClient {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ConduitApiClient.class);
    private static final int PAGE_SIZE = 100;

    private final URI baseUrl;
    private final HttpClient http = HttpClient.newBuilder().connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5)).build();
    private final ObjectMapper mapper = new ObjectMapper();
    private String token;

    public ConduitApiClient(URI baseUrl) {
        this.baseUrl = baseUrl;
    }

    /** 登录并保存 accessToken，之后的请求自动带 Bearer 头；状态码非 200 抛 IllegalStateException。 */
    public void login(String username, String password) {
        JsonNode body = send("POST", "/api/v1/auth/login", Map.of("username", username, "password", password), 200);
        token = body.get("accessToken").asText();
    }

    /** 同 {@link #findOrCreateProduct(String, String)}，新建时使用 standard-json 解码器。 */
    public String findOrCreateProduct(String name) {
        return findOrCreateProduct(name, "standard-json");
    }

    /** 按名称复用产品；新建时使用给定解码器。已有产品的解码器不同时只告警，不修改。 */
    public String findOrCreateProduct(String name, String codec) {
        for (int page = 1; ; page++) {
            JsonNode result = send("GET", "/api/v1/products?page=" + page + "&size=" + PAGE_SIZE, null, 200);
            for (JsonNode item : result.get("items")) {
                if (name.equals(item.get("name").asText())) {
                    String existing = item.path("codec").asText("");
                    if (!existing.isEmpty() && !existing.equals(codec)) {
                        log.warn("Product '{}' uses codec {} but the simulator sends {}", name, existing, codec);
                    }
                    log.debug("Product '{}' found: productKey={} codec={}", name, item.get("productKey").asText(),
                            existing);
                    return item.get("productKey").asText();
                }
            }
            if ((long) page * PAGE_SIZE >= result.get("total").asLong()) {
                break;
            }
        }
        String productKey = send("POST", "/api/v1/products", Map.of("name", name, "codec", codec), 201)
                .get("productKey").asText();
        log.debug("Product '{}' created: productKey={} codec={}", name, productKey, codec);
        return productKey;
    }

    /**
     * 产品已定义的属性标识。
     *
     * @return 按接口返回顺序的标识集合；产品没有物模型时为空
     */
    public Set<String> propertyIdentifiers(String productKey) {
        JsonNode list = send("GET", "/api/v1/products/" + encode(productKey) + "/properties", null, 200);
        Set<String> ids = new LinkedHashSet<>();
        list.forEach(p -> ids.add(p.get("identifier").asText()));
        return ids;
    }

    /** 导入物模型文档；mode 为服务端支持的导入模式（如 merge）。 */
    public void importThingModel(String productKey, JsonNode document, String mode) {
        send("PUT", "/api/v1/products/" + encode(productKey) + "/thing-model?mode=" + encode(mode), document, 200);
        log.debug("Thing model imported into {} (mode={}, properties={})", productKey, mode,
                document.path("properties").size());
    }

    /** 设备列表中模拟器关心的字段。 */
    public record DeviceSummary(String deviceKey, String name, String status) {
    }

    /** 租户列表中模拟器关心的字段。 */
    public record TenantSummary(String code, String name, String status) {
    }

    /** 产品列表中模拟器关心的字段；propertyCount 为物模型属性数。 */
    public record ProductSummary(String productKey, String name, String codec, int propertyCount) {
    }

    public List<String> listDeviceKeys(String productKey) {
        return listDevices(productKey).stream().map(DeviceSummary::deviceKey).toList();
    }

    /** 分页拉取产品下全部设备（每页 100 条，直到取完）。 */
    public List<DeviceSummary> listDevices(String productKey) {
        List<DeviceSummary> devices = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int page = 1; ; page++) {
            JsonNode result = send("GET", "/api/v1/devices?productKey=" + encode(productKey)
                    + "&page=" + page + "&size=" + PAGE_SIZE, null, 200);
            result.get("items").forEach(item -> devices.add(summary(item)));
            if ((long) page * PAGE_SIZE >= result.get("total").asLong()) {
                return devices;
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 产品下最多 limit 台启用中的设备：请求带 status=ENABLED，并按返回项的 status 再过滤一次；取够即停止翻页。
     */
    public List<DeviceSummary> listEnabledDevices(String productKey, int limit) {
        List<DeviceSummary> devices = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int page = 1; devices.size() < limit; page++) {
            JsonNode result = send("GET", "/api/v1/devices?productKey=" + encode(productKey)
                    + "&status=ENABLED&page=" + page + "&size=" + PAGE_SIZE, null, 200);
            for (JsonNode item : result.get("items")) {
                DeviceSummary device = summary(item);
                if (devices.size() < limit && "ENABLED".equals(device.status())) {
                    devices.add(device);
                }
            }
            if ((long) page * PAGE_SIZE >= result.get("total").asLong()) {
                break;
            }
        }
        return devices;
    }

    /** 全部启用中的租户（平台管理员）：请求带 status=ACTIVE，并按返回项的 status 再过滤一次。 */
    public List<TenantSummary> listActiveTenants() {
        List<TenantSummary> tenants = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int page = 1; ; page++) {
            JsonNode result = send("GET", "/api/v1/platform/tenants?status=ACTIVE&page=" + page + "&size="
                    + PAGE_SIZE, null, 200);
            for (JsonNode item : result.get("items")) {
                String status = item.path("status").asText("");
                if ("ACTIVE".equals(status)) {
                    tenants.add(new TenantSummary(item.get("code").asText(), item.path("name").asText(""), status));
                }
            }
            if ((long) page * PAGE_SIZE >= result.get("total").asLong()) {
                return tenants;
            }
        }
    }

    /** 平台管理员进入租户：之后的请求使用代入令牌（以租户管理员身份操作该租户）。 */
    public void enterTenant(String code) {
        token = send("POST", "/api/v1/platform/tenants/" + encode(code) + "/enter", null, 200)
                .get("accessToken").asText();
        log.debug("Entered tenant {}", code);
    }

    /** 退出代入的租户：之后的请求换回平台管理员自己的令牌。 */
    public void exitTenant() {
        token = send("POST", "/api/v1/auth/exit-tenant", null, 200).get("accessToken").asText();
        log.debug("Exited the impersonated tenant");
    }

    /** 当前租户的全部产品（分页取完）。 */
    public List<ProductSummary> listProducts() {
        List<ProductSummary> products = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int page = 1; ; page++) {
            JsonNode result = send("GET", "/api/v1/products?page=" + page + "&size=" + PAGE_SIZE, null, 200);
            result.get("items").forEach(item -> products.add(new ProductSummary(item.get("productKey").asText(),
                    item.path("name").asText(""), item.path("codec").asText(""), item.path("propertyCount").asInt())));
            if ((long) page * PAGE_SIZE >= result.get("total").asLong()) {
                return products;
            }
        }
    }

    /** 产品物模型的属性定义（类型、单位、上下限、小数位、枚举项），按接口返回顺序。 */
    public List<PropertyDef> properties(String productKey) {
        JsonNode list = send("GET", "/api/v1/products/" + encode(productKey) + "/properties", null, 200);
        List<PropertyDef> defs = new ArrayList<>();
        for (JsonNode p : list) {
            List<PropertyDef.EnumOption> items = new ArrayList<>();
            p.path("enumItems").forEach(e -> items.add(
                    new PropertyDef.EnumOption(e.get("value").asLong(), e.path("label").asText(""))));
            defs.add(new PropertyDef(p.get("identifier").asText(), p.path("name").asText(""),
                    p.path("dataType").asText(""), textOrNull(p, "unit"), doubleOrNull(p, "minValue"),
                    doubleOrNull(p, "maxValue"), intOrNull(p, "decimals"), items));
        }
        return defs;
    }

    /** 修改设备名称；接口要求同时提交状态，传入当前状态以保持不变。 */
    public void renameDevice(String deviceKey, String name, String status) {
        send("PUT", "/api/v1/devices/" + encode(deviceKey), Map.of("name", name, "status", status), 200);
        log.debug("Device {} renamed to '{}'", deviceKey, name);
    }

    public void deleteDevice(String deviceKey) {
        send("DELETE", "/api/v1/devices/" + encode(deviceKey), null, 204);
    }

    public void deleteProduct(String productKey) {
        send("DELETE", "/api/v1/products/" + encode(productKey), null, 204);
    }

    /** 创建设备；明文密钥只在此刻返回一次，调用方需自行保存。 */
    public DeviceCredentials createDevice(String productKey, String name) {
        JsonNode body = send("POST", "/api/v1/devices", Map.of("productKey", productKey, "name", name), 201);
        String deviceKey = body.get("device").get("deviceKey").asText();
        log.debug("Device created: deviceKey={} name='{}' (productKey={})", deviceKey, name, productKey);
        return new DeviceCredentials(productKey, deviceKey, body.get("deviceSecret").asText());
    }

    /** 重置设备密钥并返回新的明文密钥（旧密钥立即失效）。 */
    public DeviceCredentials resetSecret(String productKey, String deviceKey) {
        JsonNode body = send("POST", "/api/v1/devices/" + encode(deviceKey) + "/reset-secret", null, 200);
        log.debug("Device secret reset: deviceKey={}", deviceKey);
        return new DeviceCredentials(productKey, deviceKey, body.get("deviceSecret").asText());
    }

    private static DeviceSummary summary(JsonNode item) {
        return new DeviceSummary(item.get("deviceKey").asText(), item.path("name").asText(""),
                item.path("status").asText("ENABLED"));
    }

    private static String textOrNull(JsonNode node, String field) {
        JsonNode value = node.get(field);
        return value == null || value.isNull() ? null : value.asText();
    }

    private static Double doubleOrNull(JsonNode node, String field) {
        JsonNode value = node.get(field);
        return value == null || value.isNull() ? null : value.asDouble();
    }

    private static Integer intOrNull(JsonNode node, String field) {
        JsonNode value = node.get(field);
        return value == null || value.isNull() ? null : value.asInt();
    }

    /**
     * 发送 JSON 请求并解析响应体。状态码与 expectedStatus 不符时抛 IllegalStateException（含响应体）；
     * 204 之类空响应体由调用方忽略返回值。
     */
    private JsonNode send(String method, String path, Object body, int expectedStatus) {
        try {
            HttpRequest.Builder request = HttpRequest.newBuilder(baseUrl.resolve(path))
                    .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10))
                    .header("Content-Type", "application/json");
            if (token != null) {
                request.header("Authorization", "Bearer " + token);
            }
            request.method(method, body == null
                    ? HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.noBody()
                    : HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofByteArray(mapper.writeValueAsBytes(body)));

            HttpResponse<byte[]> response = http.send(request.build(), HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofByteArray());
            log.debug("REST {} {} -> {}", method, path, response.statusCode());
            if (response.statusCode() != expectedStatus) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(method + " " + path + " returned " + response.statusCode()
                        + ": " + new String(response.body(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            }
            return mapper.readTree(response.body());
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new IllegalStateException(e);
        }
    }

    private static String encode(String value) {
        return URLEncoder.encode(value, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/SimulatedDevice.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 一台要模拟的设备：连接凭据、上报格式（按产品解码器）与读数来源（演示电表或按物模型生成）。
 *
 * @param credentials MQTT 凭据（含产品 Key，用于拼 topic）
 * @param format      上报载荷格式
 * @param readings    每次上报的读数来源
 */
public record SimulatedDevice(DeviceCredentials credentials, PayloadFormat format, ReadingSource readings) {

    /**
     * 演示产品的设备：三相电表读数（{@link ReadingGenerator}，种子为 deviceKey 的哈希），
     * operatingState 为 true 时附带运行状态 st（见 {@link OperatingState}）。
     */
    public static SimulatedDevice demo(DeviceCredentials credentials, PayloadFormat format, boolean operatingState) {
        ReadingGenerator generator = new ReadingGenerator(credentials.deviceKey().hashCode());
        return new SimulatedDevice(credentials, format, (ts, intervalSeconds) -> {
            Map<String, Object> reading = generator.next(intervalSeconds);
            if (operatingState) {
                reading.put(OperatingState.IDENTIFIER, OperatingState.at(credentials.deviceKey(), ts));
            }
            return reading;
        });
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/TenantDiscovery.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import dev.conduit.simulator.ConduitApiClient.DeviceSummary;
import dev.conduit.simulator.ConduitApiClient.ProductSummary;
import dev.conduit.simulator.ConduitApiClient.TenantSummary;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * 多租户发现（V0.2-R spec 5.1，启动时一次）：平台管理员列出启用中的租户，按 SIM_TENANTS 过滤后逐个进入，
 * 取物模型非空、解码器可生成的产品及其启用设备（每产品最多 maxDevicesPerProduct 台），重置这些设备的密钥，再退出租户。
 * 任一租户或产品失败只记 WARN，跳过它继续处理其他的。
 */
public final class TenantDiscovery {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(TenantDiscovery.class);

    private TenantDiscovery() {
    }

    /**
     * 发现到的一个租户产品。
     *
     * @param format     按产品解码器选定的上报格式
     * @param properties 产品物模型的属性定义
     * @param devices    已重置密钥的设备凭据
     */
    public record DiscoveredProduct(String tenantCode, String productKey, String productName, PayloadFormat format,
                                    List<PropertyDef> properties, List<DeviceCredentials> devices) {
    }

    /**
     * 发现要模拟的租户产品。
     *
     * @param platform             已用平台管理员登录的客户端（发现过程中会进入、退出租户）
     * @param filter               模拟哪些租户
     * @param maxDevicesPerProduct 每产品最多模拟的设备数
     * @param skipProductKeys      跳过的产品（演示产品，由 DemoProvisioner 处理）
     * @return 至少有一台设备的产品，按租户与产品的列表顺序
     */
    public static List<DiscoveredProduct> discover(ConduitApiClient platform, TenantFilter filter,
                                                   int maxDevicesPerProduct, Set<String> skipProductKeys) {
        List<DiscoveredProduct> result = new ArrayList<>();
        for (TenantSummary tenant : platform.listActiveTenants()) {
            if (!filter.includes(tenant.code())) {
                log.debug("Tenant {} is not in SIM_TENANTS, skipped", tenant.code());
                continue;
            }
            try {
                platform.enterTenant(tenant.code());
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                log.warn("Cannot enter tenant {} ({}), skipping it: {}", tenant.code(), tenant.name(), e.getMessage());
                continue;
            }
            try {
                List<DiscoveredProduct> products = discoverTenant(platform, tenant, maxDevicesPerProduct,
                        skipProductKeys);
                result.addAll(products);
                log.info("Tenant {} ({}): {} products with {} devices to simulate", tenant.code(), tenant.name(),
                        products.size(), products.stream().mapToInt(p -> p.devices().size()).sum());
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                log.warn("Discovery failed in tenant {} ({}), skipping it: {}", tenant.code(), tenant.name(),
                        e.getMessage());
            } finally {
                try {
                    platform.exitTenant();
                } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                    log.warn("Cannot exit tenant {}: {}", tenant.code(), e.getMessage());
                }
            }
        }
        return result;
    }

    /** 在已进入的租户内发现产品；单个产品失败只记 WARN 并跳过。 */
    private static List<DiscoveredProduct> discoverTenant(ConduitApiClient platform, TenantSummary tenant,
                                                          int maxDevicesPerProduct, Set<String> skipProductKeys) {
        List<DiscoveredProduct> products = new ArrayList<>();
        for (ProductSummary product : platform.listProducts()) {
            if (skipProductKeys.contains(product.productKey())) {
                log.debug("Product {} is the demo product, simulated by the demo flow", product.productKey());
                continue;
            }
            if (product.propertyCount() == 0) {
                log.debug("Product {} ({}) has no thing model, skipped", product.productKey(), product.name());
                continue;
            }
            Optional<PayloadFormat> format = PayloadFormat.fromCodec(product.codec());
            if (format.isEmpty()) {
                log.warn("Product {} ({}) uses codec {} which the simulator cannot produce, skipped",
                        product.productKey(), product.name(), product.codec());
                continue;
            }
            try {
                List<PropertyDef> properties = platform.properties(product.productKey());
                if (properties.stream().noneMatch(PointClassifier::simulated)) {
                    log.debug("Product {} has no property the simulator can generate, skipped", product.productKey());
                    continue;
                }
                List<DeviceCredentials> devices = new ArrayList<>();
                for (DeviceSummary device : platform.listEnabledDevices(product.productKey(), maxDevicesPerProduct)) {
                    devices.add(platform.resetSecret(product.productKey(), device.deviceKey()));
                }
                if (devices.isEmpty()) {
                    log.debug("Product {} has no enabled device, skipped", product.productKey());
                    continue;
                }
                products.add(new DiscoveredProduct(tenant.code(), product.productKey(), product.name(), format.get(),
                        properties, devices));
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                log.warn("Skipping product {} ({}) in tenant {}: {}", product.productKey(), product.name(),
                        tenant.code(), e.getMessage());
            }
        }
        return products;
    }
}
```

Create `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/SimulationPlanner.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

/**
 * 组装各租户产品的模拟设备：平台管理员密码为空时什么也不做（只模拟演示产品）；否则登录平台管理员，
 * 经 {@link TenantDiscovery} 发现产品，为每台设备配上按物模型生成读数的 {@link ThingModelReadingSource}。
 */
public final class SimulationPlanner {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(SimulationPlanner.class);

    private SimulationPlanner() {
    }

    /**
     * 各租户产品的模拟设备。
     *
     * @param clients         创建平台管理员客户端（密码为空时不会调用）
     * @param demoProductKeys 演示产品 Key，发现时跳过
     * @return 设备列表；未开启多租户模拟或平台管理员登录失败时为空列表（登录失败记 WARN）
     */
    public static List<SimulatedDevice> tenantDevices(SimulatorConfig config, Supplier<ConduitApiClient> clients,
                                                      Set<String> demoProductKeys) {
        if (!config.multiTenant()) {
            log.info("SIM_PLATFORM_PASSWORD is empty: simulating only the demo product '{}'", config.productName());
            return List.of();
        }
        ConduitApiClient platform = clients.get();
        try {
            platform.login(config.platformUsername(), config.platformPassword());
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            log.warn("Platform admin {} cannot log in, simulating only the demo product: {}",
                    config.platformUsername(), e.getMessage());
            return List.of();
        }
        ZoneId zone = ZoneId.systemDefault();
        List<SimulatedDevice> devices = new ArrayList<>();
        for (TenantDiscovery.DiscoveredProduct product : TenantDiscovery.discover(platform, config.tenants(),
                config.maxDevicesPerProduct(), demoProductKeys)) {
            for (DeviceCredentials credentials : product.devices()) {
                devices.add(new SimulatedDevice(credentials, product.format(),
                        new ThingModelReadingSource(product.properties(), credentials.deviceKey(), zone)));
            }
            log.info("Simulating {} devices of product '{}' ({}) in tenant {} with {} payloads",
                    product.devices().size(), product.productName(), product.productKey(), product.tenantCode(),
                    product.format().codec());
        }
        return devices;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Simulate per-device formats and readings, and wire the main flow**

Replace `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/DeviceSimulator.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.MqttClient;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.datatypes.MqttQos;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.Mqtt5AsyncClient;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.message.publish.Mqtt5PublishResult;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.message.publish.puback.Mqtt5PubAck;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.ScheduledExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

/**
 * 每台设备一个 MQTT 连接，按固定周期以 QoS1 上报；各设备的首次上报在一个周期内均匀错开。
 * 每台设备有自己的上报格式与读数来源（{@link SimulatedDevice}）；首次连接失败时按 {@link ConnectRetry} 重试。
 */
public class DeviceSimulator {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(DeviceSimulator.class);
    private static final int MAX_PUBLISH_ATTEMPTS = 10;
    private static final long RETRY_DELAY_MS = 1_000;
    private static final long CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS = 5_000;
    static final Duration DEFAULT_COMPLETION_TIMEOUT = Duration.ofMinutes(10);

    private final String host;
    private final int port;
    private final Duration interval;
    private final int messagesPerDevice;
    private final Duration completionTimeout;
    private final PayloadFormat format;
    private final boolean operatingState;
    private ConnectRetry connectRetry = new ConnectRetry();

    public DeviceSimulator(String host, int port, Duration interval, int messagesPerDevice) {
        this(host, port, interval, messagesPerDevice, DEFAULT_COMPLETION_TIMEOUT, PayloadFormat.STANDARD, false);
    }

    public DeviceSimulator(String host, int port, Duration interval, int messagesPerDevice,
                           Duration completionTimeout) {
        this(host, port, interval, messagesPerDevice, completionTimeout, PayloadFormat.STANDARD, false);
    }

    public DeviceSimulator(String host, int port, Duration interval, int messagesPerDevice,
                           Duration completionTimeout, PayloadFormat format) {
        this(host, port, interval, messagesPerDevice, completionTimeout, format, false);
    }

    /**
     * 完整构造器。format 与 operatingState 只用于 {@link #run(List)}（演示电表）；{@link #runDevices(List)} 按每台设备自己的设置。
     *
     * @param messagesPerDevice 每台设备的上报条数，0 表示一直运行
     * @param operatingState    run(List) 时是否附带运行状态 st；只有产品物模型中 st 为 ENUM 时才应开启（列类型全表共享，
     *                          无物模型的产品会把 st 存成浮点列，与演示产品的整数列冲突）
     */
    public DeviceSimulator(String host, int port, Duration interval, int messagesPerDevice,
                           Duration completionTimeout, PayloadFormat format, boolean operatingState) {
        this.host = host;
        this.port = port;
        this.interval = interval;
        this.messagesPerDevice = messagesPerDevice;
        this.completionTimeout = completionTimeout;
        this.format = format;
        this.operatingState = operatingState;
    }

    /** 替换连接重试策略（测试用较短的间隔与次数）。 */
    DeviceSimulator withConnectRetry(ConnectRetry retry) {
        this.connectRetry = retry;
        return this;
    }

    /** 以演示电表读数模拟这些设备（构造时的 format 与 operatingState），语义同 {@link #runDevices(List)}。 */
    public void run(List<DeviceCredentials> devices) throws InterruptedException {
        runDevices(devices.stream().map(d -> SimulatedDevice.demo(d, format, operatingState)).toList());
    }

    /**
     * 连接所有设备并按周期上报，阻塞到结束。messagesPerDevice &gt; 0 时为有限模式：每台发完即结束，
     * 超过 completionTimeout 未完成或有消息最终发送失败则抛 IllegalStateException；否则无限运行直到被中断。
     * 每台设备的首次连接按 connectRetry 重试（默认间隔 2 秒、最多 15 次），仍连不上则抛异常；
     * 结束时（含异常）关闭线程池并断开所有连接。
     *
     * @throws IllegalStateException 某台设备重试后仍连不上、超时未完成或有消息最终发送失败
     */
    public void runDevices(List<SimulatedDevice> devices) throws InterruptedException {
        boolean finite = messagesPerDevice > 0;
        long intervalMs = interval.toMillis();
        AtomicInteger threadCounter = new AtomicInteger();
        ScheduledExecutorService scheduler = Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(4, r -> {
            Thread t = new Thread(r, "simulator-" + threadCounter.incrementAndGet());
            t.setDaemon(true);
            return t;
        });
        CountDownLatch finished = new CountDownLatch(finite ? devices.size() : 1);
        AtomicInteger failedMessages = new AtomicInteger();
        AtomicInteger doneMessages = new AtomicInteger();
        List<Mqtt5AsyncClient> connected = new ArrayList<>();

        try {
            List<Mqtt5AsyncClient> clients = new ArrayList<>();
            for (SimulatedDevice device : devices) {
                clients.add(connect(device.credentials(), connected));
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < devices.size(); i++) {
                SimulatedDevice device = devices.get(i);
                DeviceCredentials d = device.credentials();
                Mqtt5AsyncClient client = clients.get(i);
                String topic = "conduit/%s/%s/up/property".formatted(d.productKey(), d.deviceKey());
                AtomicInteger sent = new AtomicInteger();
                AtomicInteger completed = new AtomicInteger();
                AtomicLong lastTs = new AtomicLong();
                long initialDelay = intervalMs * i / Math.max(1, devices.size());

                Runnable onMessageDone = () -> {
                    doneMessages.incrementAndGet();
                    if (finite && completed.incrementAndGet() == messagesPerDevice) {
                        finished.countDown();
                    }
                };

                scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(() -> {
                    try {
                        if (finite && sent.get() >= messagesPerDevice) {
                            return;
                        }
                        int seq = sent.incrementAndGet();
                        long ts = Math.max(System.currentTimeMillis(), lastTs.get() + 1);
                        lastTs.set(ts);
                        Map<String, Object> reading = device.readings().next(ts, intervalMs / 1000.0);
                        byte[] payload = device.format().encode(d.deviceKey() + "-" + seq, ts, reading);
                        log.debug("Publishing {} seq={} ts={} bytes={}", d.deviceKey(), seq, ts, payload.length);
                        if (log.isTraceEnabled()) {
                            String body = new String(payload, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                            log.trace("Payload of {}: {}", d.deviceKey(),
                                    body.length() <= 256 ? body : body.substring(0, 256) + "...(truncated)");
                        }
                        publish(scheduler, client, d.deviceKey(), topic, payload, 1, onMessageDone, failedMessages);
                    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                        log.error("Report task failed for {}", d.deviceKey(), e);
                        if (finite) {
                            failedMessages.incrementAndGet();
                            onMessageDone.run();
                        }
                    }
                }, initialDelay, intervalMs, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
            }

            log.info("Simulating {} devices every {} ms", devices.size(), intervalMs);
            if (finite) {
                boolean completedInTime = finished.await(completionTimeout.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
                if (!completedInTime) {
                    throw new IllegalStateException("Simulator did not finish within " + completionTimeout
                            + ": " + doneMessages.get() + " of " + (long) devices.size() * messagesPerDevice
                            + " messages done, " + failedMessages.get() + " failed");
                }
                if (failedMessages.get() > 0) {
                    throw new IllegalStateException(failedMessages.get() + " of "
                            + (long) devices.size() * messagesPerDevice + " messages failed to publish");
                }
            } else {
                finished.await();
            }
        } finally {
            scheduler.shutdownNow();
            for (Mqtt5AsyncClient c : connected) {
                disconnectQuietly(c);
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 连接一台设备，失败时按 connectRetry 重试。每次尝试新建客户端；失败的客户端立即断开（停止其自动重连），
     * 成功的加入 connected，由 runDevices 结束时统一断开。
     *
     * @throws IllegalStateException 重试次数用尽
     */
    private Mqtt5AsyncClient connect(DeviceCredentials d, List<Mqtt5AsyncClient> connected)
            throws InterruptedException {
        return connectRetry.call("device " + d.deviceKey() + " to " + host + ":" + port, () -> {
            Mqtt5AsyncClient client = buildClient(d);
            try {
                client.connect().get(CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                disconnectQuietly(client);
                throw e;
            }
            connected.add(client);
            log.debug("Device {} connected to {}:{}", d.deviceKey(), host, port);
            return client;
        });
    }

    /** 构造一台设备的 MQTT5 客户端（以 deviceKey 为用户名、密钥为密码，开启自动重连）。 */
    private Mqtt5AsyncClient buildClient(DeviceCredentials d) {
        return MqttClient.builder().useMqttVersion5()
                .identifier(d.deviceKey())
                .serverHost(host).serverPort(port)
                .automaticReconnectWithDefaultConfig()
                .simpleAuth().username(d.deviceKey()).password(d.secret().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
                .applySimpleAuth()
                .buildAsync();
    }

    /** 断开客户端；对从未连上的客户端也会停止其自动重连循环。断开本身的失败只记日志。 */
    private static void disconnectQuietly(Mqtt5AsyncClient client) {
        try {
            client.disconnect().join();
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            log.debug("Disconnect failed: {}", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    /**
     * 以 QoS1 发布一条消息；失败（异常或 PUBACK 错误码）时间隔 1 秒重试，最多 10 次，
     * 最终失败计入 failed。无论成败 onDone 恰好回调一次（调度器已关闭时除外）。
     */
    private void publish(ScheduledExecutorService scheduler, Mqtt5AsyncClient client, String deviceKey,
                         String topic, byte[] payload, int attempt, Runnable onDone, AtomicInteger failed) {
        client.publishWith().topic(topic).qos(MqttQos.AT_LEAST_ONCE).payload(payload).send()
                .whenComplete((result, error) -> {
                    String problem = error != null ? String.valueOf(error.getMessage()) : problemOf(result);
                    if (problem == null) {
                        onDone.run();
                        return;
                    }
                    log.warn("Publish failed for {} (attempt {}): {}", deviceKey, attempt, problem);
                    if (attempt >= MAX_PUBLISH_ATTEMPTS) {
                        failed.incrementAndGet();
                        onDone.run();
                        return;
                    }
                    try {
                        scheduler.schedule(() -> {
                            try {
                                publish(scheduler, client, deviceKey, topic, payload, attempt + 1, onDone, failed);
                            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                                log.error("Retry failed for {}", deviceKey, e);
                                failed.incrementAndGet();
                                onDone.run();
                            }
                        }, RETRY_DELAY_MS, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
                    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                        // scheduler already shut down: the run is over, nothing left to account for
                        log.debug("Retry not scheduled for {}: {}", deviceKey, e.getMessage());
                    }
                });
    }

    /** 从发布结果中提取失败原因；成功（含 result 为 null）返回 null。 */
    private static String problemOf(Mqtt5PublishResult result) {
        if (result == null) {
            return null;
        }
        if (result.getError().isPresent()) {
            return String.valueOf(result.getError().get().getMessage());
        }
        if (result instanceof Mqtt5PublishResult.Mqtt5Qos1Result qos1) {
            Mqtt5PubAck ack = qos1.getPubAck();
            if (ack.getReasonCode().isError()) {
                return "PUBACK " + ack.getReasonCode();
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}
```

Replace `conduit-simulator/src/main/java/dev/conduit/simulator/SimulatorApplication.java` with:

```java
package dev.conduit.simulator;

import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * 设备模拟器入口（演示/压测用，独立于平台进程）：通过 REST 准备演示产品、物模型与设备；配置了平台管理员密码时，
 * 再发现各租户有物模型的产品与启用设备（{@link SimulationPlanner}）；然后用各设备凭据经 MQTT 连接 EMQX 周期上报，
 * 驱动整条 设备 -> EMQX -> transport -> 总线 -> server 链路。
 */
public final class SimulatorApplication {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(SimulatorApplication.class);

    private SimulatorApplication() {
    }

    /**
     * 失败时显式 System.exit(1)：未连上的 HiveMQ 客户端的自动重连线程是非守护线程，
     * 仅抛出异常 JVM 不会退出，容器的 restart: on-failure 也就无从触发。
     */
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int status = 0;
        try {
            run();
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            status = 1;
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            log.error("Simulator failed", e);
            status = 1;
        }
        System.exit(status);
    }

    /**
     * 主流程：读配置、登录（等待 server 就绪）、准备演示设备、缺少演示属性时合并导入演示物模型、
     * 发现各租户的模拟设备，然后开始模拟上报。有限模式下返回即表示全部发送成功。
     */
    private static void run() throws InterruptedException {
        SimulatorConfig config = SimulatorConfig.load();
        ConduitApiClient api = new ConduitApiClient(config.apiUrl());
        loginWithRetry(api, config);

        PayloadFormat format = config.payloadFormat();
        List<DeviceCredentials> demo = DemoProvisioner.provision(api, config.productName(), config.deviceCount(),
                format.codec());
        log.info("Provisioned {} devices under product '{}'", demo.size(), config.productName());
        if (!demo.isEmpty() && DemoProvisioner.ensureThingModel(api, demo.getFirst().productKey())) {
            log.info("Imported demo thing model into product '{}'", config.productName());
        }

        // 演示产品的物模型含枚举 st，附带运行状态；e2e 等无物模型的产品不能发 st（见 DeviceSimulator 构造器说明）
        List<SimulatedDevice> devices = new ArrayList<>();
        demo.forEach(d -> devices.add(SimulatedDevice.demo(d, format, true)));
        Set<String> demoProductKeys = demo.isEmpty() ? Set.of() : Set.of(demo.getFirst().productKey());
        devices.addAll(SimulationPlanner.tenantDevices(config, () -> new ConduitApiClient(config.apiUrl()),
                demoProductKeys));

        new DeviceSimulator(config.mqttHost(), config.mqttPort(), config.interval(), config.messagesPerDevice(),
                DeviceSimulator.DEFAULT_COMPLETION_TIMEOUT, format, true).runDevices(devices);
    }

    /** 一键启动时 server 可能还没就绪，最多等 2 分钟。 */
    private static void loginWithRetry(ConduitApiClient api, SimulatorConfig config) throws InterruptedException {
        for (int attempt = 1; ; attempt++) {
            try {
                api.login(config.adminUsername(), config.adminPassword());
                return;
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                if (attempt >= 60) {
                    throw e;
                }
                log.info("Waiting for Conduit API ({}): {}", attempt, e.getMessage());
                Thread.sleep(2_000);
            }
        }
    }
}
```
