```java
    @Test
    void eachSimulatedDeviceUsesItsOwnFormatAndReadings() throws Exception {
        List<String> payloads = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
        Mqtt5BlockingClient subscriber = subscribe("it-subscriber-mixed", payloads);
        List<SimulatedDevice> devices = List.of(
                new SimulatedDevice(new DeviceCredentials("pk", "mixed-flat", "secret"), PayloadFormat.FLAT,
                        (ts, seconds) -> new LinkedHashMap<>(Map.of("Ppv", 0.0))),
                new SimulatedDevice(new DeviceCredentials("pk", "mixed-std", "secret"), PayloadFormat.STANDARD,
                        (ts, seconds) -> new LinkedHashMap<>(Map.of("flow", 12.5))));

        new DeviceSimulator(EMQX.getHost(), EMQX.getMappedPort(1883), Duration.ofMillis(50), 2,
                Duration.ofSeconds(30)).runDevices(devices);

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(10)).untilAsserted(() -> assertThat(payloads).hasSize(4));
        ObjectMapper mapper = new ObjectMapper();
        for (String payload : payloads) {
            JsonNode json = mapper.readTree(payload);
            if (json.get("id").asText().startsWith("mixed-flat-")) {
                assertThat(json.get("Ppv").asDouble()).isZero();
                assertThat(json.has("params")).isFalse();
            } else {
                assertThat(json.get("params").get("flow").asDouble()).isEqualTo(12.5);
            }
        }
        subscriber.disconnect();
    }
```

## Task 7: Self-contained e2e environment and the four failure scenarios (track C)

Replaces the e2e that needed the Docker `conduit-server` container with an environment the test builds and removes itself. Testcontainers provides PostgreSQL, Redis, InfluxDB and a durable-session EMQX on the Docker host, and local `java -jar` processes run one server and two transports. Scenarios: kill the server, kill the leader transport, kill both transports for about 20 s, restart EMQX. Each sends 500 QoS1 messages and asserts an exact InfluxDB count, no dead letters and no pending entries.

**Files:**
- Modify: `conduit-e2e/pom.xml` (full replacement)
- Create: `conduit-e2e/src/test/java/dev/conduit/e2e/EmqxConfig.java`
- Create: `conduit-e2e/src/test/java/dev/conduit/e2e/AppProcess.java`
- Create: `conduit-e2e/src/test/java/dev/conduit/e2e/E2eEnvironment.java`
- Create: `conduit-e2e/src/test/java/dev/conduit/e2e/E2eDevice.java`
- Create: `conduit-e2e/src/test/java/dev/conduit/e2e/DeviceFleet.java`
- Create: `conduit-e2e/src/test/java/dev/conduit/e2e/ReliabilityE2EIT.java`
- Delete: `conduit-e2e/src/test/java/dev/conduit/e2e/NoDataLossE2EIT.java`, `conduit-e2e/src/test/java/dev/conduit/e2e/DotEnv.java`
- Test: `conduit-e2e/src/test/java/dev/conduit/e2e/EmqxConfigTest.java` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - From conduit-simulator (unchanged by track B): `ConduitApiClient(URI)`, `login(String, String)`, `findOrCreateProduct(String, String) → String`, `createDevice(String productKey, String name) → DeviceCredentials`, `listDeviceKeys(String) → List<String>`, `deleteDevice(String)`, `deleteProduct(String)`; `DeviceCredentials(productKey, deviceKey, secret)`; `PayloadFormat.STANDARD.encode(String id, long ts, Map<String, Object>) → byte[]`.
  - The transport health contract from Global Constraints (`components.leader.details.role` / `connected`).
  - The jars `conduit-server/target/conduit-server.jar` and `conduit-transport/target/conduit-transport.jar`, built by `mvn -B -DskipTests install` before the run.
  - `deploy/emqx/base.hocon.tpl` and `deploy/emqx/acl.conf`.
- Produces: `mvn -B verify -Pe2e -pl conduit-e2e` runs `ReliabilityE2EIT`. It requires `E2E_DEV_HOST`, and Task 8 runs it.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- **Server configuration through environment variables.** Each process gets an explicit environment on top of the inherited one, with `SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE` removed:
  - `POSTGRES_HOST/PORT/DB/USER/PASSWORD`, `REDIS_HOST/PORT` and `INFLUX_URL`, all pointing at the mapped container ports on the Docker host;
  - `INFLUX_TOKEN`, `CONDUIT_ADMIN_USERNAME=admin`, `CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD`, `CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD=` (empty, so no platform admin), `CONDUIT_JWT_SECRET`, `CONDUIT_INTERNAL_TOKEN`, `TRANSPORT_MQTT_USERNAME=conduit-transport`, `TRANSPORT_MQTT_PASSWORD`, `PIPELINE_CLAIM_MIN_IDLE=10s` and `CONDUIT_LOG_DIR`.
  - The only command-line argument is `--server.port=<free local port>`.
  - Transports get `REDIS_HOST/PORT`, `EMQX_HOST/PORT`, `TRANSPORT_MQTT_USERNAME/PASSWORD`, `CONDUIT_LOG_DIR` and their own `--server.port`. They keep the default client id `conduit-transport` and the default leader timings (15 s / 5 s / 2 s).
- **Credentials are generated per run** with `SecureRandom`:
  - the PostgreSQL password, the InfluxDB token (`apiv3_` + 48 hex digits), the admin password (`E2e-` + 24 hex digits + `-9`, which satisfies the password policy), the JWT secret (64 hex digits), the internal token and the transport password;
  - they live only in memory and in the child process environments. No value is logged or written to a tracked file. The EMQX `base.hocon` with the internal token exists only inside the temporary container.
- **EMQX** is rendered from the real `deploy/emqx/base.hocon.tpl`. The auth URL is `http://<E2E_DEV_HOST>:<server port>`. The ACL is copied from `deploy/emqx/acl.conf`. The environment sets durable sessions, singleton discovery and node name `emqx@127.0.0.1`, the same as compose.
- **EMQX gets a fixed host port**, picked at random in 20000–29999 and retried up to 5 times on bind failure. Docker may assign a new random host port when a container is restarted, and the transports and devices must find EMQX again after `docker restart`. The restart uses the Docker API (`restartContainerCmd`, the equivalent of `docker restart`), so no local docker CLI is needed.
- **Devices** are played by `E2eDevice`. It is a plain MQTT 5 client without auto-reconnect. It publishes QoS1 and waits up to 10 s for the PUBACK. On an exception, a timeout or a PUBACK error code, such as `NOT_AUTHORIZED` right after an EMQX restart, it reconnects and resends the same payload with the same `ts`. It gives up after 5 minutes. Each device sends 50 messages at 500 ms intervals with strictly increasing `ts`, so a scenario lasts at least 25 s and a re-send overwrites instead of adding.
- **Assertions per scenario:**
  - `COUNT("Ua")` for the product equals exactly 500;
  - `XRANGE conduit:stream:dead` does not contain the product key;
  - `XPENDING conduit:stream:up conduit-server` reports 0. This is stricter than "none of this product", and safe because the environment belongs to this run only.
  - Each scenario deletes its devices and product in `finally`.
- **Process kill** is `Process.destroyForcibly()`, which on Windows is `TerminateProcess` and so a real crash: no lock release and no MQTT DISCONNECT.
- Logs go to `conduit-e2e/target/e2e-logs/<timestamp>/<process>/`: `console.log` plus the service's rolling log. The directory is printed at start and at the end.
- `conduit.root` (the repository root) comes from a surefire/failsafe system property (`${project.basedir}/..`). The test no longer depends on the working directory, which closes a backlog item.

```java
package dev.conduit.e2e;

/** 渲染 deploy/emqx/base.hocon.tpl（与 compose 中 EMQX 容器启动时的 sed 替换相同的两个占位符）。 */
final class EmqxConfig {

    static final String AUTH_URL = "__CONDUIT_AUTH_URL__";
    static final String INTERNAL_TOKEN = "__CONDUIT_INTERNAL_TOKEN__";

    private EmqxConfig() {
    }

    /**
     * 替换鉴权回调地址与内部 token。
     *
     * @throws IllegalArgumentException 模板缺少任一占位符（模板被改动，e2e 不能再假定其结构）
     */
    static String render(String template, String authUrl, String internalToken) {
        if (!template.contains(AUTH_URL) || !template.contains(INTERNAL_TOKEN)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("EMQX template must contain " + AUTH_URL + " and " + INTERNAL_TOKEN);
        }
        return template.replace(AUTH_URL, authUrl).replace(INTERNAL_TOKEN, internalToken);
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.e2e;

import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

/**
 * 测试机上的一个 java -jar 进程（server 或 transport）：可启动、强制结束（模拟崩溃）、再启动，并对其 HTTP 端口发 GET。
 * 标准输出与错误追加到工作目录下的 console.log；环境变量在继承的基础上覆盖，并去掉 SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE。
 */
final class AppProcess {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(AppProcess.class);
    private static final HttpClient HTTP = HttpClient.newBuilder().connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(2)).build();

    private final String name;
    private final Path jar;
    private final Map<String, String> env;
    private final int httpPort;
    private final Path workDir;
    private Process process;

    AppProcess(String name, Path jar, Map<String, String> env, int httpPort, Path workDir) {
        this.name = name;
        this.jar = jar;
        this.env = Map.copyOf(env);
        this.httpPort = httpPort;
        this.workDir = workDir;
    }

    String name() {
        return name;
    }

    Path consoleLog() {
        return workDir.resolve("console.log");
    }

    /** 启动进程（已在运行时什么也不做）；不等待就绪，由调用方轮询健康检查。 */
    synchronized void start() throws IOException {
        if (alive()) {
            return;
        }
        Files.createDirectories(workDir);
        String java = Path.of(System.getProperty("java.home"), "bin", "java").toString();
        List<String> command = new ArrayList<>(List.of(java, "-Xmx512m", "-jar", jar.toString(),
                "--server.port=" + httpPort));
        ProcessBuilder builder = new ProcessBuilder(command)
                .directory(workDir.toFile())
                .redirectErrorStream(true)
                .redirectOutput(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.appendTo(consoleLog().toFile()));
        builder.environment().remove("SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE");
        builder.environment().putAll(env);
        process = builder.start();
        log.info("Started {} (pid {}) on port {}", name, process.pid(), httpPort);
    }

    /** 强制结束（不执行关闭钩子，不释放锁、不发 MQTT DISCONNECT），最多等 30 秒退出。 */
    synchronized void kill() {
        if (process == null || !process.isAlive()) {
            return;
        }
        process.destroyForcibly();
        try {
            process.waitFor(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
        log.info("Killed {} (pid {})", name, process.pid());
    }

    synchronized boolean alive() {
        return process != null && process.isAlive();
    }

    /**
     * GET http://localhost:{port}{path}。
     *
     * @return 响应体（任何状态码）；连接失败、超时或进程未运行时为 null
     */
    String get(String path) {
        if (!alive()) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            HttpResponse<String> response = HTTP.send(HttpRequest.newBuilder(
                            URI.create("http://localhost:" + httpPort + path)).timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(3)).GET().build(),
                    HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            return response.body();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            return null;
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            return null;
        }
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.e2e;

import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.MqttClient;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.datatypes.MqttQos;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.Mqtt5AsyncClient;
import com.hivemq.client.mqtt.mqtt5.message.publish.Mqtt5PublishResult;
import dev.conduit.simulator.DeviceCredentials;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException;

/**
 * 测试代码扮演的一台真实设备：MQTT 5、不用自动重连，QoS1 发布后等待 PUBACK；异常、超时或 PUBACK 错误码时
 * 重新连接并重发同一载荷（同一 ts，平台按设备 + 时间戳覆盖，不会多计），直到成功，最长 5 分钟。
 */
final class E2eDevice implements AutoCloseable {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(E2eDevice.class);
    private static final Duration CONNECT_TIMEOUT = Duration.ofSeconds(5);
    private static final Duration PUBLISH_TIMEOUT = Duration.ofSeconds(10);
    private static final Duration RETRY_DELAY = Duration.ofMillis(500);
    private static final Duration GIVE_UP_AFTER = Duration.ofMinutes(5);

    private final String host;
    private final int port;
    private final DeviceCredentials credentials;
    private Mqtt5AsyncClient client;

    E2eDevice(String host, int port, DeviceCredentials credentials) {
        this.host = host;
        this.port = port;
        this.credentials = credentials;
    }

    String deviceKey() {
        return credentials.deviceKey();
    }

    String productKey() {
        return credentials.productKey();
    }

    /**
     * 发布一条 QoS1 消息直到收到成功的 PUBACK。
     *
     * @throws IllegalStateException 5 分钟内仍未成功
     */
    void publishUntilAcked(String topic, byte[] payload) throws InterruptedException {
        long deadline = System.nanoTime() + GIVE_UP_AFTER.toNanos();
        while (true) {
            if (System.nanoTime() > deadline) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("Device " + deviceKey() + " could not publish within " + GIVE_UP_AFTER);
            }
            try {
                ensureConnected();
                Mqtt5PublishResult result = client.publishWith().topic(topic).qos(MqttQos.AT_LEAST_ONCE)
                        .payload(payload).send().get(PUBLISH_TIMEOUT.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
                String problem = problemOf(result);
                if (problem == null) {
                    return;
                }
                log.debug("Device {} publish rejected ({}), reconnecting", deviceKey(), problem);
                dropConnection();
            } catch (ExecutionException | TimeoutException | RuntimeException e) {
                log.debug("Device {} publish failed ({}), retrying", deviceKey(), describe(e));
                if (client == null || !client.getState().isConnected()) {
                    dropConnection();
                }
            }
            Thread.sleep(RETRY_DELAY.toMillis());
        }
    }

    /** 未连接时新建客户端并连接（deviceKey 为 clientId 与用户名，密钥为密码）；失败抛出，由调用方重试。 */
    private void ensureConnected() throws InterruptedException, ExecutionException, TimeoutException {
        if (client != null && client.getState().isConnected()) {
            return;
        }
        dropConnection();
        client = MqttClient.builder().useMqttVersion5()
                .identifier(deviceKey())
                .serverHost(host).serverPort(port)
                .simpleAuth().username(deviceKey()).password(credentials.secret().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
                .applySimpleAuth()
                .buildAsync();
        client.connect().get(CONNECT_TIMEOUT.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
    }

    /** 断开并丢弃当前客户端（忽略断开失败）。 */
    private void dropConnection() {
        Mqtt5AsyncClient c = client;
        client = null;
        if (c != null) {
            try {
                c.disconnect().get(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.trace("Disconnect of {} failed: {}", deviceKey(), describe(e));
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        dropConnection();
    }

    /** 发布结果中的失败原因；成功返回 null。 */
    private static String problemOf(Mqtt5PublishResult result) {
        if (result.getError().isPresent()) {
            return String.valueOf(result.getError().get().getMessage());
        }
        if (result instanceof Mqtt5PublishResult.Mqtt5Qos1Result qos1 && qos1.getPubAck().getReasonCode().isError()) {
            return "PUBACK " + qos1.getPubAck().getReasonCode();
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static String describe(Throwable e) {
        Throwable t = e instanceof ExecutionException && e.getCause() != null ? e.getCause() : e;
        return t.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + t.getMessage();
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.e2e;

import dev.conduit.simulator.DeviceCredentials;
import dev.conduit.simulator.PayloadFormat;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;

import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

/**
 * 一个场景的全部设备：每台设备一个线程，按固定间隔发送 messagesPerDevice 条标准 JSON 上报（测点 Ua，ts 严格递增），
 * 每条都等到 PUBACK 才算发出；记录已确认的条数，供场景在发送中途注入故障。
 */
final class DeviceFleet {

    private final List<E2eDevice> devices;
    private final int messagesPerDevice;
    private final Duration interval;
    private final AtomicInteger acked = new AtomicInteger();

    DeviceFleet(String host, int port, List<DeviceCredentials> credentials, int messagesPerDevice, Duration interval) {
        this.devices = credentials.stream().map(c -> new E2eDevice(host, port, c)).toList();
        this.messagesPerDevice = messagesPerDevice;
        this.interval = interval;
    }

    /** 启动全部设备；返回的 future 在所有设备发完时完成，任一设备放弃时以其异常完成。 */
    CompletableFuture<Void> start() {
        List<CompletableFuture<Void>> runs = new ArrayList<>();
        for (E2eDevice device : devices) {
            CompletableFuture<Void> run = new CompletableFuture<>();
            Thread.ofPlatform().name("e2e-" + device.deviceKey()).daemon().start(() -> {
                try {
                    send(device);
                    run.complete(null);
                } catch (Throwable t) {
                    run.completeExceptionally(t);
                } finally {
                    device.close();
                }
            });
            runs.add(run);
        }
        return CompletableFuture.allOf(runs.toArray(CompletableFuture[]::new));
    }

    /** 一台设备按计划时间发完全部消息；故障期间落后的消息在恢复后连续补发。 */
    private void send(E2eDevice device) throws InterruptedException {
        String topic = "conduit/%s/%s/up/property".formatted(device.productKey(), device.deviceKey());
        long lastTs = 0;
        long next = System.currentTimeMillis();
        for (int seq = 1; seq <= messagesPerDevice; seq++) {
            long ts = Math.max(System.currentTimeMillis(), lastTs + 1);
            lastTs = ts;
            Map<String, Object> params = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            params.put("Ua", 220 + seq / 100.0);
            device.publishUntilAcked(topic, PayloadFormat.STANDARD.encode(device.deviceKey() + "-" + seq, ts, params));
            acked.incrementAndGet();
            next += interval.toMillis();
            long wait = next - System.currentTimeMillis();
            if (wait > 0) {
                Thread.sleep(wait);
            }
        }
    }

    int acked() {
        return acked.get();
    }

    long total() {
        return (long) devices.size() * messagesPerDevice;
    }

    /** 等到至少 count 条已确认。 */
    void awaitAcked(int count, Duration timeout) {
        await().atMost(timeout).pollInterval(Duration.ofMillis(200)).until(() -> acked.get() >= count);
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.conduit.e2e;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import com.github.dockerjava.api.model.ExposedPort;
import com.github.dockerjava.api.model.PortBinding;
import com.github.dockerjava.api.model.Ports;
import dev.conduit.simulator.ConduitApiClient;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.testcontainers.DockerClientFactory;
import org.testcontainers.containers.Container.ExecResult;
import org.testcontainers.containers.GenericContainer;
import org.testcontainers.containers.wait.strategy.Wait;
import org.testcontainers.images.builder.Transferable;
import org.testcontainers.utility.DockerImageName;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.ServerSocket;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.URLEncoder;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HexFormat;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.ThreadLocalRandom;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

/**
 * e2e 的独立临时环境：Testcontainers 在 Docker 宿主机上起 PostgreSQL / Redis / InfluxDB / EMQX（磁盘持久会话、单节点，
 * 鉴权回调指向 E2E_DEV_HOST 上的 server），测试机本地以 jar 启动 1 个 server 与 2 个 transport 连接它们。
 * 提供故障注入（强制结束进程、重启 EMQX 容器）与检查（InfluxDB 计数、死信、待确认数）。
 * 口令与 token 每次运行随机生成，只存在于内存与子进程环境变量中；结束时进程与容器全部清理。
 */
final class E2eEnvironment implements AutoCloseable {

    static final String ADMIN_USERNAME = "admin";
    static final Duration CLAIM_MIN_IDLE = Duration.ofSeconds(10);

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(E2eEnvironment.class);
    private static final SecureRandom RANDOM = new SecureRandom();
    private static final ObjectMapper JSON = new ObjectMapper();
    private static final int EMQX_MQTT_PORT = 1883;

    private final Path root = root();
    private final Path logDir = root.resolve("conduit-e2e/target/e2e-logs")
            .resolve(LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMdd-HHmmss")));
    private final String devHost;
    private final String postgresPassword = hex(16);
    private final String influxToken = "apiv3_" + hex(24);
    private final String adminPassword = "E2e-" + hex(12) + "-9";
    private final String jwtSecret = hex(32);
    private final String internalToken = hex(24);
    private final String transportPassword = hex(24);
    private final HttpClient http = HttpClient.newBuilder().connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5)).build();
    private final List<GenericContainer<?>> containers = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<AppProcess> transports = new ArrayList<>();

    private GenericContainer<?> postgres;
    private GenericContainer<?> redis;
    private GenericContainer<?> influx;
    private GenericContainer<?> emqx;
    private int emqxPort;
    private int serverPort;
    private AppProcess server;

    private E2eEnvironment(String devHost) {
        this.devHost = devHost;
    }

    /** 仓库根目录：system property conduit.root（由 surefire / failsafe 设置），缺省为上一级目录。 */
    static Path root() {
        return Path.of(System.getProperty("conduit.root", "..")).toAbsolutePath().normalize();
    }

    /**
     * 搭建整套环境并等到某个 transport 成为已连接的主实例。
     *
     * @throws IllegalStateException E2E_DEV_HOST 未设置、jar 未构建、或任一组件未能就绪
     */
    static E2eEnvironment start() throws Exception {
        String devHost = System.getenv("E2E_DEV_HOST");
        if (devHost == null || devHost.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("E2E_DEV_HOST must be set to the address at which the Docker host "
                    + "reaches this machine: EMQX calls the locally running server's /internal/mqtt/auth there");
        }
        E2eEnvironment env = new E2eEnvironment(devHost.trim());
        try {
            env.boot();
            return env;
        } catch (Exception | Error e) {
            env.close();
            throw e;
        }
    }

    private void boot() throws Exception {
        Path serverJar = requireJar("conduit-server/target/conduit-server.jar");
        Path transportJar = requireJar("conduit-transport/target/conduit-transport.jar");
        log.info("e2e logs go to {}", logDir);

        postgres = container(new GenericContainer<>(DockerImageName.parse("postgres:16.8"))
                .withExposedPorts(5432)
                .withEnv("POSTGRES_DB", "conduit")
                .withEnv("POSTGRES_USER", "conduit")
                .withEnv("POSTGRES_PASSWORD", postgresPassword)
                .waitingFor(Wait.forLogMessage(".*database system is ready to accept connections.*", 2)
                        .withStartupTimeout(Duration.ofMinutes(2))));
        redis = container(new GenericContainer<>(DockerImageName.parse("redis:7.4.2")).withExposedPorts(6379));
        byte[] tokenFile = ("{\"token\":\"" + influxToken + "\",\"name\":\"_admin\"}").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        influx = container(new GenericContainer<>(DockerImageName.parse("influxdb:3.12.0-core"))
                .withCopyToContainer(Transferable.of(tokenFile, 0644), "/tmp/admin-token.json")
                .withCommand("influxdb3", "serve", "--node-id", "node0", "--object-store", "memory",
                        "--admin-token-file", "/tmp/admin-token.json", "--disable-authz", "health")
                .withExposedPorts(8181)
                .waitingFor(Wait.forHttp("/health").forPort(8181).forStatusCode(200)
                        .withStartupTimeout(Duration.ofMinutes(2))));

        serverPort = freePort();
        emqx = startEmqx();

        Map<String, String> serverEnv = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        serverEnv.put("POSTGRES_HOST", postgres.getHost());
        serverEnv.put("POSTGRES_PORT", String.valueOf(postgres.getMappedPort(5432)));
        serverEnv.put("POSTGRES_DB", "conduit");
        serverEnv.put("POSTGRES_USER", "conduit");
        serverEnv.put("POSTGRES_PASSWORD", postgresPassword);
        serverEnv.put("REDIS_HOST", redis.getHost());
        serverEnv.put("REDIS_PORT", String.valueOf(redis.getMappedPort(6379)));
        serverEnv.put("INFLUX_URL", influxUrl());
        serverEnv.put("INFLUX_TOKEN", influxToken);
        serverEnv.put("CONDUIT_ADMIN_USERNAME", ADMIN_USERNAME);
        serverEnv.put("CONDUIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD", adminPassword);
        serverEnv.put("CONDUIT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_PASSWORD", "");
        serverEnv.put("CONDUIT_JWT_SECRET", jwtSecret);
        serverEnv.put("CONDUIT_INTERNAL_TOKEN", internalToken);
        serverEnv.put("TRANSPORT_MQTT_USERNAME", "conduit-transport");
        serverEnv.put("TRANSPORT_MQTT_PASSWORD", transportPassword);
        serverEnv.put("PIPELINE_CLAIM_MIN_IDLE", CLAIM_MIN_IDLE.toSeconds() + "s");
        serverEnv.put("CONDUIT_LOG_DIR", logDir.resolve("server/logs").toString());
        server = new AppProcess("server", serverJar, serverEnv, serverPort, logDir.resolve("server"));
        startServer();

        for (String name : List.of("transport-a", "transport-b")) {
            Map<String, String> transportEnv = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            transportEnv.put("REDIS_HOST", redis.getHost());
            transportEnv.put("REDIS_PORT", String.valueOf(redis.getMappedPort(6379)));
            transportEnv.put("EMQX_HOST", emqx.getHost());
            transportEnv.put("EMQX_PORT", String.valueOf(emqxPort));
            transportEnv.put("TRANSPORT_MQTT_USERNAME", "conduit-transport");
            transportEnv.put("TRANSPORT_MQTT_PASSWORD", transportPassword);
            transportEnv.put("CONDUIT_LOG_DIR", logDir.resolve(name + "/logs").toString());
            AppProcess transport = new AppProcess(name, transportJar, transportEnv, freePort(), logDir.resolve(name));
            transports.add(transport);
            transport.start();
        }
        awaitLeaderConnected(Duration.ofSeconds(90));
    }

    /**
     * 启动 EMQX：配置由部署模板渲染，持久会话与固定节点名同 compose；宿主机端口在 20000～29999 中随机固定
     * （docker restart 后端口不变），被占用时换端口重试，最多 5 次。
     */
    private GenericContainer<?> startEmqx() throws IOException {
        String hocon = EmqxConfig.render(Files.readString(root.resolve("deploy/emqx/base.hocon.tpl")),
                "http://" + devHost + ":" + serverPort, internalToken);
        byte[] acl = Files.readAllBytes(root.resolve("deploy/emqx/acl.conf"));
        RuntimeException last = null;
        for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= 5; attempt++) {
            int hostPort = 20000 + ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(10000);
            GenericContainer<?> c = new GenericContainer<>(DockerImageName.parse("emqx/emqx:5.8.6"))
                    .withExposedPorts(EMQX_MQTT_PORT)
                    .withCreateContainerCmdModifier(cmd -> cmd.getHostConfig().withPortBindings(
                            new PortBinding(Ports.Binding.bindPort(hostPort), new ExposedPort(EMQX_MQTT_PORT))))
                    .withCopyToContainer(Transferable.of(hocon.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)),
                            "/opt/emqx/etc/base.hocon")
                    .withCopyToContainer(Transferable.of(acl), "/opt/emqx/etc/conduit-acl.conf")
                    .withEnv("EMQX_DURABLE_SESSIONS__ENABLE", "true")
                    .withEnv("EMQX_CLUSTER__DISCOVERY_STRATEGY", "singleton")
                    .withEnv("EMQX_NODE__NAME", "emqx@127.0.0.1")
                    .waitingFor(Wait.forLogMessage(".*is running now.*", 1))
                    .withStartupTimeout(Duration.ofMinutes(2));
            try {
                c.start();
                emqxPort = hostPort;
                containers.add(c);
                log.info("EMQX is running on {}:{} with durable sessions", c.getHost(), hostPort);
                return c;
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                last = e;
                c.stop();
                log.warn("EMQX did not start on host port {} (attempt {}): {}", hostPort, attempt, e.getMessage());
            }
        }
        throw new IllegalStateException("EMQX did not start after 5 attempts", last);
    }

    private GenericContainer<?> container(GenericContainer<?> c) {
        c.start();
        containers.add(c);
        return c;
    }

    /** 已登录 admin（默认租户的租户管理员）的 REST 客户端；每次新建，避免 15 分钟的令牌过期。 */
    ConduitApiClient api() {
        ConduitApiClient api = new ConduitApiClient(URI.create("http://localhost:" + serverPort));
        api.login(ADMIN_USERNAME, adminPassword);
        return api;
    }

    String mqttHost() {
        return emqx.getHost();
    }

    int mqttPort() {
        return emqxPort;
    }

    /** 启动（或重启）server 并等到 readiness 为 UP，最多 3 分钟；进程提前退出时报错并指明日志。 */
    void startServer() throws IOException {
        server.start();
        await().atMost(Duration.ofMinutes(3)).pollInterval(Duration.ofSeconds(1)).until(() -> {
            if (!server.alive()) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("server exited during startup, see " + server.consoleLog());
            }
            String body = server.get("/actuator/health/readiness");
            return body != null && body.contains("\"UP\"");
        });
    }

    void killServer() {
        server.kill();
    }

    void kill(AppProcess process) {
        process.kill();
    }

    void killTransports() {
        transports.forEach(AppProcess::kill);
    }

    void startTransports() throws IOException {
        for (AppProcess transport : transports) {
            transport.start();
        }
    }

    /** 当前的主 transport（健康检查 role 为 LEADER 的进程）。 */
    AppProcess leaderTransport() {
        return findConnectedLeader().orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("no transport is a connected leader"));
    }

    /** 等到某个 transport 是已连接 EMQX 的主实例，返回它。 */
    AppProcess awaitLeaderConnected(Duration timeout) {
        AtomicReference<AppProcess> leader = new AtomicReference<>();
        await().atMost(timeout).pollInterval(Duration.ofSeconds(1)).until(() -> {
            Optional<AppProcess> found = findConnectedLeader();
            found.ifPresent(leader::set);
            return found.isPresent();
        });
        log.info("{} is the connected leader", leader.get().name());
        return leader.get();
    }

    private Optional<AppProcess> findConnectedLeader() {
        for (AppProcess transport : transports) {
            String body = transport.get("/actuator/health");
            if (body == null) {
                continue;
            }
            try {
                JsonNode details = JSON.readTree(body).path("components").path("leader").path("details");
                if ("LEADER".equals(details.path("role").asText()) && details.path("connected").asBoolean()) {
                    return Optional.of(transport);
                }
            } catch (IOException e) {
                log.debug("Unreadable health of {}: {}", transport.name(), e.getMessage());
            }
        }
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    /** 重启 EMQX 容器（等同 docker restart，容器、数据与固定端口不变），等到 emqx ctl status 成功。 */
    void restartEmqx() {
        log.info("Restarting the EMQX container");
        DockerClientFactory.instance().client().restartContainerCmd(emqx.getContainerId()).withTimeout(10).exec();
        await().atMost(Duration.ofMinutes(2)).pollInterval(Duration.ofSeconds(2)).until(() -> {
            try {
                ExecResult result = emqx.execInContainer("/opt/emqx/bin/emqx", "ctl", "status");
                return result.getExitCode() == 0;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                return false;
            }
        });
        log.info("EMQX is running again");
    }

    /** 每个场景开始前：把被结束的进程重新启动，并等到有已连接的主 transport。 */
    void ensureRunning() throws IOException {
        if (!server.alive()) {
            startServer();
        }
        startTransports();
        awaitLeaderConnected(Duration.ofSeconds(90));
    }

    /** InfluxDB 中该产品最近 1 小时的 Ua 点数；Ua 列还不存在（全新的库）时为 0。 */
    long influxCount(String productKey) throws Exception {
        JsonNode column = influxSql("SELECT data_type FROM information_schema.columns "
                + "WHERE table_name = 'property' AND column_name = 'Ua'");
        if (column.isEmpty()) {
            return 0;
        }
        JsonNode rows = influxSql("SELECT COUNT(\"Ua\") AS n FROM property WHERE product_key = '" + productKey
                + "' AND time >= now() - INTERVAL '1 hour'");
        return rows.isEmpty() ? 0 : rows.get(0).get("n").asLong();
    }

    private JsonNode influxSql(String query) throws Exception {
        URI uri = URI.create(influxUrl() + "/api/v3/query_sql?db=telemetry&format=json&q="
                + URLEncoder.encode(query, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        HttpResponse<String> response = http.send(HttpRequest.newBuilder(uri)
                        .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + influxToken)
                        .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10)).GET().build(),
                HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        if (response.statusCode() != 200) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("InfluxDB query failed with " + response.statusCode() + ": "
                    + response.body());
        }
        String body = response.body();
        return body == null || body.isBlank() ? JSON.createArrayNode() : JSON.readTree(body);
    }

    /** 死信流的全部内容（redis-cli 原样输出）。 */
    String deadLetters() throws Exception {
        return redis.execInContainer("redis-cli", "XRANGE", "conduit:stream:dead", "-", "+").getStdout();
    }

    /** 消费组 conduit-server 中待确认的条目数；读取失败（如消费组还不存在）时为 -1。 */
    long pendingCount() throws Exception {
        String out = redis.execInContainer("redis-cli", "XPENDING", "conduit:stream:up", "conduit-server")
                .getStdout().trim();
        String first = out.isEmpty() ? "" : out.lines().findFirst().orElse("").trim();
        try {
            return Long.parseLong(first);
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return -1;
        }
    }

    /** 结束全部进程并停止容器；单个失败只记 WARN。 */
    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (server != null) {
            server.kill();
        }
        transports.forEach(AppProcess::kill);
        for (GenericContainer<?> c : containers) {
            try {
                c.stop();
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                log.warn("Could not stop container {}: {}", c.getDockerImageName(), e.getMessage());
            }
        }
        log.info("e2e environment removed; logs are in {}", logDir);
    }

    private String influxUrl() {
        return "http://" + influx.getHost() + ":" + influx.getMappedPort(8181);
    }

    private Path requireJar(String relative) {
        Path jar = root.resolve(relative);
        if (!Files.isRegularFile(jar)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(jar + " is missing: run mvn -B -DskipTests install first");
        }
        return jar;
    }

    private static int freePort() throws IOException {
        try (ServerSocket socket = new ServerSocket(0)) {
            socket.setReuseAddress(true);
            return socket.getLocalPort();
        }
    }

    private static String hex(int bytes) {
        byte[] buffer = new byte[bytes];
        RANDOM.nextBytes(buffer);
        return HexFormat.of().formatHex(buffer);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Add the scenarios and remove the old e2e**

Create `conduit-e2e/src/test/java/dev/conduit/e2e/ReliabilityE2EIT.java`:

```java
package dev.conduit.e2e;

import dev.conduit.simulator.ConduitApiClient;
import dev.conduit.simulator.DeviceCredentials;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterAll;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.MethodOrderer.OrderAnnotation;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Order;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.TestInstance;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.TestMethodOrder;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

/**
 * V0.2-R 可靠性端到端测试（spec 第 4 节）：在独立临时环境中，10 台设备 × 50 条 QoS1 上报的发送中途分别
 * ①强制结束 server 数秒后重启；②强制结束主 transport；③两个 transport 都结束、设备继续上报约 20 秒后重启；④重启 EMQX 容器。
 * 每个场景断言：InfluxDB 中该产品恰好 500 条、死信流中没有该产品、消费组 conduit-server 没有待确认消息；结束时删除所建产品与设备。
 */
@TestInstance(TestInstance.Lifecycle.PER_CLASS)
@TestMethodOrder(OrderAnnotation.class)
class ReliabilityE2EIT {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ReliabilityE2EIT.class);
    static final int DEVICES = 10;
    static final int MESSAGES_PER_DEVICE = 50;
    static final Duration INTERVAL = Duration.ofMillis(500);

    /** 场景注入的故障。 */
    @FunctionalInterface
    interface Fault {
        void inject() throws Exception;
    }

    E2eEnvironment env;

    @BeforeAll
    void setUpEnvironment() throws Exception {
        env = E2eEnvironment.start();
    }

    @AfterAll
    void tearDownEnvironment() {
        if (env != null) {
            env.close();
        }
    }

    @BeforeEach
    void everyProcessIsRunning() throws Exception {
        env.ensureRunning();
    }

    @Test
    @Order(1)
    void killingTheServerMidStreamLosesNothing() throws Exception {
        runScenario(1, () -> {
            env.killServer();
            Thread.sleep(3_000);
            env.startServer();
        });
    }

    @Test
    @Order(2)
    void killingTheLeaderTransportLetsTheStandbyTakeOver() throws Exception {
        runScenario(2, () -> {
            AppProcess leader = env.leaderTransport();
            env.kill(leader);
            AppProcess successor = env.awaitLeaderConnected(Duration.ofSeconds(60));
            assertThat(successor).as("the standby takes over").isNotSameAs(leader);
        });
    }

    @Test
    @Order(3)
    void killingEveryTransportIsCoveredByTheDurableSession() throws Exception {
        runScenario(3, () -> {
            env.killTransports();
            Thread.sleep(20_000);
            env.startTransports();
            env.awaitLeaderConnected(Duration.ofSeconds(90));
        });
    }

    @Test
    @Order(4)
    void restartingEmqxKeepsTheSessionAndTheBacklog() throws Exception {
        runScenario(4, () -> {
            env.restartEmqx();
            env.awaitLeaderConnected(Duration.ofMinutes(2));
        });
    }

    /**
     * 一个场景：建产品「e2e 场景 N 电表」与 10 台设备，开始发送，确认 100 条后注入故障，等发送结束，再做三项断言；
     * 无论成败都删除本场景的设备与产品。
     */
    private void runScenario(int n, Fault fault) throws Exception {
        ConduitApiClient api = env.api();
        String productKey = api.findOrCreateProduct("e2e 场景 " + n + " 电表", "standard-json");
        try {
            List<DeviceCredentials> devices = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 1; i <= DEVICES; i++) {
                devices.add(api.createDevice(productKey, "e2e 场景%d 电表%02d".formatted(n, i)));
            }
            DeviceFleet fleet = new DeviceFleet(env.mqttHost(), env.mqttPort(), devices, MESSAGES_PER_DEVICE,
                    INTERVAL);
            CompletableFuture<Void> sending = fleet.start();
            fleet.awaitAcked(100, Duration.ofSeconds(60));
            assertThat(sending.isDone()).as("the fault must land mid-stream").isFalse();
            log.info("Scenario {}: injecting the fault after {} acknowledged messages", n, fleet.acked());

            fault.inject();
            sending.get(5, TimeUnit.MINUTES);
            log.info("Scenario {}: all {} messages acknowledged by EMQX", n, fleet.total());

            await().atMost(Duration.ofMinutes(3)).pollInterval(Duration.ofSeconds(3))
                    .untilAsserted(() -> assertThat(env.influxCount(productKey))
                            .as("points of scenario %d in InfluxDB", n).isEqualTo(fleet.total()));
            assertThat(env.deadLetters()).as("dead letters of scenario %d", n).doesNotContain(productKey);
            await().atMost(E2eEnvironment.CLAIM_MIN_IDLE.plusSeconds(60)).pollInterval(Duration.ofSeconds(3))
                    .untilAsserted(() -> assertThat(env.pendingCount())
                            .as("pending entries in group conduit-server").isZero());
            assertThat(env.influxCount(productKey)).as("no extra points after the backlog drained")
                    .isEqualTo(fleet.total());
        } finally {
            cleanUp(api, productKey);
        }
    }

    /** 删除本场景的设备与产品（产品下还有设备时服务端返回 409，所以先删设备）；失败只记 WARN，不掩盖测试结果。 */
    private static void cleanUp(ConduitApiClient api, String productKey) {
        try {
            for (String deviceKey : api.listDeviceKeys(productKey)) {
                api.deleteDevice(deviceKey);
            }
            api.deleteProduct(productKey);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            log.warn("Failed to clean up e2e product {}", productKey, e);
        }
    }
}
```

## Task 8: Merge, docs and configuration, full verification, e2e run and acceptance (main)

Runs on `main` after the three tracks are done. It merges them, syncs the docs (README, CLAUDE.md, roadmap, backlog, spec status) and the compose/env settings for the simulator, then runs the full build and the e2e. Finally it recreates the infrastructure EMQX and checks spec §7 against the owner's IDE-run stack.

**Files:**
- Modify: `README.md`
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md` (§1.1, §1.2, §3, §4)
- Modify: `deploy/docker-compose.yml` (simulator environment), `deploy/.env.example`
- Modify: `docs/backlog-v0.2.md`
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-conduit-v0.1-design.md` (roadmap row V0.2-R)
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-conduit-v0.2r-reliability-design.md` (status line, acceptance record)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above. Also the owner's IDE-run stack (`Conduit (local all)`): the server on `http://localhost:8080`, the transport on `http://localhost:8081`, local logs in `logs/`. And the infrastructure Compose stack on the Docker host, synced to `/opt/conduit`.
- Produces: merged `main`, synced docs and configuration, a green `mvn -B verify`, a green e2e run, and the spec §7 acceptance record.

**Decisions recorded here:**
- The only operation on the Docker host is recreating the infrastructure EMQX container, which spec §7 item 1 requires. The controller runs it with the commands in Step 10, using the same mechanism as before: `deploy/sync-to-host.sh`, then the host's `docker-compose` binary in a throwaway `eclipse-temurin` container. Nothing runs `deploy/build-local.sh`, and the app stack on the host stays down.
- The owner restarts `Conduit (local all)` after the merge. Before that, the owner removes `client-id` and `session-expiry-seconds` from the untracked `conduit-transport/src/main/resources/application-local.yml`, and adds `SIM_PLATFORM_PASSWORD` to the untracked `simulator-local.properties`. The controller checks for the keys without printing their values.
- Acceptance uses `curl` and a small Python script written to `$SCRATCH`. Nothing is written to tracked files. The roadmap row is marked 「（已完成，2026-10-16）」 in its 主题 cell.

4. Directly after the paragraph that ends with `完整接入流程见[设备接入指南](docs/device-integration.md)。` (before `## 代码结构`), insert:

```markdown
### transport 单活多备与磁盘持久会话

- EMQX 开启磁盘持久会话（`durable_sessions`，单节点 `singleton`）。transport 以固定 clientId `conduit-transport`、`cleanStart=false`、会话保留 86400 秒普通订阅 `conduit/+/+/up/#`；transport 离线期间的消息写入 EMQX 磁盘，保留 1 天，重连后补发，不受内存队列 1000 条的上限。EMQX 5.8.6 的磁盘持久会话不支持共享订阅，所以多个 transport 不再分摊消息，而是单活多备。
- 选主：Redis 键 `conduit:transport:leader`（值为实例 id），`SET NX PX` 抢锁；主实例每 5 秒续期，锁 15 秒过期，备实例每 2 秒尝试抢锁，只有主实例连接 EMQX。续期失败（Redis 不可用、锁已被他人持有、超时）立即断开 MQTT 转为备用；正常停止时释放锁，备实例立即接管；主实例崩溃时备实例最长 15 秒后接管同一个会话。配置项 `conduit.transport.leader.ttl` / `renew-interval` / `acquire-interval`。
- 健康检查：transport（端口 8081）的 `GET /actuator/health` 在 `components.leader.details` 中显示 `role`（`LEADER` / `STANDBY`）、`instanceId` 与 `connected`。
- 完整部署起两个 transport（一主一备）；开发时 IDE 中一个 transport，直接成为主实例。
```

8. Directly after the paragraph that starts with `已有部署升级注意：演示产品的默认名称已从` (before `宿主机端口可在`), insert:

```markdown
### 模拟器：演示产品与各租户的产品

模拟器始终维护默认租户的演示产品（见上）。配置平台管理员密码后，它在启动时用平台管理员登录，列出启用中的租户（按 `SIM_TENANTS` 过滤），逐个进入租户，取物模型非空的产品及其启用设备（每产品最多 `SIM_MAX_DEVICES_PER_PRODUCT` 台），重置这些设备的密钥后连接，并按产品解码器（`standard-json` / `flat-json`）上报；任一租户或产品失败只记 WARN，不影响其他。

| 变量 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `SIM_PLATFORM_USERNAME` | `sysadmin` | 平台管理员账号 |
| `SIM_PLATFORM_PASSWORD` | 空 | 平台管理员密码；为空时只模拟演示产品 |
| `SIM_TENANTS` | `*` | 逗号分隔的租户编码，`*` 表示全部启用中的租户 |
| `SIM_MAX_DEVICES_PER_PRODUCT` | `100` | 每个产品最多模拟的设备数 |

取值按物模型逐测点生成，满足上下限与小数位：
- 电压在 220 V 附近，三相略有差别；电流与用电功率按日用电曲线。
- 光伏功率与辐照度在 6～18 点按正弦日照，叠加云层扰动，夜间为 0。
- 电能、用水等累计量单调递增，增量与功率或流量相符；日发电量每天零点归零。
- 环境温度昼夜变化，绕组等设备温度随负载升高；流量有早晚用水高峰。
- 水压、pH、浊度、余氯、湿度、PM2.5、CO₂、风速在合理范围内缓慢漂移。
- 枚举多数时间取正常值（标签含 运行/发电/开启/正常，否则第一个值），同一小时内不变；布尔多数为否。
- 其他数值在上下限内随机游走；文本跳过。

日曲线按模拟器所在时区计算（Docker 中 `TZ=Asia/Shanghai`）。

> **注意**：模拟器会重置所模拟设备的密钥，真实设备随之连不上，只应对演示租户使用；用 `SIM_TENANTS` 限定范围。
```

13. Replace the whole section from `## 端到端测试：杀进程不丢数据` up to, but not including, `## 已知限制` with:

````markdown
## 端到端测试：故障场景不丢数据

`conduit-e2e`（`-Pe2e` 时才参与构建）在完全独立的临时环境中验证四种故障下数据不丢。Testcontainers 在 Docker 宿主机上起 PostgreSQL 16.8、Redis 7.4.2、InfluxDB 3.12.0-core，以及开启磁盘持久会话的 EMQX 5.8.6（端口随机，与 `conduit` 开发环境互不影响）；测试机本地以 jar 启动 1 个 server 与 2 个 transport。每个场景新建产品「e2e 场景 N 电表」与 10 台设备，每台以 QoS1 发送 50 条（未收到 PUBACK 时重连重发），发送中途注入故障：

| 场景 | 故障 | 验证的机制 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 强制结束 server，3 秒后重启 | 未确认消息被重新认领处理 |
| 2 | 强制结束主 transport | 备实例接管，磁盘积压补齐 |
| 3 | 两个 transport 都结束，设备继续上报约 20 秒后重启 | 磁盘持久会话兜底 |
| 4 | 重启 EMQX 容器 | 设备与 transport 重连，会话与积压不丢 |

每个场景断言 InfluxDB 中该产品恰好 500 条、死信流中没有该产品、消费组 `conduit-server` 没有待确认消息，结束时删除所建产品与设备。口令与 token 每次运行随机生成。

```bash
mvn -B -DskipTests install                                    # 先打包 server / transport jar 并安装模拟器
E2E_DEV_HOST=<dev-host> mvn -B verify -Pe2e -pl conduit-e2e
```

- `E2E_DEV_HOST`（必填）：Docker 宿主机访问测试机的地址。EMQX 的鉴权回调打到测试机上本地运行的 server，未设置时测试直接失败并说明原因；测试机防火墙需允许该 JDK 的 java 接受入站连接。
- 其余沿用 `DOCKER_HOST` / `TESTCONTAINERS_HOST_OVERRIDE`。
- 耗时约 6～10 分钟，不进默认构建。进程日志在 `conduit-e2e/target/e2e-logs/<时间>/`（`server`、`transport-a`、`transport-b` 各一个目录），失败时先看这里。
````

4. §1.2, directly after the **投递语义** bullet, insert these three bullets:

```markdown
- **transport 单活多备**（V0.2-R）：
  - 会话与订阅：所有实例共用 clientId `conduit-transport`（`conduit.transport.mqtt.client-id`）、`cleanStart=false`、会话保留 86400 秒，普通订阅 `conduit/+/+/up/#`。EMQX 5.8.6 的磁盘持久会话拒绝共享订阅（原因码 158），所以放弃多实例分摊。
  - 选主：Redis 键 `conduit:transport:leader`，`SET NX PX` 抢锁，Lua 比对 id 后 `PEXPIRE` 续期、`DEL` 释放；ttl 15s，续期 5s，抢锁 2s。只有主实例连接 EMQX；续期失败立即断开并转为备用，正常停止时释放锁。transport 的 Redis 命令超时为 2s。
  - 重连：适配器不用 HiveMQ 自动重连。断线或会话被接管时，由选主协调器先续期确认仍持锁，再重新连接；SUBACK 含错误码视为失败。
  - 健康检查：`components.leader.details` 显示 `role` / `instanceId` / `connected`。
- **e2e**（V0.2-R）：`ReliabilityE2EIT` 在 Testcontainers 临时环境中运行。EMQX 开启持久会话，并固定一个 20000～29999 的随机宿主机端口，以便 docker restart 后端口不变。测试本地以 jar 启动 1 个 server、2 个 transport，配置全部经环境变量传入，口令每次随机生成；设备由测试代码扮演（QoS1 直到收到 PUBACK，断线重连）。四个场景各发 500 条，断言计数恰好、无死信、无待确认。运行需 `E2E_DEV_HOST`：先 `mvn -B -DskipTests install`，再 `mvn -B verify -Pe2e -pl conduit-e2e`。
- **模拟器多租户**（V0.2-R）：
  - 发现：`SIM_PLATFORM_PASSWORD` 非空时，用平台管理员（`SIM_PLATFORM_USERNAME`）发现 `SIM_TENANTS` 中启用租户的物模型产品与启用设备（每产品最多 `SIM_MAX_DEVICES_PER_PRODUCT` 台），并重置其密钥；任一租户失败只记 WARN。
  - 生成：按解码器选上报格式，按物模型规则生成数据（spec 5.2），取值满足上下限与小数位；日曲线按系统时区（Docker 中 `TZ=Asia/Shanghai`）。
```

```markdown

## V0.2-R 遗留（2026-10-16）

- 升级 EMQX（评估同时支持磁盘持久会话与共享订阅的版本）后改回「多实例共享订阅 + 持久会话」，提升接入吞吐；当前单活多备只有主实例消费。
- 主备切换窗口（最长一个续期间隔）内，旧主若未察觉失锁，可能与新主以同一 clientId 互相踢；协调器重连前先续期可止住，期间的重复消息由写库去重吸收。
- transport 健康检查状态恒为 UP，备实例与「主实例但连不上 EMQX」都只体现在 details（`role`、`connected`）里；需要告警时再细分。
- 模拟器每次启动都重置所模拟设备的密钥；累计量与游走状态只在内存中，重启后从合理初值重新开始，曲线在重启处不连续。
- 模拟器按单位与名称关键字识别测点，识别不了的按上下限随机游走；新增行业物模型时可能需要补关键字。
- e2e 的 EMQX 使用 20000～29999 中随机的固定宿主机端口（docker restart 后端口不变），极少数情况下端口被占用会换端口重试，最多 5 次。
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Recreate the infrastructure EMQX with durable sessions (spec §7 item 1)**

The controller runs these. They sync the compose files to the Docker host and recreate only the `emqx` service with the host's `docker-compose` binary, as for earlier infrastructure changes:

```bash
export MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1
DOCKER="$DOCKER_CLI" deploy/sync-to-host.sh
COMPOSE_CLI="$DOCKER_CLI run --rm -v /var/run/docker.sock:/var/run/docker.sock -v /opt/conduit:/opt/conduit eclipse-temurin:21.0.6_7-jre /opt/conduit/bin/docker-compose -p conduit -f /opt/conduit/docker-compose.yml"
$COMPOSE_CLI up -d --force-recreate emqx
$COMPOSE_CLI ps emqx
$DOCKER_CLI exec conduit-emqx-1 /opt/emqx/bin/emqx ctl conf show durable_sessions | grep -E '^\s*enable'
$DOCKER_CLI exec conduit-emqx-1 /opt/emqx/bin/emqx ctl conf show cluster | grep -E 'discovery_strategy'
$DOCKER_CLI exec conduit-emqx-1 sh -c 'echo "$EMQX_NODE__NAME"'
$DOCKER_CLI logs --since 10m conduit-emqx-1 2>&1 | grep -iE "singleton|error" | head -20
```

Expected:
- `ps` shows `conduit-emqx-1` up and, after about 30 s, `(healthy)`.
- `enable = true`, `discovery_strategy = singleton`, and `emqx@127.0.0.1`.
- No line saying 「cluster discovery strategy must be 'singleton'」.

```bash
grep -noE '^\s*(client-id|session-expiry-seconds):' conduit-transport/src/main/resources/application-local.yml
grep -c '^SIM_PLATFORM_PASSWORD=.\+' conduit-simulator/src/main/resources/simulator-local.properties
grep -E '^SIM_INTERVAL_MS=' conduit-simulator/src/main/resources/simulator-local.properties
SIM_START_MS=$(date +%s%3N); echo "$SIM_START_MS"
```

Expected: the first command prints nothing, the second prints `1`, and the third prints the local reporting interval. Set `INTERVAL_MS` to that value, or to `10000` if the key is absent. If the first command prints a line, ask the owner to remove it and restart the transport.

- [ ] **Step 12: Acceptance items 2 and 3 (local transport is leader; tenant devices simulated)**

Item 2:

```bash
curl -s http://localhost:8081/actuator/health | grep -oE '"role":"[A-Z]+"|"connected":(true|false)'
grep -E "became leader|MQTT connected to" logs/conduit-transport.log | tail -2
```

Expected: `"role":"LEADER"` and `"connected":true`. The last two log lines are `Instance … became leader, starting protocol adapters` and `MQTT connected to … as conduit-transport (session expiry 86400 s), subscribed to conduit/+/+/up/#`.

Item 3: write the check script.

```bash
mkdir -p "$SCRATCH"
cat > "$SCRATCH/v02r_accept.py" <<'EOF'
"""V0.2-R 验收检查（不入库）：tenants 检查三个演示租户的模拟数据；gap 检查一台演示设备的曲线在给定窗口内无缺口。"""
import json
import os
import sys
import time
import urllib.request

API = os.environ.get("API", "http://localhost:8080")


def call(method, path, token=None, body=None):
    data = None if body is None else json.dumps(body).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(API + path, data=data, method=method, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    if token:
        req.add_header("Authorization", "Bearer " + token)
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as resp:
        raw = resp.read()
        return json.loads(raw) if raw else None


def login(user, password):
    return call("POST", "/api/v1/auth/login", body={"username": user, "password": password})["accessToken"]


def tenants():
    sys_token = login(os.environ.get("SYS_USER", "sysadmin"), os.environ["SYS_PASS"])
    by_name = {t["name"]: t for t in call("GET", "/api/v1/platform/tenants?size=100", sys_token)["items"]}
    night = time.localtime().tm_hour < 6 or time.localtime().tm_hour >= 18
    ok = True
    for name in ["华东电力", "华南水务", "西北新能源"]:
        tenant = by_name.get(name)
        if tenant is None:
            print(f"{name}: tenant not found")
            ok = False
            continue
        imp = call("POST", f"/api/v1/platform/tenants/{tenant['code']}/enter", sys_token)["accessToken"]
        online = call("GET", "/api/v1/devices?online=true&size=100", imp)
        sampled = online["items"][:20]
        with_values = sum(1 for d in sampled if call("GET", f"/api/v1/devices/{d['deviceKey']}/latest", imp)["values"])
        print(f"{name} ({tenant['code']}): online={online['total']} withLatest={with_values}/{len(sampled)}")
        ok = ok and online["total"] > 0 and with_values > 0
        for product in call("GET", "/api/v1/products?size=100", imp)["items"]:
            devices = call("GET", f"/api/v1/devices?productKey={product['productKey']}&online=true&size=1", imp)["items"]
            if not devices:
                continue
            dk = devices[0]["deviceKey"]
            for prop in call("GET", f"/api/v1/products/{product['productKey']}/properties", imp):
                text = (prop["identifier"] + " " + (prop.get("name") or "")).lower()
                unit = (prop.get("unit") or "").lower()
                if unit in ("kw", "w") and ("光伏" in text or "pv" in text):
                    latest = {v["identifier"]: v["value"] for v in call("GET", f"/api/v1/devices/{dk}/latest", imp)["values"]}
                    value = latest.get(prop["identifier"])
                    print(f"  PV {product['name']}/{prop['identifier']}: latest={value} night={night}")
                    if night:
                        ok = ok and value == 0
                if unit in ("m³", "m3") and "累计" in text:
                    now = int(time.time() * 1000)
                    history = call("GET", f"/api/v1/devices/{dk}/history?properties={prop['identifier']}"
                                          f"&from={now - 600000}&to={now}", imp)
                    values = [p[1] for p in history["series"][0]["points"]]
                    monotonic = all(b >= a for a, b in zip(values, values[1:]))
                    print(f"  water {product['name']}/{prop['identifier']}: points={len(values)} monotonic={monotonic}")
                    ok = ok and monotonic and len(values) > 1
        call("POST", "/api/v1/auth/exit-tenant", imp)
    print("RESULT", "PASS" if ok else "FAIL")


def gap(device_key, start, end, max_gap):
    token = login(os.environ.get("ADMIN_USER", "admin"), os.environ["ADMIN_PASS"])
    if device_key == "auto":
        device_key = call("GET", "/api/v1/devices?online=true&size=1", token)["items"][0]["deviceKey"]
    series = call("GET", f"/api/v1/devices/{device_key}/history?properties=Ua&from={start}&to={end}", token)["series"][0]
    stamps = [p[0] for p in series["points"]]
    gaps = [b - a for a, b in zip(stamps, stamps[1:])]
    largest = max(gaps) if gaps else None
    print(f"device={device_key} mode={series['mode']} points={len(stamps)} maxGapMs={largest}")
    print("RESULT", "PASS" if series["mode"] == "RAW" and gaps and largest <= max_gap else "FAIL")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1] == "tenants":
        tenants()
    else:
        gap(sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3]), int(sys.argv[4]), int(sys.argv[5]))
EOF
```

Wait about 2 minutes after `SIM_START_MS` so every device has reported several times, then run:

```bash
SYS_PASS="$SYSADMIN_PASSWORD" python -X utf8 "$SCRATCH/v02r_accept.py" tenants
grep -E "Tenant .* products with .* devices to simulate|Simulating .* devices of product" logs/conduit-simulator.log | tail -20
grep -E "WARN .*(Cannot enter tenant|Discovery failed|Skipping product|cannot log in)" logs/conduit-simulator.log | tail -5
$DOCKER_CLI exec conduit-redis-1 redis-cli XREVRANGE conduit:stream:dead + "$SIM_START_MS" | grep -c FIELD_REJECTED
```

Expected:
- The script prints one line per tenant with `online>0` and `withLatest>0`. Water totals report `monotonic=True`. If the local time is between 18:00 and 06:00, PV lines show `latest=0.0`; in daytime the PV check is informational, and the owner confirms it in Step 14. The last line is `RESULT PASS`.
- The simulator log has one `Tenant …` line per simulated tenant and `Simulating … devices of product …` lines.
- The WARN grep prints nothing.
- The FIELD_REJECTED count is `0`.

- [ ] **Step 13: Acceptance item 4 (stop the local transport for about a minute)**

Ask the owner to stop `Conduit Transport (local)` in IDEA, wait about one minute, and start it again. The controller records the two moments as the owner reports them:

```bash
STOP_MS=$(date +%s%3N)    # when the owner says the transport is stopped
START_MS=$(date +%s%3N)   # when the owner says it is running again
```

Wait 2 minutes, then:

```bash
grep -E "became leader|MQTT connected to" logs/conduit-transport.log | tail -2
ADMIN_PASS="$ADMIN_PASSWORD" python -X utf8 "$SCRATCH/v02r_accept.py" gap auto $((STOP_MS - 60000)) $((START_MS + 60000)) $((2 * INTERVAL_MS))
```

Expected: the transport log shows a new `became leader` and `MQTT connected to` after `START_MS`. If IDEA killed the process without a graceful shutdown, these lines appear up to 15 s later, when the old lock expires. The script prints `mode=RAW`, a `maxGapMs` no larger than twice the reporting interval, and `RESULT PASS`, which means the minute without a transport was backfilled.

- [ ] **Step 14: Owner visual checks (browser, `http://localhost:5173`)**

Ask the owner to check:
1. As `sysadmin`, enter 华东电力, 华南水务 and 西北新能源 in turn. Their device lists show devices 在线, and the device detail metric cards show values with mini trend lines.
2. A PV inverter's 历史 tab shows a sine-shaped power curve in daytime and 0 at night. Check the next morning over the last 24 h if the acceptance ran in daytime.
3. A water meter's cumulative volume only rises, and a daily-energy curve drops to 0 at midnight.
4. The demo product 「三相电表（演示）」 shows no gap in its curves around the minute the transport was stopped.
5. The home page dead-letter tile of each tenant shows no `FIELD_REJECTED` for the last hour.

- [ ] **Step 15: Record the acceptance and commit**

Append to `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-conduit-v0.2r-reliability-design.md`:

```markdown

## 9. 验收记录（2026-10-16）

- 全量测试：`mvn -B verify` 通过（含 transport 选主、持久会话、同 clientId 互踢、模拟器发现与生成规则的测试）。
- 第 7 节：
  1. 基础设施 EMQX 以 `EMQX_DURABLE_SESSIONS__ENABLE=true`、`singleton`、固定节点名重建，配置生效。
  2. 本地 transport 健康检查 `role` 为 `LEADER`、`connected` 为 true。
  3. 华东电力、华南水务、西北新能源的设备在线且有最新值，累计用水单调递增，死信中无 `FIELD_REJECTED`；光伏夜间为 0 由所有者目视确认。
  4. 本地 transport 停止约 1 分钟后重启，曲线无缺口（最大间隔不超过两个上报周期）。
  5. e2e 四个场景全部通过。
- 界面项：所有者按实施计划 Task 8 Step 14 目视确认。
```

If any check failed, record the failing item and what was observed instead of 「通过」 or the passing wording. Fix it in a follow-up commit and rerun that item before writing the record.

```bash
git add docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-conduit-v0.2r-reliability-design.md
git commit -m "docs: record the V0.2-R acceptance"
```

The only execution-time content is the acceptance record in Task 8 Step 15, written for the passing case with an explicit instruction for a failing item. Operator values appear only as the placeholders in the Global Constraints table.

**Type consistency:**
- `LeaderLock` (`instanceId`, `tryAcquire`, `renew`, `release`) is implemented by `RedisLeaderLock` and by `LeaderElectionTest.FakeLock`, and used by `LeaderElection`. `RedisLeaderLock(StringRedisTemplate, String, Duration, String)` is used identically in `TransportApplication`, `RedisLeaderLockIT` and `LeaderElectionIT`.
- `LeaderProperties(key, ttl, renewInterval, acquireInterval)` is constructed the same way in `LeaderElectionTest`, `LeaderElectionIT` and `LeaderPropertiesTest`, and bound from `conduit.transport.leader.*` in `application.yml`.
- `ProtocolAdapter` (`name`, `start`, `stop`, `isConnected`, `needsRestart`) is implemented by `MqttProtocolAdapter` and `LeaderElectionTest.FakeAdapter`, and used by `LeaderElection`.
- `MqttTransportProperties(host, port, clientId, username, password, sessionExpirySeconds, ownClientPrefix)` takes 7 arguments in `MqttProtocolAdapterTest`, `MqttProtocolAdapterIT` and `LeaderElectionIT`.
- `MqttProtocolAdapter` test seams `openSession()`, `Session`, `onPublish(Session, Mqtt5Publish)`, `onDisconnected(Session, MqttClientDisconnectedContext)`, `requireGranted(Mqtt5SubAck)` and `isSessionTakenOver(Throwable)` match `MqttProtocolAdapterTest`.
- `LeaderElection.role()`, `Role.LEADER/STANDBY`, `instanceId()` and `adaptersConnected()` match `LeaderHealthIndicator`, the tests and the health contract (`role`, `instanceId`, `connected`), which `E2eEnvironment.findConnectedLeader` reads.
- `SimulatorConfig` has 14 components. `platformUsername()`, `platformPassword()`, `multiTenant()`, `tenants()` and `maxDevicesPerProduct()` are used by `SimulationPlanner` and `SimulatorConfigTest`.
- `TenantFilter.parse/ALL/all/includes/codes` match `TenantDiscovery`, `TenantFilterTest`, `SimulatorConfigTest` and `TenantDiscoveryTest`.
- `PropertyDef(identifier, name, dataType, unit, minValue, maxValue, decimals, enumItems)` and `PropertyDef.EnumOption(long, String)` match `ConduitApiClient.properties`, `PointClassifier`, `PointGenerator`, `ValueRanges`, `ValueShaper` and all simulator tests.
- `ReadingSource.next(long, double)` is implemented by `ThingModelReadingSource` and by the lambdas in `SimulatedDevice.demo` and `DeviceSimulatorIT`, and called in `DeviceSimulator.runDevices`.
- `TenantDiscovery.discover(ConduitApiClient, TenantFilter, int, Set<String>)` and `DiscoveredProduct(tenantCode, productKey, productName, format, properties, devices)` match `SimulationPlanner` and `TenantDiscoveryTest`.
- `SimulationPlanner.tenantDevices(SimulatorConfig, Supplier<ConduitApiClient>, Set<String>)` matches `SimulatorApplication` and the tests.
- `SimulatedDevice(credentials, format, readings)` and `SimulatedDevice.demo(DeviceCredentials, PayloadFormat, boolean)` match `DeviceSimulator.run`, `SimulatorApplication` and `DeviceSimulatorIT`.
- `ConduitApiClient` methods used by the e2e (`login`, `findOrCreateProduct(String, String)`, `createDevice`, `listDeviceKeys`, `deleteDevice`, `deleteProduct`) keep their signatures in the Task 6 rewrite.
- `E2eEnvironment.start/api/mqttHost/mqttPort/startServer/killServer/kill/killTransports/startTransports/leaderTransport/awaitLeaderConnected/restartEmqx/ensureRunning/influxCount/deadLetters/pendingCount/close` and `CLAIM_MIN_IDLE` match `ReliabilityE2EIT`. `EmqxConfig.render(String, String, String)` and `E2eEnvironment.root()` match `EmqxConfigTest`.
- Log texts that Task 8 greps match the code:
  - `became leader` (`LeaderElection.acquireTick`);
  - `MQTT connected to … as … (session expiry … s), subscribed to …` (`MqttProtocolAdapter.start`);
  - `Tenant … products with … devices to simulate` (`TenantDiscovery`);
  - `Simulating … devices of product` (`SimulationPlanner`);
  - `Cannot enter tenant`, `Discovery failed`, `Skipping product`, `cannot log in`;
  - `Scenario N: injecting the fault after` (`ReliabilityE2EIT`).

**Resolved ambiguities:**
- **Compose transports:** two service definitions (`conduit-transport`, `conduit-transport-2`, YAML anchor, shared image `conduit-conduit-transport:latest`) instead of `deploy.replicas: 2`. Compose would honour replicas, because there is no `container_name` and no host port. But both replicas would write and roll the same `conduit-transport.log` on the shared `conduit-logs` volume.
- **Fixed EMQX node name `emqx@127.0.0.1`** in compose and the e2e. The image otherwise names the node after the container IP, and an IP change on recreate or restart would orphan the durable-session data. A side effect is that the old IP-named runtime data is no longer used; it is documented in the README.
- **Reconnection:** HiveMQ automatic reconnect is dropped. The election coordinator restarts the adapter after renewing the lock. A SUBACK with any error code counts as failure. A taken-over session is never resumed by the kicked instance.
- **Global publish filter `ALL`**, so messages replayed from the durable session before the new client re-subscribes still go through ack-after-XADD.
- **"Renewal timeout"** is the Redis command timeout, set to 2 s for the transport. `SET NX` also refuses the current holder, so after a Redis blip the former leader waits at most one TTL.
- **Health:** status always `UP`. The component is named `leader`, with details `role`, `instanceId` and `connected`. `show-details: always`.
- **Adapter start retries** back off from 1 s to 10 s.
- **e2e:**
  - The server is configured only through env vars plus `--server.port`; credentials are generated per run.
  - `PIPELINE_CLAIM_MIN_IDLE=10s`.
  - EMQX gets a fixed random host port (20000–29999), because `docker restart` may remap random ports.
  - The restart goes through the Docker API.
  - The pending check requires the whole group to have 0 pending entries, which is stricter than per product.
  - The run is `mvn -B -DskipTests install`, then `mvn -B verify -Pe2e -pl conduit-e2e`, without `-am`, which would rerun every upstream module's tests.
  - The repository root comes from the `conduit.root` system property.
- **Simulator:**
  - `SIM_PLATFORM_PASSWORD` defaults to empty in compose and `.env.example` (opt-in), because it resets device secrets.
  - A failed platform login falls back to demo-only with a WARN.
  - Products are skipped when they are the demo product (by key), have no properties, have only TEXT properties, or use a codec the simulator cannot produce.
  - Only `ACTIVE` tenants and `ENABLED` devices are simulated, filtered both server-side and client-side.
  - Day curves use the system time zone, and the simulator container gets `TZ: Asia/Shanghai`.
- **Classification and value rules (spec §5.2):**
  - A total needs an energy or volume unit, or no unit plus a keyword. Daily totals are recognized by `eday`/`daily`/`today`/`日`.
  - An ENUM takes the normal value in 85 % and a BOOL is `true` in 5 % of the (device, property, hour) slots.
  - The decimals default to 3 when unset. The exact order and constants are in the Task 5 decisions.
- **Acceptance:** PV-at-night is asserted only when the check runs between 18:00 and 06:00. Otherwise the owner confirms it visually. The "no gap" check allows at most two reporting intervals between raw points.
